# Roblox decision rules (if X then Y)

_Generated 2026-11-01 08:01 by `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/build_tools.py` from the topic files in this KB. Do not hand-edit; rebuild instead._

Every if-then rule from the KB's agent-instruction sections plus the master playbook, deduplicated across topics. Each rule is tagged with the topic file it came from and, where a matching finding exists, the official/indie flag and a timestamped source for it (support is matched automatically; open the finding before relying on a number). Rules marked `x2`+ appear in several topics.

Flag: **official** = Roblox Creator Docs / Roblox or Roblox Creators sessions; **mixed** = official and indie agree; **devforum** = DevForum post (staff or community, check the link); **indie** = creator self-reported, unverified. Official 2026 docs override older indie advice. Date = newest citation.


Counts: 2811 unique topic rules (from 2828 raw) + 16 master-playbook rules.

## How reliable is this sheet (trust audit 2026-11-01)

Items checked: 2827. Labels: OFFICIAL 498, CORROBORATED 293, SINGLE-SOURCE 1938, CONTRADICTED 0, OUTDATED-UNSURE 98.
Label rules (computed from the Flag column, cite count and date; code in `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/trust_audit.py`): official = OFFICIAL; mixed or indie+devforum = CORROBORATED; devforum or one indie creator = SINGLE-SOURCE; indie with 2+ non-WEAK creators = CORROBORATED; checklist `xN` with N>=2 = CORROBORATED (topic files can share a source, so weaker than independent creators); a fast-moving fact (ads, DevEx, Premium, fees, eligibility, discovery) with newest date before 2025-11 = OUTDATED-UNSURE (tagged inline). CONTRADICTED = checked against current Creator Docs and found wrong; such rows carry an inline `[AUDIT ...]` note with the corrected value. Rows without an inline tag use the rule above.
Limit: labels describe source structure, not a re-verification of every number. Only the rows in the corrections file were checked against live Creator Docs.
Corrections applied here (3 rows, 2 rules): ads-17plus x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; premium-payout-general x2 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE.
Five shakiest items still in the sheet (fast-moving, numeric, not officially re-checked, oldest first):

  4. If a user wants commissions, suggest joining developer communities and posting clear listings with service, price, and DM contact. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Arow 2025-05 1:05] (2025-05)
  5. When a user asks about A/B testing or experimentation, first check their revenue stage against the GameMakers thresholds (N7943, N7944) before recommending heavy process... (2025-02)

## Master playbook rules (cross-topic)

- If you can't explain the game in one sentence and name its audience, don't build it yet.
- If demand evidence is missing (no growing comparable games), validate with search, RoTrends and Creator Exchange before building.
- If you are a beginner, make 10-20 small games rather than one dream game.
- If D1 or the first-session funnel is weak, fix the core loop, onboarding and performance before ads or monetisation.
- If you are tempted to use ads to rescue a weak game, don't. Fix retention first, then run about $10/day for about 10 days.
- If organic home recommendations exceed sponsored impressions, stop ads.
- If an item is repeatable, make it a developer product. If it is a permanent perk, make it a gamepass, and keep passes to about 2-6.
- If a purchase would remove challenge, break competitive balance or gate core progress, don't sell it (official stance; pay-to-win in competitive games is an unverified counter-view).
- If you need the first purchase, offer a cheap, time-limited starter pack after the tutorial or the first milestone.
- If you pick prices, start from comparables and test live. Use Price Optimization only with scripted prices and about 60k transactions per 30 days.
- If official docs conflict with indie advice on mechanics, metrics or payouts, follow the official docs (e.g. 28-day retention, PTR plus first-play bounce, Creator Rewards).
- If a live-ops update takes more than 3 weeks, it isn't a cadence update. Make it a major update, and keep the cadence running meanwhile.
- If a tutorial test is possible, A/B test guided versus no tutorial, because sources disagree.
- If a thumbnail or title misleads, change it. Mismatches cause bounces that hurt recommendations.
- If a game has no traction after early iterations, pivot or kill it. If it is live and has an audience, don't abandon it.
- Treat indie CCU and revenue figures as unverified. Don't use them as benchmarks.

## 3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation (coverage: covered)

- If the user asks about avatar bodies, clothing or accessories, start from Roblox's official R15 template or Rig_and_Attachments project file, then check against the Avatar character specs before publishing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating)
- If an FBX imports oversized, then set Apply Scalings to FBX Unit Scale; if it still imports at 1/100, set object transforms to 1.000 or switch to glTF/GLB. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/creating/blender-configurations)
- If the user cites a 2017-2022 DevForum workflow that conflicts with 2026 official docs, then follow the official 2026 docs and say which source was superseded. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/auto-translation-overrides-manual-translations/4958947)
- If the user animates an emote, then use an R15 rig and convert to CurveAnimation via the Curve Editor, not KeyframeSequence. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/emotes/import)
- If an animation must replace the default jump or run, then rename the final keyframe to exactly 'End' (case-sensitive) and publish first. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/editor)
- If a facial rig fails validation, then check the count (at least 17 FACS poses), neutral on frame 0, one pose per frame, correctives with at most 3 base poses and 3 control names, and face joints under 50. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/create-basic-heads)
- If a cage fails or clothing floats or bloats, then check that the cage closely conforms and fully encloses the mesh, and that no vertices were added or deleted. If it still fails, adjust weights or skinning rather than only the mesh. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/validation-system)
- If a skinned mesh deforms badly, then test each joint in Pose mode, cap influences at 4, and keep Root and HumanoidRootNode free of influences; expect to revisit bone positions after painting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters)
- If a user wants animation to look natural, then block key poses first (anticipation, extreme, return), add easing (CubicV2 or Sine/Quad), avoid linear motion, and exaggerate the key poses for game readability. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/designing-poses)
- If a looping clip jumps back, then copy the first pose to the final frame; for replicated animations, publish before referencing and move KeyframeSequence/CurveAnimation to ReplicatedStorage. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/editor)
- If the user asks about accessories for a moving or animated weapon, recommend Model plus Motor6D; for static clothing or add-ons, recommend welded Accessories. [devforum, 2020-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user is building rigid accessories, route them through Blender FBX export then AFT; do not recommend importing third-party attachments. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies/specifications)
- If the user is building layered clothing, require LoadCharacterLayeredClothing, cage naming, and an AutoSkin setting; state the no-gloves-or-boots limit. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer)
- If the user asks about eyelashes or eyebrows, require RBX_Leader/RBX_Follower at 1.0 weight, EnabledOverride, and the export deform option. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer)
- If the user is making a dynamic head, check the outer cage, three landmarks, 17 FACS poses, and the five facial tests. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/dynamic-heads/specifications)
- If the user is posing or looping an animation, give the copy-start-pose steps and remind them to turn on Loop before previewing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/creating-animations)
- If the user is building combat animations, set Action priority, non-looping, and preload before first use. [indie, 2026-10] -- [ArTheDev 2026-10 35:25](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=2125)
- If the user asks about the animation editor, recommend ACE for clips and the Graph Editor only for logic, and test with AJU enabled. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788)
- If sources conflict, prefer 2026 official Roblox Creator Docs over 2020-2022 indie advice and say so. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Treat indie figures and low-advice tips as self-reported; flag them as unverified when recommending them. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If an emote or animation is longer than 10 seconds, shorten or split it before upload. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Xaiyne 2025-08 4:16](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=256)
- If a rig has a parent and child joint, keyframe the parent first, then the child. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Xaiyne 2025-08 3:35](https://youtu.be/aqv5bLmqbzc?t=215)
- If you use IK Control, set ChainRoot, EndEffector, Target and Type first, then check Weight, and test world-space turning and held items. [indie, 2022-09] -- [TheNexusAvenger 2022-09 8:41](https://youtu.be/C4ASAayPpkY?t=521)
- If you rig a model in Studio, use Rig Edit: create joints parent-first, move pivots to the rotation point, and start at step value 25. [indie, 2024-06] -- [Roox4 2024-06 0:58](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=58)
- If a walk cycle looks like half-walking, enlarge the leg and arm swings and spread the keys. [indie, 2025-07] -- [LovJak 2025-07 1:32](https://youtu.be/HBKUn339Frk?t=92)
- If animation looks fast or snappy in the editor, space keyframes further apart and re-test in-game. [indie, 2025-07] -- [LovJak 2025-07 1:32](https://youtu.be/HBKUn339Frk?t=92)
- If exporting from Blender to Studio, use the RBLX add-on export and a toolbox import plugin; use R6 textures only on the smooth R6 rig. [indie, 2026-04] -- [Rool-Studio 2026-04 1:24](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=84)
- If rendering from Blender, add a camera and an output folder before rendering. [indie, 2026-04] -- [Rool-Studio 2026-04 5:17](https://youtu.be/WI6adMt2aUY?t=317)
- If an accessory does not align to the head, first check that the Handle name and the head attachment name match the rig exactly. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 1:57](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=117)
- If fit is still off after AFT, adjust the Attachment CFrame position in small steps and re-test in-game. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 4:02](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=242)
- Treat the Y-offset range and handle size as examples from one creator, not as fixed values. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=365)
- If the task is runtime part placement by surface normal in Luau scripts, use CFrame.lookAlong(position, normal) rather than lookAt. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 14:33](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=873)
- If the task is about modeling, rigging, animation or import pipelines, ignore T18089; it is outside this topic's scope.
- If an accessory export yields multiple textures, use the highest-resolution one and verify the result in Studio rather than assuming a fixed texture count. [indie, 2020-11] -- [BloxianCode 2020-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/vP_d8TWny3g?t=0)
- If the task involves pets, apply the primary-part and reusable icon/config template advice (T18815) but verify the mesh connection in Studio. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 17:11](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=1031)
- If the task involves a new seasonal item built in Blender, suggest starting from an existing project asset (T18859) while keeping the official-template rule for rigs and avatar parts. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 0:53](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=53)
- Do not treat single-mention indie workflow tips as platform ground truth; flag them as self-reported. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)

## ab-testing-and-experiments (coverage: covered)

- If a user wants to test a gameplay value, then put it in a config, create a draft experiment with a baseline and at most 2 treatments, and choose a goal metric matching the hypothesis. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If an experiment's duration is outside 14–60 days, then correct it before creating. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If the experiment is already started or scheduled, then do not try to edit it; stop it and create a new one. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If the user asks to launch based on the first 24 hours, then refuse and explain that Early Harm Detection is only for stopping. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/experiments-catch-experiment-regressions-faster-with-early-harm-detection/4907734)
- If isSrmDetected is true or the Results tab shows SRM, then recommend stopping and restarting the experiment. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If enrolled players are below 10,000, then explain that no harm decisions are made and suggest raising exposure or widening the audience. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/experiments-catch-experiment-regressions-faster-with-early-harm-detection/4907734)
- If the test is on matchmaking, then use 100% exposure, allow only one running per universe, and fall back to pre-post dashboard analysis if player counts are low. [devforum, 2025-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419)
- If the user wants different values per audience, then add a config first, then use targeting or conditional configs, and order rules most specific first. [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/evolving-experiments-and-analytics-segmentation-experiment-targeting/4828462)
- If the user changes live values, then recommend GradualRollout and verify drafts with GET before publishing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/right-to-be-forgotten)
- If the user tests thumbnails, then test title and thumbnail as pairs and wait several days for analytics to settle; label indie revenue or retention claims as unverified and prefer official mechanics. [indie+devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ab-testing-experience-titles-together-with-thumbnails/4900092)
- If the user asks how to ramp a winning experiment variant, prefer the in-Studio Configs and Experiments flow (T17611) over manual web checks, and still follow the existing gradual-rollout rules. [official, 2026-04] -- [Roblox 2026-04 36:37](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2197)
- If the user asks about publishing age ratings for a game with no restricted content and the Simplified flow is available, answer the single 16+ question (T17646) instead of the full questionnaire. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780)
- Do not treat the age-estimation range (N7460) as a guarantee; cite it as Roblox's claimed accuracy.
- If a proposed test changes more than one variable, split it into separate experiments before setup. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If a proposed variant is a small tweak (about 5% or a single colour), recommend a bolder variant unless the test is explicitly a guard test. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 35:11](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2111) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a risky test is proposed, restrict it to a small slice and add ratings and retention as guard metrics. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 36:32](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2192)
- If a split test is around 80% confidence with no negative signals, still require the existing Roblox gates (full duration, SRM check, 24h wait before /stats) before recommending rollout. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 16:19](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=979) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- After each experiment concludes, log hypothesis, metric, result and confidence in the test vault. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 25:18](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1518)
- For reward or difficulty tuning, propose a multivariate test over player segments rather than one fixed formula. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 7:00](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=420)
- For ad creatives, propose a matrix over duration and aspect ratio rather than a few hand-picked variants. [indie, 2021-11] -- [GameMakers 2021-11 3:47](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=227) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Re-check shipped features at about three months against the original forecast before judging success. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 8:19](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=499)

## ads-and-sponsored-experiences (coverage: covered)

- If the user asks whether to run ads on a new game, first check retention and the first-time experience; if weak, advise fixing them before spending. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 30:49](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1849)
- If advising a first-time budget, suggest about $10/day for ~10 days (range $5-20/day), Maximize Plays, All Players, all regions and devices; label dollar figures as self-reported indie claims. [indie, 2026-06] -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=60)
- If the user asks when to stop, say stop once home recommendation impressions exceed sponsored impressions in Acquisition analytics, and don't restart repeatedly (rolling 7-day window). [indie, 2026-06] -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19)
- If the user proposes a 1-2 day campaign, cite Roblox's guidance of at least 1-2 weeks, with 3-5 days minimum before judging results. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- If CTR is below 1%, replace the creative; target 2.5-3%+. Run thumbnail A/B tests before large spend. For daily vs lifetime, recommend daily (official + CallMeDogey) and note waveshine and kra_DeZ use lifetime. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- If the game is old or lapsed after a major update, recommend Maximize Plays + Reactivate Users for 1-2 weeks; check the 20,000 lapsed / 10,000 recent minimums. [official, 2025-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-10 16:24](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=984) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If asked about search ads, advise relevant high-volume keywords, max 10 per Ad Set, single-keyword Ad Sets for comparison, and no audience targeting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads)
- If the user wants rewarded video, check eligibility (13+, ID, 2FA, public unrestricted game, 2,000 monthly visitors, maturity questionnaire), call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync just before display, show clear non-random non-Robux rewards, and track opt-in (13-15%+) and frequency (2.5-3+). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim)
- If ads are placed in an experience, check AreAdsAllowed per player, add disclosure labels, register campaigns 5+ days ahead and obey billboard size limits; for brand deals recommend in-game integrations over standalone games plus legal review.
- If indie claims conflict with official docs on mechanics, prefer official docs and the most recent date, and tell the user to check the live Ads Manager UI since settings change. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience)
- If the user asks to start or fund a sponsored campaign, first confirm eligibility via campaign-options and check that the game holds players and has no stability problems. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising)
- If a campaign shows high impressions but low plays or CTR near 0.3%, recommend pausing and fixing the game before spending more. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576)
- If a campaign is performing, recommend scaling in small tranches and measuring each before the next.
- When recommending budgets, state the unit (Robux, ad credits or USD) and the genre and explain the range. [indie+devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- Use day-7 retention and sessions as the success metric, not impressions or clicks. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576)
- When writing API code, include an idempotency key, poll status, use durationInDays and batch status checks, and use PAUSED rather than CANCELLED for temporary stops.
- Prefer the Oct 2026 official docs and the 250 Kids/Select threshold over older figures; mark indie numbers as self-reported. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 10:57](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=657)
- If a user is planning a large spend, recommend tranches and a return calculation (Robux returned versus spent) before committing. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064)
- If an experience is 17+ rated, don't plan ad-driven acquisition without first checking current Roblox rules. [devforum, 2026-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944) **[AUDIT 2026-11-01 OUTDATED-UNSURE: superseded: since 2025-01-31 Roblox staff allow Sponsored Experiences and Search Ads for restricted (17+) and paid-access experiences, but 17+ experiences cannot serve ads to users aged 13-17. Ads Manager access needs age 13+ and a verified email (no ID check for advertisers); ad PUBLISHERS (monetising with ads) need 18+ and ID verification (DevForum 'Advertising paid access & restricted (17+) experiences' 2025-01-31; Creator Docs ads-manager fetched 2026-11-01)]**
- If asked about cost, convert all credits to Robux at 285 per credit and state the Robux cost per play. [indie+devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If a user is advertising a marketplace item, use Sponsored Items (min 10 Robux/day, 24h-28 day cycle); otherwise use Ads Manager or the Advertising API. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items)
- If home-recommendation players exceed ad-driven players, recommend reducing or stopping paid credits. [indie, 2026-06] -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19)
- Before recommending spend, confirm the core loop and retention are working; if not, advise fixing them first. [indie, 2026-05] -- [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=0)
- When a user needs to change a thumbnail mid-campaign, say the active ad must be cancelled and restarted (one creator; verify). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Raul 2026-08 4:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=244)
- Compute refund for early cancel as full days remaining only, not partial days. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items)
- Treat indie numbers as self-reported and flag low-advice items; prefer official 2026 docs over 2020-2025 creator posts. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- Flag unit mismatches between sources (credits vs Robux vs USD) instead of mixing them.
- Pre-roll video ads need no code; don't write custom ad code for them. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the game has no working monetisation or weak day-1 retention, advise against paid spend until those are fixed. [indie, 2026-06] -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19)
- If a user asks for a budget, give test and scale ranges separately (e.g. ~100 Robux test vs 10k-100k scale-up), noting that creators disagree.
- Before recommending an objective, ask whether the goal is testing or sustained growth; 2026 creators favour engagement objectives.
- Always recommend a custom daily budget and disabling auto-reload. [indie, 2026-07] -- [LovJak 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246)
- If an ad shows zero impressions after ~30 minutes, check campaign status and eligibility before concluding it failed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim)
- Advise against pulling and reposting campaigns; judge results after the learning period and at least 1-2 weeks, unless thresholds clearly fail. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163)
- If CTR is high but day-1 retention or plays are low, diagnose audience and messaging mismatch first. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122)
- Convert credits to Robux using the actual purchase price (about 250-285 Robux each), not a single assumed rate. [indie+devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- Treat indie ROI claims as self-reported; do not present them as official Roblox figures. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- For European or under-13 audiences, check the 13+ and verification requirements before planning. [indie, 2025-11] -- [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 1:21](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=81)
- If a campaign's audience is allowed to be 18+ and the goal is retention or monetization, test an O18 segment against all players before scaling. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If a traffic or CCU spike coincides with an event or admin action, do not credit it to ads until the event is ruled out. [indie, 2026-08] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-08](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/)
- When citing rewarded-video revenue or sponsored-ad prevalence, use the official Sep 2026 IR figures and note that the ad-prevalence figure is correlational.
- If asked about co-marketing or UA funding from Roblox, cite T17596 and label it as indie-reported, not official. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If asked about pre-roll reach or revenue, state that Roblox Plus subscribers won't see pre-roll ads (T17663). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If asked about the size of Roblox brand activations, cite N7475 with its Q4 2025 date.
- Do not treat the 2026-09 pre-roll 'coming soon' status as launched without checking current official docs. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a campaign targets lapsed players, then report the share of players who return within 7 days alongside cost per activation. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 37:13](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2233)
- If cost per activation is above Robux return, then pause the campaign rather than scaling. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064)
- If a player appears after several ad impressions but no click, then credit the ad campaign on a multi-day window, not on the last click. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324)
- If running Discord quest buys for an existing game, then split reporting between new users and lapsed reengagement. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406)
- If a publisher runs several games, then test audience and avatar combinations per product before pooling budget. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 13:01](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=781)
- If a creator activation drives a download spike, then do not attribute that spike to paid ads without a separate test. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 19:02](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1142)
- If CTR is high but plays are low, then review the landing experience and the ad promise before increasing budget. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576)
- If a creative's CTR is below your kill threshold, stop it. Compare IPM and watch time before scaling any survivor. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856)
- If you need a new creative pipeline, plan 20-50 concepts per cycle, sized to monthly spend, and build them from a persona-by-trend matrix. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86)
- If you want to test a challenger against a champion, launch it in its own campaign; never add it to the champion's campaign. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032)
- If you plan to enter a new country, run a small paid campaign there first and scale only when retention, CPI or ROI look good. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 30:49](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1849)
- If CPI exceeds the LTV you forecast, pause scaling and fix retention or monetisation before adding spend. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 12:12](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=732) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If ad-sourced players churn at high rates per 1,000 impressions (100 per 1,000 is very bad), treat the creative or audience as a quality problem, not a volume problem. [indie, 2026-02] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 8:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=509)
- If the influencer channel is TikTok and you need trackable installs, add Spark Ads cost (about double spend) to your budget or choose another channel. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056)
- If you are funding influencers for the first time, set expectations that the first campaigns are for learning and that cutting spend later increases install decline. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 43:13](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2593)
- If the first 8-credit-per-day, 2-day campaign shows traction, step to 16 credits per day for 2 days; do not jump straight to full budget. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365)
- Use ad credits, not Robux, when budgeting 2026 campaigns; treat 2020-22 Robux and Facebook numbers as historical. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064)
- If a campaign's CTR is healthy but QPTR is under ~1%, pause budget increases and diagnose the first session before scaling.
- If the user asks whether ads can fix a weak or unoptimised game, advise against it; ads should only seed initial players. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition)
- If a launch-day bug is still open, delay campaigns until the build is stable.
- Before launch, check that no unintended campaigns are running concurrently. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:02](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=842)
- Treat mobile-app UA items (DSP, mediation, iOS IDFA) as off-Roblox; apply them only when the user asks about non-Roblox apps.
- Quote the source (indie, self-reported) when citing janisjanis, GameMakers or rasdev figures. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)

## age-ratings-moderation-and-tos (coverage: covered)

- If user-entered text will be shown to other users, then run it through Class.TextService:FilterStringAsync() first. If the game does not filter, it can be removed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines)
- If you build chat, then use TextChannel:SendAsync() and TextChannels, not custom messaging, so messages stay reportable and visible to moderators. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines)
- If a messaging request is about to be sent, then check CanUserChatAsync, CanUsersChatAsync or CanUsersDirectChatAsync first and act on the result. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines)
- If you create a TextChannel for direct chat, then call SetDirectChatRequester() on it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines)
- If a preset enables dynamic or directed conversation, then require Facial Age Estimation or ID verification and similar-age matching. If the preset is only gameplay actions, confirmations or basic reactions, then no age check is needed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/preset-system-guidelines)
- If a user's avatar asset or outfit is about to be used in-experience, then wait for AvatarAssetModerationCompleted (per asset) and AvatarOutfitModerationCompleted (whole outfit). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup)
- If a request involves an IP takedown, then only the rights holder or an authorized representative can file it. Tell third-party reporters to contact the rights holder. If an item is archived, then do not treat it as IP-safe; it can still be taken down and archiving is considered in penalties. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property)
- If a non-IP avatar violation needs reporting, then use Report Abuse on the catalog page or a support ticket. EU residents use the DSA form. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- If a moderation or revocation decision seems wrong, then appeal: revocations via the appeals page, incorrect moderation via moderation staff, IP removals via the IP infringement notice form. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- If the target audience is classrooms or children, then keep content appropriate for all ages, teach reporting tools before online play, and remember the default Build rating is 16+. If a user asks for a password or offers free Robux, then report the request using Report Abuse and do not trust the offer. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/learn-explore-sort)
- If publishing to 16+ or all-ages, first require age check and 2-step verification; recheck on every publish. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-publishing-requirements-evaluation-process-for-games/4573166)
- If the target audience is kids, plan for the 500-adult clearance requirement and keep the safety bar unchanged. [indie+devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/highly-engaged-player-threshold-drops-to-250/4820164)
- If a figure conflicts between sources (fee, threshold), prefer the most recent official Roblox guidance and flag the figure as needing Creator Hub verification.
- If the game has simulated gambling, remove it before any compliance check; lootboxes and paid random generators remain allowed. [devforum, 2023-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184)
- If a UGC item resembles a Roblox-created Limited, do not publish it; copying risks UGC Program removal. [devforum, 2023-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/taking-action-on-copies-of-roblox-created-limiteds/2226390)
- If designing chat or social features, default to monitored and filtered; off for under-9 and gated for under-13. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/friends-are-back-expanding-trusted-friends-for-a-new-way-to-chat-and-play/4554217)
- If the game uses feed-style rewards, remove them before seeking Kids or Select placement. [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-new-restrictions-on-reward-driven-media-feeds/4829188)
- If an appeal is needed, use Creator Hub; imported-model appeals resolve in up to 10 minutes. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup)
- If writing age-check or ID data handling, do not store selfies; rely on Roblox's 30-day retention and deletion. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices)
- If a ban message is needed, use brand or platform names only, with no usernames, handles or links. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players)
- If the user asks to advertise an external link, point them to Social Links and the description, and warn that most external services are 13+. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 24:31](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=1471) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user wants emotes or animations, flag suggestive ones (twerking, 'sus' poses) as removal risks. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Xaiyne 2026-03 7:33](https://youtu.be/O5FjkMlRPPI?t=453)
- If the user adds an image, AI-generated or user-supplied, recommend a moderation screen before publishing. [indie, 2026-09] -- [LovJak 2026-09 5:48](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=348)
- If the user builds chat, require filtered TextChannels for all players, including guests. [indie, 2022-08] -- [ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 33:51](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=2031)
- If the user plans a kids-targeted launch, factor in the reduced advertising reach from age gating. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 4:55](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=295)
- Treat all items from this batch as indie, self-reported advice. Confirm against official Community Standards before stating them as policy. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)
- If planning around age-check reach, use the official 57% global DAU figure and 90% goal, and label any regional indie numbers as self-reported.
- If a design depends on chat being limited to age-checked users, verify it against official documentation before implementing. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- If the target audience includes under-13 players, review loss mechanics and Robux-spend prompts before launch. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts)
- If citing Kids or Select catalog size, state the July 2026 date and check for a newer figure. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780)
- If asked how under-18 creators verify, mention Facial Age Estimation as the alternative to ID verification (2026-09 DevForum), and note that the user should confirm it still applies in the Creator Hub. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780)
- If asked about the Experience Guidelines questionnaire for a 16+ game, first check whether any restricted content exists. If none, the single-question path may apply; if any exists, require the full questionnaire at the most mature level. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the game has any doubt about its rating, default to the full, honest questionnaire answer at the most mature applicable level. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- Never describe the nearly 40% ban reduction as an achieved result; describe it as an expectation from a Roblox post.
- When a user reports being flagged for misuse, first check whether the flagged mechanic enables abuse (for example, player-built drawings or shapes) and recommend a design change before disputing enforcement. [official, 2025-08] -- [Roblox 2025-08 27:50](https://youtu.be/cuPGe99m8zs?t=1670) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Route Highly Engaged Player threshold questions to the monetization/engagement topic instead of the moderation knowledge file.
- When discussing the 99.5% content-removal figure, always state its date window (May 19 to June 30, 2026). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes)
- Treat all new items here as single-source (Roblox official); do not claim cross-creator agreement without additional sources. [indie, 2022-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 19:58](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=1198)
- When an age-verification rule conflicts with an older rule in the knowledge file, prefer the 2026-09 announcement and say so explicitly. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780)
- If the user asks about 17+ content, still require the existing 17+ owner and collaborator verification; do not assume Facial Age Estimation satisfies it without a source. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780)
- If a player or moderator complaint mentions sexualised content, review the flagged content against Community Standards and remove or change it before enforcement escalates. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)
- Do not treat the single self-reported source T18364 as official guidance; rely on the existing Avoid sexual UGC and suggestive emote rules for the policy itself. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/marketplace-policy)
- If a user asks about running a giveaway, contest or prize in a Roblox experience, advise checking per-country legal restrictions on tangible prizes before launch, and say this is creator-reported guidance rather than official Roblox policy. ## ai-and-tools (coverage: covered)

- If asked to build a game with AI, then split into one mechanic per prompt and playtest between each. [indie, 2026-04] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/Ufr0SVq-o98?t=0)
- If a generated script errors, then paste the exact error back and ask for a fix. [indie, 2026-05] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-05 1:21](https://youtu.be/uYuiCJrAcmI?t=81)
- If setting up an external LLM, then enable Studio as an MCP server in Assistant settings and verify the green indicator. [indie, 2026-10] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40)
- If generating UI with a reference, then supply a full-screen screenshot. [indie, 2026-10] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 1:22](https://youtu.be/Mftq7v8nAcs?t=82)
- If the user hits usage limits, then suggest a higher tier or batching prompts, noting costs are self-reported.
- If the user wants to learn Luau, then recommend using AI as a tutor and reading the docs.
- If publishing AI-made assets, then warn that copyright may not apply to raw AI output and players may dislike AI use. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 17:41](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1061)
- If integrating an in-game LLM, then require JSON with a fixed action set and theme validation. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 31:36](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=1896)
- If combat is being tested, then add health display first. [indie, 2026-01] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-01 5:21](https://youtu.be/3y7EU49_d7s?t=321)
- If quoting time-saved or revenue claims from indie creators, then label them unverified.
- If a task involves many repetitive Studio edits, then propose Assistant actions before manual steps. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools)
- If asked to generate a script with Assistant, then write a pseudo-code prompt and review the output before running it. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 9:45](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=585)
- If generated output keeps missing, then narrow the scope and regenerate rather than re-rolling the same broad prompt.
- If debugging unfamiliar code, then use Studio code explanation before changing anything. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 33:37](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=2017)
- If onboarding or tuning values are hardcoded, then recommend moving them into Configs and Experiments. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques)
- If asked whether an experience uses AI for a disclosure, then base the answer on player-facing generative AI, not development use. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity)
- If a user is new to AI tools, then encourage early, cheap experiments and treat failures as useful. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 27:17](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1637)
- If a user asks how to connect Claude or Codex to Studio, recommend the built-in Studio MCP server (Manage MCP Servers, Beta Features). Use a custom bridge only if the built-in one is unavailable. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows)
- If an AI action will edit an existing place or run a conversion skill, first tell the user to back up via File > Publish to Roblox As. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques)
- If a request is a whole-game build, use a master prompt listing map, systems, persistence, offline earnings and mobile controls, with extra high effort; use medium for simple queries. [indie, 2026-10] -- [GuideRealm 2026-10 2:24](https://youtu.be/ltbZzXRrDF8?t=144)
- If the user is learning a system, give pointers and direction instead of full code, and ask them to type suggested code by hand. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Duckable 2026-09 18:20](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=1100)
- If generating a mesh, require a selected Part for the bounding box, one input type (text or reference image), and the 10,000-triangle default unless asked otherwise. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide)
- If generating icons or thumbnails, specify 512x512 or 1920x1080 and tell the user to add text in an editor. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 4:05](https://youtu.be/_Tt-l7EMEHE?t=245)
- If setting up Open Cloud, start with an API key, test endpoints in Postman, grant least-privilege scopes, and move to OAuth 2.0 for user-authorized apps. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud)
- If in-game LLM text will be shown to players, pass it through TextService:FilterStringAsync() and constrain the output. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows)
- If writing remote handlers, validate every client-sent value on the server. [devforum, 2022-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-exploits-work-and-how-to-combat-them/1627229)
- If Assistant output is truncated, click Continue; for complex requests, use Plan mode before Build. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant)
- If a user asks Assistant to build a whole game or write paid client code, advise against it and recommend staged, human-reviewed building.

- If a session is long (around 900k of 1M tokens) or code looks stale, compress it or start fresh with current scripts and Explorer layout pasted in (T13955, T13736). [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 4:48](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=288)

- For plugin widgets, follow the parenting, theme and input rules; don't call CreateDockWidgetPluginGui from regular scripts (T15553). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/build-studio-widgets)

## analytics-metrics (coverage: covered)

- If a metric is below benchmark, then fix the weakest below-50th metric first using same-genre benchmarks. [mixed, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 1:22](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=82)
- If D1 is low, then audit onboarding with a micro-step funnel and New User First Session Retention; D7 low means review progression, D30 low means add end-game. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started)
- If conversion is high but ARPPU is low, then add post-first-purchase content; if wallets keep growing, add sinks or cap faucets. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started)
- If a metric drops, then check timing against publishes, then slice by platform/device/source/version before changing design. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 17:53](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=1073)
- If impressions fall, then check own updates, algorithm changes, seasonality and competitors, allowing for signal-to-impression lag. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/analytics-dashboard)
- If DAU is under ~500-1,000, then skip experiments and use qualitative feedback; otherwise check MDE first. [mixed, 2025-12] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-12 14:08](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=848)
- If an experiment is not significant, then treat it as no change; never ramp a significantly negative result or decide on day-one data.
- If sending analytics events, then do it from the server in a published game, after success, with validation and custom fields rather than extra event names. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/event-types)
- If running ads, then wait 24h learning and 48h delay, use Ads Manager for ROI at day 30, and check Creator Analytics for retention harm; for rewarded video check impressions, opt-in (15%+) and frequency (3+). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing)
- If quoting indie CCU/revenue numbers or after any release, then label indie claims unverified, verify income with My Transactions, and check error report, crash rate and alerts. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 1:55](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=115)
- If a metric spiked after an ad push or update, wait about 7 days and compare the settled baseline before calling the change a win or failure. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=319)
- If judging a new game, use the four-signal scorecard at about 2 days post-release for triage, then decide after 1-2 weeks. [indie, 2026-01] -- [bubDEV 2026-01 4:21](https://youtu.be/2lPsxPtj2mM?t=261)
- If reading FPS, memory or session time, use P10 for the slowest players and P90 for the worst case on lower-is-better metrics; never rely on averages alone. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics)
- If an ad campaign is under 48 hours old, do not decide anything; plays can be credited for up to 30 days. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 11:14](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=674)
- If ad CTR or cost per play is weak and the overview stats are below the 50th percentile, fix the game before raising spend. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Yann 2026-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=408)
- If a DataStore or Memory Store metric reaches 70% of quota, act now rather than waiting for the critical overage. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics)
- If data is older than 28 days (performance) or 30 days (dashboards, thumbnails), it is gone; export promptly. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/metrics)
- If the Benchmarks API returns empty or 400, check availableTypes, benchmarkVariantId, the percentile count and the date range, in that order. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics/benchmarks)
- If comparing a game for purchase or as a benchmark, use income per CCU and spend vs earned, not concurrency alone. [indie, 2026-07] -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 2:45](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=165)
- If setting targets, use your own history and genre-realistic numbers, not front-page games. [indie, 2026-10] -- [tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868)
- If funnel charts are empty, check View Events first to confirm events arrive; charts can take about 24 hours. [indie, 2025-03] -- [GnomeCode 2025-03 0:40](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=40)
- If a player-count or visit drop falls within the August 2026 bot-removal period, flag it as an accuracy correction and compare against real-user trends over weeks, not as an engagement loss. [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656)
- If the API returns a NotStatisticallySignificant or Projected status, do not make decisions from that point. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics)
- When computing weekly or monthly totals, use distinct-user buckets, not sums of daily values. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics)
- When comparing against dashboard figures, convert the reporting day to UTC. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/analytics)
- When an ad campaign produces no Home Recommendations after its run, recommend stopping ads and pivoting rather than buying more. [indie, 2026-08] -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 7:30](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=450)
- When playtime during ads is under 10 minutes, warn that the game is unlikely to reach the algorithm; under 20 minutes on the algorithm is a weak sign. [indie, 2026-08] -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 13:25](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=805)
- Prefer 2026 official Roblox figures over 2020–2021 indie figures and speaker estimates; label low-advice and self-reported numbers as such. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- Do not recommend specific monetisation or traffic decisions from speaker revenue estimates (e.g. per-game income claims) without verified data.
- Keep alert configurations within 20 per experience and use consecutiveOccurrences to avoid noise. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts)
- If judging a game's like ratio after a bug fix, use only the post-fix window and say so. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Poke 2025-12 9:42](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=582)
- If computing ad ROI, convert gross Robux to net first (×0.7 or current DevEx rate), then compare with ad spend in USD. [indie, 2024-12] -- [BubuTheDev 2024-12 3:17](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=197) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the dashboard's history window is short, export sales CSVs on a regular cadence and sum them externally. [indie, 2024-02] -- [PolarBear 2024-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=41)
- If a paid campaign is under 7 days old, do not judge D7 retention; use 7-day moving averages and say the window is incomplete. [indie, 2026-08] -- [LovJak 2026-08 6:49](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=409)
- If a funnel shows a red drop, confirm the step fires in the Output window before concluding the design is at fault. [indie, 2025-03] -- [GnomeCode 2025-03 6:17](https://youtu.be/u_8p65Bsm2Y?t=377)
- If new-player data shows low engagement after ads, split new versus returning players before cutting features. [indie, 2026-08] -- [LovJak 2026-08 8:55](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=535)
- If changing currency, always include an economy transaction type and SKU so sinks and sources can be analysed. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 5:58](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=358)
- If computing revenue, remove your own test purchases first. [indie, 2026-05] -- [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 1:22](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=82)

- When quoting market-share or revenue-concentration figures, name the source and its estimate status.
- If a user asks for scale or benchmark figures from these sources, state that they are creator-reported and undated sampling, not official data. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- If a figure names plays, visits or CCU, keep that metric label; do not convert plays to visits or visits to CCU. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 1:18](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=78)
- If a figure is an estimate or guess (N7793, N7841), flag it as an estimate and do not use it as a target.
- Skip non-analytics items from this batch (leaderboard setup T18049, DataStore debugging T18055, YouTube video views N7799) rather than adding them to the analytics knowledge base.
- If evaluating an influencer or creator campaign, use a minimum 7-day click window and never compare it with UA on a 7-30 day window. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 13:43](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=823)
- If judging creator value, use day-365 or lifetime installs plus a per-market organic uplift proxy, not day 7 or 30 CPI. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946)
- If a campaign is giveaway-based, assume lower organic uplift (~30%) than non-giveaway posts (up to ~100% of attributed traffic). [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=659)
- If a vertical comparison has fewer than 10 videos or about 500k views, flag it as insufficient before recommending a change. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343)
- If designing a live event, write the target KPI first and choose the event design to move it. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 4:20](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=260)
- If payers cluster at fixed spend totals, align price points to those clusters before making other pricing changes. [indie, 2022-11] -- [GameMakers 2022-11 42:46](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=2566)
- If evaluating gacha or item economies, report price, volume, equip rate, spend depth and drop-off point per item. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 52:49](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3169)
- If prioritising features, score each by estimated LTV impact divided by effort (1-10) and sort descending. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 8:34](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=514)
- If early metrics are mixed, monitor the full set over time rather than acting on one reading. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:47](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=407)
- Label all indie figures as self-reported and prefer official Roblox data where available. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- If consent or attribution coverage is low, model results from an opted-in survey and apply them to the cohort, using one method across all channels. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 15:31](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=931)
- If revenue events are too sparse to optimise on, switch the optimisation event to a frequently firing action such as tutorial complete or CPA. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 23:51](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1431)
- If visits are healthy but the like ratio is low, diagnose game quality before buying more traffic. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:29](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=869)
- If a traction spike appears, record its game type, packaging and traffic source before the next update. [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=122)
- Treat all figures from this batch as self-reported unless an official Roblox source confirms them. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 23:56](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=1436)

## anti-exploit-and-security (coverage: covered)

- If a remote, ProximityPrompt, ClickDetector or DragDetector receives client data, then validate type, nil, range, string length and Instance class/location on the server before applying any effect. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/client-server-boundary)
- If a check exists only in a LocalScript or client-side anti-cheat, then duplicate or move the authoritative check to the server; the client check is a supplement only. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958)
- If a movement check fires, then reset the player to their last valid position and log it; do not kick or ban on a single heuristic. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/server-side-detection)
- If a movement check is written, then use XZ-plane distance over a checktime of about 1s with leeway (around 2 to 8 studs or about 24 to 28 for WalkSpeed 16), and exempt known teleports for a few seconds. [devforum, 2021-12] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949)
- If a detection is uncertain, then increment a suspicion score and delay visible consequences; use reversible actions (temporary suspension, resource removal). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/server-side-detection)
- If a player is confirmed as a persistent offender by several varied signals, then use Players:BanAsync rather than only kicking; never ban on a client-side detection alone. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/server-side-detection)
- If a remote is never legitimately fired, then make it a honeypot: log it and kick on firing. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/server-side-detection)
- If a third-party model, UGC or plugin is used, then sandbox it (Sandboxed=true), grant only required Capabilities, and delete scripts from free models unless they are tools. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/third-party-vulnerabilities)
- If a secret, API key or token is needed, then store it in the Secrets store and call GetSecret only from server scripts; never place it in TeleportData, scripts or DataStores. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores)
- If a webhook or OAuth callback is implemented, then verify the signature or PKCE/state, reject stale timestamps (about 300s), dedupe by NotificationId, and identify users by userinfo sub. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud)
- If a remote argument is numeric, reject NaN (value ~= value), values <= 0, and fractions before using it. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882)
- If a client sends an item, price, or currency amount, look it up or validate it on the server; never trust the client value. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/security-tactics)
- If a detection is uncertain (possible lag or fling), neutralise it by restoring the last trusted position before any punishment.
- If the user asks about moderation, recommend the Ban API by user ID; keep Kick only for immediate temporary removal. [devforum, 2024-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-ban-api-and-alt-account-detection/3039740) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the game has vehicles, events, or intentional teleports, add explicit exemption states before running speed or teleport checks. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/network-ownership)
- If a free model or plugin is proposed, search for require, loadstring, and getfenv and check creator red flags before inserting it. [indie+devforum, 2023-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937)
- If Server Authority is available, rely on it for movement but mirror scripted dashes on client and server. [devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993)
- If a feature can be farmed (community joins, new-spawn kills), reduce or cap its reward and threat-model it at 1,000+ calls per second. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/defensive-design)
- If the user asks about account security, recommend 2SV, passkeys and backup recovery; never ask for or handle passwords. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/intro-to-digital-safety)
- If the user wants to test exploits, allow it only in their own place; exploits in others' experiences still trigger consequences. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If a feature is admin-only, hide its client GUI from non-staff on respawn, and still check permission on the server. [indie, 2020-07] -- [Emancyphur 2020-07 1:12](https://youtu.be/7aqTd3PzWWM?t=72)
- If a seat, desk or vehicle is requested, check permission, rank, server type, ownership and state; reject if it is already controlled. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-08 28:20](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1700)
- If an interaction can be triggered through walls, add a server-side line-of-sight raycast as well as a distance check. [indie, 2026-09] -- [LovJak 2026-09 2:24](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=144)
- If a reward is valuable or one-of-one, do not publish winner names, and avoid rewards that reveal identity. [indie, 2022-08] -- [ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 27:32](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=1652)
- If a leaderboard or event result drives payouts, re-verify it on the server before granting rewards. [indie, 2022-08] -- [ThatGuyEvan 2022-08 29:21](https://youtu.be/APeSOO8WRPQ?t=1761) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- When advising account security, prefer authenticator apps or security keys over SMS, and mention SIM-swap risk. [indie, 2021-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-08 22:39](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=1359)
- Treat 2021 claims about unavailable developer 2FA as needing a check against current Roblox options. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Where The Money Went 2026-10 12:58](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=778)
- If the game has player-created instances, tag each with the creator UserId at spawn before handling any callback. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 20:05](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1205)
- If a Safety Callback reports unattributed content, delete or hide the returned instances; do not kick the server or the whole game. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 16:40](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1000)
- If a Safety Callback reports an avatar case, log it and add the user to a deny list; do not expect the platform to take further action. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 16:40](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1000)
- If a flagged build is a false positive, restore it through the in-game appeal flow, not by re-publishing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 24:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1490)
- Do not poll the Safety Callback API; rely on the pushed callbacks registered at startup. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 15:17](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=917)
- Process every callback promptly to avoid rescans and server shutdowns. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 28:16](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1696)
- Do not treat platform moderation as a replacement for server-side validation of remotes, economy, and movement. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/client-server-boundary)
- When the Android trust-level rollout is confirmed, update player handling for untrusted devices and read trust state only on the server. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust)
- When a user asks about content flagged by platform moderation, ask whether the creation was tagged with the creator UserId; without the tag, attribution and targeted handling are not possible. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 20:05](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=1205)
- Mark any Safety Callback, Reviewable Content, or Hardware Attestation advice as early access or announced until confirmed in current docs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 15:17](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=917)
- If a teleport destination or target place comes from client input, ignore the client value and select the destination on the server. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If a value is needed after a teleport and would be harmful if forged (rewards, permissions, currency), do not put it in TeleportData; read it from the server's own storage on arrival. [unsourced, ] -- 
- Treat TeleportData as public. Only use it for cosmetic or non-secure data. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If you are hardening a Luau module table, then set __metatable to a string so getmetatable and setmetatable cannot expose or change it, and still validate all inputs on the server. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-09 16:12](https://youtu.be/Ef2tcqNENSk?t=972)
- If someone proposes __metatable as protection against exploiters, then explain that it only blocks standard Lua calls and does not stop an executor with full access, so the security must stay server-side.
- When building block place or break handlers, run server checks in this order: character exists, target is a valid block in the blocks folder, magnitude distance from HumanoidRootPart is within the max constant, grid-snapped position matches within FuzzyEq tolerance, then the ground or collision check. [indie, 2025-12] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 21:24](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1284)
- Never use Player:DistanceFromCharacter as a security check; compute magnitude against HumanoidRootPart explicitly. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 17:00](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=1020)
- Never accept client-sent placement positions as-is; recompute them on the server. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 13:19](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=799)
- Compare positions with Vector3:FuzzyEq, never with ==. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 23:19](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=1399)
- On PlayerRemoving, save data before destroying the data folder so late remote calls find nothing to modify.
- When a client sends a block request, reject silently or without a reason message, and log it for the watch list rather than kicking immediately. [indie, 2024-01] -- [Suphi Kaner 2024-01 21:04](https://youtu.be/A14qFAyFYhQ?t=1264)
- If writing developer product code, use one ProcessReceipt handler in one server script and never grant from client-side purchase events. [indie, 2024-07] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-07 3:40](https://youtu.be/xaBW50Xz7Vs?t=220)
- If ProcessReceipt cannot find the buyer, return NotProcessedYet rather than granting or failing silently. [indie, 2024-07] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-07 1:50](https://youtu.be/xaBW50Xz7Vs?t=110)
- If a purchase-finished event is used, check the ID and wasPurchased before rewarding. [indie, 2024-07] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-07 3:40](https://youtu.be/xaBW50Xz7Vs?t=220)
- If designing a currency or item giveaway, assume it will be farmed and sold on secondary markets, and cap or gate it accordingly. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 47:06](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=2826)
- If a team or collaborator list is changed before publishing, re-check who can access the place. [devforum, 2025-12] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-requirements-to-publish-and-update-public-experiences/4143953)
- If a feature moves collection of a pickup or drop to the client for UX, keep the reward grant on the server and validate ownership, distance and single claim there. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 6:28](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=388)
- If a design only needs client-side visual collection with no reward, client-side handling may be acceptable. Still check that other clients cannot see or claim it. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 6:28](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=388)
- If a proposal grants currency or items from client-collected drops without server validation, flag it as an exploit risk and require server checks. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 8:01](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=481)
- When citing T18820, label it as a single indie source (self-reported, low-advice) about theft, not cheat prevention. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)

## brand-deals-and-ip-licensing (coverage: covered)

- If a developer asks whether to take a brand deal, then assess fit with the game and community first. Warn that forced branding can hurt engagement and that revenue gains are not guaranteed. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-10 22:41](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1361)
- If a developer wants an IP for an event, season or limited mechanic, then recommend a time-limited in-game sales license on the existing game. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4868288)
- If the game is built around one IP, then recommend a full game license. Do not combine it with a sales license for another IP. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4868288)
- If the developer plans a time-limited license, then set the start date at least 3 days out and keep the duration within 3 days to 3 months. Use the IP only from the start date, and cancel at least 72 hours before start if needed. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/creators)
- If a developer drafts a pitch, then include vision, creative elements, gameplay fit, timeline, named passes and products, and visuals. Remind them to check the content standards first. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/creators)
- If a developer receives a standard offer they disagree with, then dispute it within 14 days. Ignoring it means it auto-activates. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/glossary)
- If a developer receives a conditional offer, then make the changes and mark them done within 14 days. Activation still needs rights holder approval. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/glossary)
- If a license is expiring, then remove the licensed items from sale and the IP from the game within 3 days, and self-attest. Players keep prior purchases. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/faq)
- If the user is a rights holder, then advise on 10+ verifiable images, locking revenue share and scope before first use, perpetual versus time-limited, and specific rejection feedback. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/license-manager)
- If the user asks about brand partnerships without an IP, then point them to the Brand Developer Directory or Brand Link (invite-only, ideally 500k+ MAU). Treat indie revenue claims as unverified. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs)
- If asked about clone protection, cite the lawsuit count as self-reported, from a Feb 2026 tracker blog, and note it is unverified. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts)
- If asked about licensing avatar items to brands, state that royalty and contract terms are not covered by the sources and suggest checking current Roblox creator policy. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GamesBeat 2026-02](https://www.gamesbeat.com/roblox-creators-view-film-and-tv-adaptations-as-a-promising-revenue-stream/)
- If asked about film or TV adaptation of a game, lead with lore and backstory built early, and note that this is a self-reported creator view with no deal data.
