[indie, 2026-02] -- [GamesBeat 2026-02](https://www.gamesbeat.com/roblox-creators-view-film-and-tv-adaptations-as-a-promising-revenue-stream/)
- Do not present the Kickstarter animated-film approach as a proven funding path; it is a planned approach from one creator. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GamesBeat 2026-02](https://www.gamesbeat.com/roblox-creators-view-film-and-tv-adaptations-as-a-promising-revenue-stream/)
- If a brand deal is being structured for a game, consider a character-based integration (as in T17691) as an option alongside logo or event placements. [official, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2026-09 30:55](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=1855)
- Keep any brand character tied to the game's existing story and audience. Do not use it as a standalone ad that players would see as off-topic. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324)
- Do not treat T17691 as proof of payment, duration, or approval terms. Look those up in the official brand program documentation.

## building-and-level-design (coverage: covered)

- If a map has an enterable building, then limit it to 2-3 entry points and keep at least one dead end as a risk/reward spot. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/project-setup)
- If a competitive arena needs fairness, then build half, duplicate and rotate it, and keep both SpawnLocations equidistant from the centre. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/designing-a-map)
- If a scenic city or exploration map is being built, then avoid perfect symmetry and vary layout, roofs, heights and materials.
- If a terrain import is planned, then keep the heightmap at or below 4096 x 4096 px and plan 1 px = 4 studs; if a part is oversized, then split it before Convert to Terrain. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain)
- If a modular kit is used, then give every piece one pivot, lengths divisible by the snap value, and move one mesh at a time. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/modeling/assemble-modular-environments)
- If the player will enter a room, then make indoor walls at least 1 stud thick, set LightingStyle Realistic, and use distinct indoor and outdoor floor materials. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-indoor-environments)
- If a lighting problem appears, then check it at more than one time of day and confirm it persists across a full session. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/enhance-indoor-environments)
- If a part is static geometry, then anchor it; if it is toggled CanCollide=false, then anchor it so it does not fall through the baseplate. [indie, 2023-07] -- [Amora 2023-07 0:36](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=36)
- If the map has model extents above about 64 cubic studs, then split the model into smaller ones and enable StreamingEnabled first. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/slim)
- If the map has repeated objects, then tag them with CollectionService or group them in a folder and drive them with one script. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model)
- If building procedural terrain, apply math.abs to each noise octave before summing, then square the result, and colour by height threshold. [devforum, 2024-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400)
- If flattening procedural water, use inverse lerp above the threshold and keep the original height in preservedBaseY for colouring. [indie+devforum, 2025-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400)
- If a third-person map's interiors feel cramped, do not scale entire rooms up; keep only doorways at least 10 studs wide and walls at least 10 studs tall. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/greybox-your-environment)
- If a route forms a single loop, add a second route or branch before adding visual detail.
- If placing many repeated props, randomise position, scale, rotation and model; avoid a regular grid. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson)
- If an object will not snap, delete it and duplicate a fresh copy from the palette rather than fixing it in place. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/build-one-half)
- If using DepthOfField, use it only to focus attention; do not enable it by default. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/post-processing-effects)
- If the map is outdoors, build ground in Terrain rather than Parts for performance and lighting behaviour. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 8:26](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=506)
- If planning for more than 50 users, verify the map has room for players to interact before scaling. [indie+devforum, 2026-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/voice-chat-is-now-supported-in-servers-of-up-to-100-users/4534031)
- When official and indie advice conflict, follow the newer official 2026-10 guidance and mention the conflict; flag low-advice items as weaker evidence. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/post-processing-effects)
- If a trigger or zone part must be invisible, set Anchored, CanCollide false, Transparency 1, and height so players can't escape above it. [indie, 2023-07] -- [Amora 2023-07 0:36](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=36)
- If a map grows beyond a few thousand studs, plan fixed-size keyed chunks before building further. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-08 3:48](https://youtu.be/0RRGneqSZRY?t=228)
- If a model cannot be destroyed, remove its inactive welds. [indie, 2018-11] -- [ContextLost 2018-11 3:24](https://youtu.be/fNf3JtvrzRg?t=204)
- If a door is visible, make it collide; if it is open, remove collision entirely; never leave ghost doors. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Cumin 2025-06 7:50](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=470)
- If a ladder or stair blocks the character, add a transparent collidable Truss Ladder or WedgePart ramp rather than changing the visible model. [indie, 2025-06] -- [BubuTheDev 2025-06 2:25](https://youtu.be/0UYtSJvYe4U?t=145)
- If a large wall is needed and single parts hit the limit, stack many parts instead of resizing one. [indie, 2024-12] -- [Roox4 2024-12 6:14](https://youtu.be/_E1stqb2fNw?t=374)
- If far-from-origin glitches appear, test scaling on your own build; do not assume the reported 1,000x limit applies. [indie, 2024-12] -- [Roox4 2024-12 3:26](https://youtu.be/_E1stqb2fNw?t=206)
- If a level has long walks or large empty areas, add fast-travel points, markers, or density rather than more sprawl. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 14:01](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=841)
- If a hub has decorative props that do nothing, replace them with functional content or remove them. [indie, 2026-02] -- [Cumin 2026-02 8:06](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=486)
- Before placing blocks, sketch a top-down blueprint with at least three escape routes and check flow. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 0:52](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=52)
- If a scene runs slowly, measure visible triangles from player views before removing content. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 14:45](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=885)
- If a map has many small terrain patches, consolidate them into one dominant terrain before adding more detail. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 22:29](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=1349)
- If a vehicle or model needs per-part behaviour or textures, segment the external mesh into named parts first. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 6:35](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=395)
- Do not plan layouts around terrain scattering, splines or projected decals until they ship; mid 2027 is the stated target. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a large map shows empty areas after the objective is revealed, shrink or cut those areas before adding more content. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 4:57](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=297)
- If building a repeated enclosure or plot, make one template first, then duplicate it rather than building each copy by hand. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/build-one-half)
- If a map has a timed hazard or race to the top, check that vertical transport reaches the top within the hazard window before shipping. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 5:04](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=304)
- If a player can survive inside an enclosed house or structure unintentionally, make the roof or upper structure destructible and retest. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 2:08](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=128)
- If a mechanic needs a carried object to climb, use a ramp rather than a ladder when the object cannot reach the ladder. [indie, 2025-03] -- [BloxxedDev 2025-03 3:28](https://youtu.be/y5aNv16bT3s?t=208)
- If the map is a large lobby, add floor markers pointing to the next location in addition to on-screen objectives. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 12:10](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=730)
- If building a jumpable or climbable platform path, space gaps near 2 studs, then playtest the route.
- If placing block parts for an obby or platforming map, multi-select and anchor them in bulk after placement. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1)
- If the grid size question arises, default to 4 studs (SimTek 2025-12) unless a specific tutorial system dictates otherwise.
- If writing gameplay scripts for a tycoon or mechanic system, populate the map with real build content first; keep placeholders for greybox design only. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 5:25](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=325)
- When code loads a template or pad model by name, add a nil check or timed wait and log a clear error if it is missing. [indie, 2025-01] -- [janisjanis01 2025-01 14:04](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=844)
- When starting a new map or environment, schedule the lighting setup early rather than at the end of the build. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 1:09](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=69)
- If stages fail to connect, check rotation values first (0 vs -180) before changing positions. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 5:34](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=334)
- Before releasing a new area, verify its interaction or egg platform and the pad main parts exist. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 9:29](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=569)
- Before animating a door, split its model into parts and define hitboxes. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 1:49](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=109)
- When placing event NPCs, surround them with scenery so they stand out. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 1:34](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=94)
- When decorating a new area, choose one colour palette and apply it across the area. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 8:07](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=487)
- Keep static map geometry as unions, separate from physics-driven objects. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 8:26](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=506)
- If a map has one spawn point for all players, add more spawn parts and randomise selection. [indie, 2020-04] -- [AlvinBlox 2020-04 14:13](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=853)
- If spawn parts are visible in a playtest, set Transparency to 1 and CanCollide off, and keep them anchored. [indie, 2020-04] -- [AlvinBlox 2020-04 14:13](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=853)
- If maps or weapons are sent to clients by ReplicatedStorage, move them to ServerStorage and clone server-side. [indie, 2018-12] -- [AlvinBlox 2018-12 9:10](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=550)
- If a build will be scaled down to fit a size limit, redesign it for the target size instead. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 1:41](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=101)
- If an interior is the first thing players see at spawn-in, furnish it fully and to scale. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 8:51](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=531)
- If a goal detector must be visible during building, use a transparency of about 0.5 rather than 1. [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 6:21](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=381)
- If a trigger blocks movement or physics objects, set CanCollide off before testing. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 2:47](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=167)

## building-art-and-assets (coverage: covered)

- If starting a new map, then graybox and playtest first, and only then add art. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 10:08](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=608)
- If building UI, then use Scale, UIAspectRatioConstraint, size and text constraints, layouts, and test in the device emulator. [official, 2023-03] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-03 0:30](https://youtu.be/PjwJb_nSePY?t=30)
- If text uses TextScaled, then set UITextSizeConstraint min/max and consider longer translations. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 22:18](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1338)
- If targeting console or TV, then scale interactive UI about 1.5x, set Selectable, and use a grid layout. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 15:04](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=904)
- If an asset is a hat or layered clothing, then keep it under 4,000 triangles, unwrap UVs early, name cages exactly, export selected objects only, and keep textures at 1024 or below. [official, 2025-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-07 4:47](https://youtu.be/dlMWNO5iiGU?t=287)
- If memory is a concern, then lower texture counts and sizes, avoid atlases and heavy UI textures, and unload audio; memory beats draw calls. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 27:38](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1658)
- If enabling mesh streaming, then disable your own LODs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 27:38](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1658)
- If an older tip conflicts with a newer one (2018 vs 2024+), then use the newer one and flag it.
- If colour carries meaning, then add symbols, outlines or brightness differences, and check WCAG contrast. [official, 2023-04] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-04 0:30](https://youtu.be/uumqDp020jk?t=30)
- If using AI for art or UI, then provide a visual reference and verify the result in-game; label indie income claims unverified. [indie, 2026-10] -- [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 2:03](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=123)
- If colour distinguishes a state or team, add a symbol or outline and check the UI in grayscale. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility)
- If a sound cue carries gameplay information, add a caption or on-screen indicator for it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility)
- If UI text uses TextScaled or fixed sizes, switch to AutomaticSize and TextWrapped, and honour GuiService.PreferredTextSize. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/accessibility)
- If the project targets TV or console, build UI at lower resolution first, keep key UI in TV-safe areas, and use ScrollingFrame for dense content. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines)
- If the project targets Quest or mobile VR, check the 72 FPS target and draw calls first, then reduce SurfaceGui count and detail elsewhere. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines)
- If displaying large numbers, compact-format them (e.g. 1k) rather than showing raw digits. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2025-08 5:28](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=328)
- If supporting controllers, add shortcuts, device-matched icons and selection sounds. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines)
- If the project uses the Input Action System, prefer InputActionLabel for prompts. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/console-guidelines)
- When official 2026-10 guidance conflicts with 2023 guidance, follow the 2026-10 guidance and say so. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/auto-translation-overrides-manual-translations/4958947)
- Treat indie claims as self-reported; cite them as low-advice when they are the only source. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a user's object is decorative or unreachable, disable CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery, and CastShadow if it is small (T5828, T5829). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If a mesh will be placed more than once, upload it once and clone or package it rather than re-importing (T6254, T8273, T6912). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If a texture covers a face, use SurfaceAppearance or a material; if it must repeat, use Texture with StudsPerTileU/V (T6079, T8074, T8075). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/textures-decals)
- If sizing a texture, use the stud area: 256, 512 or 1024; cap SurfaceAppearance at 1024 (T9204, T7951). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/texture-specifications)
- If the user asks for a tool or weapon, build a transparent Handle, weld everything to it and set GripPos (T9097, T9098, T5045). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-player-tools)
- If an accessory is requested, make it a single MeshPart in one Model and run the Accessory Fitting Tool before testing on multiple bodies (T9400, T8682, T9074). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/rigid-accessories)
- If an asset is reimported, keep mesh names and tree structure stable, since reimport matches by path (T6992, T6993). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport)
- If generating assets with AI, prompt with the object plus material, age and details, and omit camera and lighting terms (T8241). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering)
- If lighting is set up, choose LightingStyle first, then tune ShadowSoftness (Realistic only), ClockTime or TimeOfDay (one only) and the ambient colours together (T7682, T7684, T9871). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/lighting)
- If indie-only advice conflicts with official Creator Docs, prefer the official item and say so explicitly. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/sound)
- If a request involves grid-based building, keep one increment per task and offset detail by 0.04 (0.2 grid) or 0.05 (0.25 grid) rather than leaving gaps or overlaps. [indie+devforum, 2021-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919)
- If a request involves borrowed or Toolbox models, scan for scripts and oversized geometry first, prefer Roblox-endorsed items, and cite the official caution about Toolbox assets. [indie, 2024-11] -- [Possible Panda 2024-11 0:41](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=41)
- If a request involves icons or reward images, use Image asset IDs, not Decal IDs (official, 2026-10). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/referral-system)
- If a request involves terrain materials, use MaterialColors and MaterialVariants; never delete an in-use material; blend boundaries with low-strength growth. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/design-the-world)
- If a request involves daytime lighting, offer ExposureCompensation around -3 with Brightness around 65, warn it works poorly at night, and set environment scales to 1 for PBR reflections. [devforum, 2021-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improve-lighting-step-by-step/1387033)
- If a request involves performance, default to Automatic RenderFidelity on distant meshes, SmoothPlastic or built-in materials, and check against the triangle and 2048 texture budgets. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design)
- If a request involves AI-generated textures or meshes, back up first, do not edit while generation runs, and treat output as a draft to check against Roblox polygon requirements. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:08](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=608)
- If a request involves animation, set Core, Movement or Action priority and the R6/R15 rig type first, then keep the first and last keyframes identical for loops. [indie, 2025-07] -- [ArTheDev 2025-07 0:50](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=50)
- If a request involves audio, verify the licence, credit the creator, filter for tracks over 1 minute when looking for music, and use positional Sounds on parts or attachments. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio)
- When indie and official sources disagree, follow the official guidance and say which source and date you used. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- If a UGC accessory or hair mesh exceeds 4,000 tris, first dissolve flat edges, merge by distance, and delete hidden faces; only then consider Decimate, as a last resort. [indie, 2025-05] -- [FrenkyRBLX 2025-05 7:17](https://youtu.be/7__AKBlwYkk?t=437)
- If hair is curve-based, lower curve resolution before converting to mesh so the triangle count drops. [indie, 2025-12] -- [FrenkyRBLX 2025-12 0:57](https://youtu.be/rtF3uoHrqU4?t=57)
- If an accessory loses parts in game, check that all parts are welded and that the central part is named Handle. [indie, 2021-04] -- [Emancyphur 2021-04 0:20](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=20)
- If a texture looks flipped or shading looks inverted, check Face Orientation and rotate UVs before re-exporting. [indie, 2025-08] -- [FrenkyRBLX 2025-08 29:13](https://youtu.be/P9RpgVY8sN8?t=1753)
- If a scene looks like a starter place, replace Lighting and skybox first; add Skybox before Atmosphere. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:11](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=71)
- If shadows are needed for a light, use SpotLights and verify current SurfaceLight support. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 3:06](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=186)
- If a free model is requested, check reviews and scripts before inserting it. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar-setup)
- If an AI-generated model is requested, export as 2K FBX, check the tri count, then fit via Avatar Editor Face bounds. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 3:29](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=209)
- If a user needs many similar objects (trees, fences, rows of buildings), propose a parametric procedural model with attributes rather than unique meshes. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 4:25](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=265)
- If a user asks to restyle or unify mixed assets, suggest retexture with one reference image. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 26:51](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1611)
- When planning with an AI assistant or pulling Creator Store assets, first ask the user to choose blocky or photoreal style, since the assistant asks for it during planning (see existing style-early rule).
- For primitive-based builds that need simulation, keep parts simple and suggest upres for visual detail only. [official, 2026-02] -- [Roblox 2026-02 20:07](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=1207)
- For interactive props, ask about behaviour as well as shape before recommending geometry. [official, 2026-02] -- [Roblox 2026-02 4:11](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=251)
- Before recommending the late-2026 collision or 4K texture features, confirm they are available; otherwise give the current workflow. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208)
- Treat OFFICIAL items as platform ground truth and indie figures as self-reported; say which applies when quoting numbers. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 40:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2432)
- If a camera zoom or move ends in a jolt, first print the start distance and height at the loop end and add a small offset. If the jolt remains, flip the camera angle at the end of the move so it looks intentional. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708)
- If you need colour variants of an item, tint a single mesh with colour lerp or hue cycling instead of creating separate meshes. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Duckable 2026-10 16:33](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=993)
- When generating an accessory with AI, use a reference image on a plain white background. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=40)
- Before publishing an accessory, verify fit in Avatar > Accessory: scale until the box turns blue, then raise it to clear the head. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 4:09](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=249)
- Keep the Handle size consistent across accessories (about X≈1.45) so fitted items scale identically. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 5:23](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=323)
- If a user asks how to place or preview a building part, use a translucent cloned preview with CanCollide and CanQuery false, and offset by the raycast Normal times half the block size. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 8:21](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=501)
- If code resizes a part and rotation looks wrong, recompute PivotOffset from half the new scaled length. [indie, 2023-10] -- [B Ricey 2023-10 11:12](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=672)
- If a user asks about free models, separate learning use from shipped use: recommend vetting or replacing free Toolbox models before release, and allow free packs only for background scenery. [indie, 2025-03] -- [BloxxedDev 2025-03 1:31](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=91) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks about story or tutorials, recommend in-world documents or characters before static text or image cards. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 6:40](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=400)
- If a user asks about explosions or impact feedback, add particles and camera shake, and keep the effects tied to the actual game state. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 1:11](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=71)
- If a user asks about camera overrides, bind at Camera.Value + 1 with RunService:BindToRenderStep and verify against current Studio. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 8:29](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=509)
- If a user asks about low-budget art, state that it is only acceptable with a clear visual identity and strong gameplay feedback. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 5:20](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=320)
- Mark indie figures and timings as self-reported; treat [low-advice] items as anecdotes rather than standards. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a task involves a simple prop or decor piece, colour it in Studio first and only switch to Blender when the shape requires it (T18615). UGC accessories still follow the Blender export rules. [indie, 2025-12] -- [janisjanis01 2025-12 4:00](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=240)
- If a user wants to test a new transparency or VFX effect, apply it to one object first and verify the look before applying it broadly (T18561, T18562). [indie, 2025-01] -- [janisjanis01 2025-01 11:36](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=696)
- If a user asks for a quick map or prototype, suggest hand-building from vetted studded Toolbox parts and note the 1-2 hour estimate is self-reported (T18643). [indie, 2026-03] -- [VuukStudios 2026-03 10:54](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=654)
- If a user is granting a tool as a reward, clone it from ReplicatedStorage rather than moving the master copy (T18410). [indie, 2019-12] -- [TheDevKing 2019-12 12:59](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=779)
- Treat all items here as indie, self-reported sources. Mark the low-advice items when giving advice based on them. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a pet, vehicle or other multi-part model will be configured or moved by script, set its PrimaryPart first, then verify it in Studio before positioning (T18772, T18832). [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 10:29](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=629)
- If an imported model faces the wrong direction, rotate it (the example was 90 degrees) before wiring up behaviour (T18759). [indie, 2024-04] -- [janisjanis01 2024-04 5:10](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=310)
- If a mesh is very heavy (the indie example was over 100,000 triangles), reduce its polygons before import; do not expect Studio to handle it (N8089, T18841). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 2:05](https://youtu.be/KhKOSmdX8sc?t=125)
- If you need a glowing look, use the Neon material with colour variants instead of looking for an emissive texture, since Roblox has no emissive textures as of the 2025-03 source (T18863). [indie, 2025-03] -- [janisjanis01 2025-03 3:46](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=226)
- If a model's texture bake across several assets looks poor, texture each model individually rather than baking shared sheets (T18830). [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 5:33](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=333)
- If a task is complex (curved or intricate shapes), add modelling time to the estimate rather than assuming it will be quick (T18866). [indie, 2025-03] -- [janisjanis01 2025-03 5:43](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=343)
- If production is tight and a common asset is needed, buying a cheap pack is a valid time-saver (T18774, N8061).
- If an item is interacted with directly (click to use, consume), prefer a modelled 3D object over a flat icon (T18862). [indie, 2025-03] -- [janisjanis01 2025-03 0:33](https://youtu.be/OAsW5osz46o?t=33)
- If a theme or area has many assets, plan the full list first and build in themed batches (T18857). [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 15:16](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=916)
- If you have finished work that would be expensive to redo (textures, pets), save and commit progress often to avoid losses (T18853). [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 11:36](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=696)
- If a user asks for a chrome or mirror finish, first check whether PBR metalness and roughness can do it; use Reflectance = 1 only as a quick fallback on simple parts. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 0:22](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=22)
- If a project's setup requires manual model placement before each run, consider a server script that builds the scene at startup. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 33:28](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2008)
- Always anchor static decor and give parts identifiable names when adding them to the Explorer. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 0:22](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=22)

## business-and-payouts (coverage: covered)

- If asked about DevEx eligibility, cite 30,000 Earned Robux, not 100,000, and mention the W-9/W-8, verified email and age 13+. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization)
- If a user asks about DevEx payout in USD, apply 0.0035 to legacy balances, which are cashed out first, and 0.0054 to eligible US 18+ purchases; otherwise say the current standard rate may have changed and check the docs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange)
- If the date is near Oct 31 2026, tell the user to submit or validate tax info before that to avoid 24% backup withholding. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/devex-portal)
- If a user has a name mismatch rejection, tell them not to resubmit and to contact Support. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/devex-portal)
- If a user asks when money arrives, give the relevant hold: 60 days for earned Robux, 30 days Creator Store, up to 7 days paid access, ads on the 25th of next month. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-local-currency)
- If a user plans to sell priced assets, check ID, age, Stripe region, individual ownership, and warn against editing bank info after approval. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store)
- If a user is offered a percentage deal for advertising, advise against it; if a studio buyout is offered, compare a 20-50% cut with keeping the game.
- If a user wants to sell a game, recommend bank escrow, proof of funds, reference checks, shareholder sign-off and disclosure of third-party assets. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 28:19](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=1699) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If quoting revenue or CCU figures from indie creators, label them unverified and prefer the official medians and tiers. `x2` [indie, 2026-10] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-10](https://rowatcher.com/games/9363735110/escape-tsunami-for-brainrots)
- If a user wants to use someone's IP, send them to License Manager and mention the 15-30% rev share and content standards. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 6:38](https://youtu.be/oxiaIhN5Ybo?t=398)
- If a user asks about ad or game profitability, first multiply gross Robux by 0.7, then add creator rewards, then subtract publishing fees and ad spend. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 19:06](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1146)
- If a user is considering buying an active game, compute price divided by expected net monthly Robux and warn if payback is long (T6622, N2911). [indie, 2026-09] -- [janisjanis01 2026-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=0)
- If a seller or group requests analytics before terms, advise declining or scrutinizing the deal (T6618, Q1053). [indie, 2026-07] -- [Duckable 2026-07 15:03](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=903)
- If a user wants to sell Marketplace assets, first confirm eligibility, then budget the upload fee and payment advance (T7095, T7096). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/publishing)
- If a user wants to sell a plugin, recommend USD on the Creator Store and note that only taxes and processing fees are deducted (T7554, N3180). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/plugins)
- If a user is setting up a group-owned game, confirm only the owner needs eligibility and warn about the 7-day overdue window (T8152). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups)
- If a user asks for a Robux-to-USD figure from an indie source, check it against the DevEx-implied rate and flag large mismatches (N2523, N2525). [indie, 2026-02] -- [1_Bruce 2026-02 0:28](https://youtu.be/---CLfBUL4k?t=28)
- If a user asks about large sale prices or top-developer earnings, label them as unverified self-reported figures (Q1105, N2930, N2952).
- If a user is planning cash flow, assume a 30-day escrow on sale commissions (T8662). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-editor)
- If a user registers License Manager licensing, keep it on a separate account from published earning games (T9178). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/get-started)
- If a user asks about DevEx eligibility, check 30,000 earned Robux, active Premium, verified email, age 13+, and tax forms, and cite the date. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization)
- If a user asks about a conversion rate, use $0.0038 per earned Robux for Robux earned on or after Sept 5, 2025 and $0.0035 for earlier balances. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange)
- If a user has an active Wallet-eligible US 18+ account, advise on Wallet readiness (2FA, logs, tax forms) rather than the monthly DevEx cooldown. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If a user is paying a collaborator, direct them to group payouts and the CSV upload, not the Transfers API. [devforum, 2026-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/disabling-cross-game-sales-of-passes-and-dev-products-and-introducing-the-transfers-api/4618396)
- If a user wants to transfer a game to a group, warn about irreversibility, the 30-day re-transfer cooldown, and pending transfers that auto-cancel after 7 days. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer)
- If a user quotes a commission, convert to USD first, add tax and fee allowance, and offer a non-Robux payment method. [indie, 2024-07] -- [VuukStudios 2024-07 1:08](https://youtu.be/NfXvHlAdIsI?t=68) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks about valuations or buyouts, compare the offer to recoupable revenue and avoid giving equity for advertising. [indie, 2026-09] -- [ItsZyonic 2026-09 5:39](https://youtu.be/RIWPfMJyVhQ?t=339)
- If a user cites a self-reported income figure, label it as unverified and do not use it as a benchmark. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 40:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2432)
- If a user asks about publishing, state the current requirement of Plus or Premium 1000/2200 and the 80 Robux upload fee. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- If a user is about to submit a payout or a group payment, remind them that it cannot be undone and to check payee history. [indie+devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/managing-your-group-revenue-and-experience-ownerships-just-became-a-lot-easier/4114133)
- If a DevEx rate in any source conflicts with the official docs or with the source's own arithmetic, flag it and do not use it as fact. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Where The Money Went 2026-10 9:49](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=589)
- When projecting creator income, compute gross Robux × 0.7 (or the Avatar price-tier share) first, then apply a low/high DevEx rate range. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Where The Money Went 2026-10 9:49](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=589)
- Never present DevEx cash-out as guaranteed; cite Roblox's discretion and the possibility of rate changes. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 1:40](https://youtu.be/vbzFbOUGERA?t=100)
- Treat indie earnings claims (one day, one game, one creator) as examples, not benchmarks; state the sample size when quoting them. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 12:10](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=730)
- For UGC or avatar item plans, assume a first-year loss unless the user has tested demand; include upload, publish and promotion costs. [indie, 2026-07] -- [ThePurpleGuy 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/zurNJYYXVhk?t=0)
- When the user is buying or selling a game, check the upfront share, transfer timing and written schedule before any payment. [indie, 2026-09] -- [LovJak 2026-09 0:15](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=15)
- When a source is a course seller, ask for item sales data before relying on income claims. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Britt Drake 2025-12 9:45](https://youtu.be/ncZn8_hopuc?t=585)
- If the user asks about taxes or deadlines, check the Oct 31, 2026 tax-info deadline and the 30% withholding and 1099 rules in the existing file. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/devex-portal)
- Label platform-wide figures (22.1% payouts, 15.8% cost of revenue) as cited from filings and verify before quoting. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If a user asks about Wallet payouts, first confirm eligibility (US-based, independent, 18+) and whether rollout has reached their account. Do not assume availability. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- If a figure comes from RoWatcher or GamesBeat, label it self-reported and do not present it as Roblox guidance.
- For platform-level economics, prefer Roblox Investor Relations (T17554, N7264, N7265) over tracker figures.
- Do not turn stock-price, market-cap, or analyst-consensus data (N7126, N7251) into creator advice.
- When modeling income, apply the existing net formula and the minimum threshold (N7231), and flag the threshold as unverified if it is not checked on the official page.
- For Oct 31, 2026 tax deadline questions, state that it has passed as of 2026-11-01 and direct the user to official tax and DevEx guidance.
- Do not cite promotional quotes (Q1757, Q1758) as evidence of payout quality or ease of use.
- Present the CEO's US under-18 share (N7210) as an estimate, not a fact.
- When a user quotes a payout total, ask whether it is platform-wide or per-game and whether it is gross or net of fees. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Prefer the most recent figure (Q3 2026 IR) when older tracker figures conflict.
- If a developer asks about cash-out cadence and is a US 18+ sole proprietor with a live Wallet, say that Wallet pays daily in USD and has no monthly cooldown; cite Q1782 and T17649. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If the developer is not yet in the Wallet rollout, keep the DevEx eligibility and monthly cash-out rules in force and say that Wallet is coming. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If the developer is outside the US, do not state a Wallet date; say DevEx continues longer in other regions with notice (Q1784). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If the game is group-owned, state that Robux still applies to groups until 2027, and that collaborators with a Wallet receive USD (T17656). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- Always label indie-reported figures (N7497 to N7502, N7499, N7506, T17591) as self-reported and low-advice; use official figures (N7520, N7521, N7535, N7536) for platform numbers. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- Do not extrapolate macro figures such as GDP or jobs into individual income expectations.
- Do not combine global creator earnings (N7501) with DevEx payouts (N7521); they measure different things. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Treat mid-2027 as the US DevEx end date when modeling multi-year revenue, and flag any model that runs past that date. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If asked about the expedited review fee, give the current figure and note the September 2026 change (N7515).
- Prefer the most recent official source when older KB rules conflict with it, and say so explicitly in the answer.
- If a user asks about the DevEx minimum balance, state 30,000 Robux as creator-stated and direct them to the official DevEx page to confirm it and any Premium requirement. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 1:23](https://youtu.be/7-b69FYDd5k?t=83)
- If a user asks about publishing costs, quote official Roblox pricing; treat the $1,000 publishing fee as unverified.
- If a user cites a publisher ranking, ask which source and methodology it uses before relying on it. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 38:44](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=2324)
- If an income claim is secondhand or anecdotal (e.g. $20,000/month for a minor), exclude it from benchmarks and say so.
- When modeling passive group or UGC income, apply a decline rate to older items and report gross Robux separately from net USD. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 2:05](https://youtu.be/7-b69FYDd5k?t=125)
- If the user references the Oct 31, 2026 tax deadline, note that it has already passed as of 2026-11-01.
- Do not treat Xbox, Netflix, or other non-Roblox deal figures as relevant to developer payouts.
- When a creator's figure lacks a date or conversion rate (e.g. AUD values), flag the missing context rather than converting it yourself.
- If estimating DevEx USD, use about $0.0035/Robux, label it an estimate, and verify the live rate before quoting it. [indie, 2026-02] -- [1_Bruce 2026-02 0:28](https://youtu.be/---CLfBUL4k?t=28)
- If a user asks when they can cash out, check the 30K minimum, the 13+ age rule, and the monthly reset on the 1st, all against official pages. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 1:23](https://youtu.be/7-b69FYDd5k?t=83)
- If modeling cash timing, allow up to 72 hours for Robux to become cashable plus about one week for bank or PayPal arrival. [indie, 2026-02] -- [1_Bruce 2026-02 1:55](https://youtu.be/---CLfBUL4k?t=115)
- If a source claims a DevEx threshold, rate, or payout time, treat it as self-reported until confirmed by the DevEx portal. [indie, 2026-02] -- [1_Bruce 2026-02 1:17](https://youtu.be/---CLfBUL4k?t=77)
- Do not use 2020 premium-payout or developer-earnings figures in current forecasts.
- If a user plans a launch, include the 100,000 Robux expedited review fee and its refund condition in the budget. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 6:40](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=400)
- If a user asks about commissions, recommend a 50/50 upfront and completion schedule and cite the hiring channels. [indie, 2023-12] -- [VuukStudios 2023-12 6:55](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=415) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks about holding earned Robux, note the group-versus-personal-funds tension and recommend keeping group funds separate. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 5:08](https://youtu.be/XzxoIlpBnJA?t=308)
- Do not treat creator claims about visit counts or top-earner totals as benchmarks.
- Do not state age-rating or ID-verification policy from these sources without official confirmation.
- If a user asks about acquisition or sale multiples, cite them as unverified 2021 speaker claims and rebuild value from the user's own revenue. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=60)
- If a user asks about cash on hand, recommend a reserve before treating the balance as spendable. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 33:22](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=2002) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user cites CrazyGames or another portal's terms for Roblox, correct them and point to official Roblox DevEx terms. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/CN4AJHnlIgY?t=0)
- Do not treat the -21.75 Robux example as a benchmark for expected losses.

## client-server-and-remotes (coverage: covered)

- If an action changes game state (currency, inventory, placement, damage, pickup), then validate on the server (existence, ownership, range, cost) before mutating; never accept client-sent results. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/client-server-boundary)
- If the client needs a return value from the server, then use a RemoteFunction (client-to-server) that returns a success boolean; for server-to-client calls use RemoteEvents instead of InvokeClient. [indie+devforum, 2024-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-2-characters-and-play-it-in-game-like-a-takedown-animation/1625809)
- If a value changes continuously (look angle, tilt, position), then throttle sends (about 0.1 s), use UnreliableRemoteEvent, keep the payload under 1,000 bytes, and interpolate on the receiving side. [devforum, 2019-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801)
- If a payload is large or frequent, then send deltas or changed items, use arrays and packed fields (or buffers), and estimate kB/s against a 25 kB/s self-budget. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-best-practices-how-to-keep-your-games-ping-low/1913475)
- If a visual effect, animation, tween or sound is cosmetic, then run it on the client; the server sends only a trigger and a location (same CFrame/Position for everyone). [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333)
- If a server rejects a client action, then resync the client UI from authoritative state and keep the local feedback reversible. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/network-simulator)
- If the client reads a runtime-created instance, remote or shared module, then WaitForChild with a timeout and nil-check before use. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/module)
- If a script must control only one player (camera, input, UI, push-to-talk), then use a LocalScript or RunContext Client; keep authoritative logic in server Scripts in ServerScriptService. [indie, 2023-03] -- [Emancyphur 2023-03 9:14](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=554)
- If the data should be hidden from other clients or used for cheat-sensitive logic, then keep it in ServerStorage/ServerScriptService or a server module and replicate only what the client needs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards)
- If order matters between remotes or between a remote and an attribute/property change, then add a sequence number or send both through the same remote type; do not assume arrival order. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority)
- If the game is competitive (shooter, racing, sports, combat), evaluate engine Server Authority first and have clients send only inputs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/security-tactics)
- If running under rollback or Server Authority, do not gate gameplay on Touched; use GetTouchingParts in BindToSimulation and store prior contacts in attributes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/deadly-lava)
- If you add a new remote, first check whether an attribute, a replica, or a batched existing remote can carry the data.
- If you call InvokeServer, wrap it in pcall and return a safe fallback; if you call InvokeClient, add a timeout around it. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-an-upgrade-button)
- If a server event fires on join or on a replicated chunk, connect listeners first and handle existing children with GetChildren(). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/services)
- If a client needs to see late-created state, send the current value on PlayerAdded to that player only. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/client-server)
- If you write shared geometry or models, generate them on the server; client-only instances will not replicate. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation)
- If you build cosmetic projectiles or VFX, create them client-side, relay only important events, and validate hits on the server. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/hit-detection-with-lasers)
- If a sync bug appears only on other clients, check whether the source is a Motor6D transform or a client-owned part before blaming the remote. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/security-tactics)
- If a figure comes from 2022-2024 indie posts (byte costs, argument limits, timing), flag it as unverified and re-check.
- If the task adds a new server feature, ship it behind a default-off server flag and ramp it in stages. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 28:14](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=1694)
- If you write a RemoteFunction, give its variable a different name from the OnServerInvoke handler. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 29:32](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=1772)
- If a LocalScript creates objects, do not write server logic that depends on them. [indie, 2023-03] -- [Emancyphur 2023-03 9:14](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=554)
- If an interaction changes player data, route it through a server-side ProximityPrompt.Triggered handler or a validated remote handler. [indie, 2023-08] -- [Amora 2023-08 10:20](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=620)
- If a feature involves timed client inputs (parry, dodge), widen the server-side window to cover latency. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 21:27](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1287)
- If the task moves computation to clients for performance (AI, turrets, aim), keep authority on the server and flag the desync trade-off. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Roox4 2025-06 6:14](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=374)
- When generating remote code with an AI tool, explicitly require server-side validation of every client value, because generated code often omits it. [indie, 2023-08] -- [Amora 2023-08 10:20](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=620)
- If the task needs respawn-safe client UI, fire a server event on CharacterAdded and clean up on the client side. [indie, 2020-07] -- [Emancyphur 2020-07 2:35](https://youtu.be/7aqTd3PzWWM?t=155)
- If state must live outside components, consider Charm atoms with replication instead of ad hoc remotes. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 10:24](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=624)
- If your code relies on objects a client created, do not assume the server or other clients can see them. [indie, 2023-03] -- [Emancyphur 2023-03 9:14](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=554)
- If a game is competitive and Server Authority is available, enable it, then use client prediction for responsiveness and let the server simulate and decide outcomes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/security-tactics)
- Treat Server Authority as shipped (per 2026-09 official statement), not experimental, when answering questions dated after 2026-09.
- If a user asks about offline play or client-held currency, state that the server must validate economy regardless of offline support (T17635). [mixed, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 33:38](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2018)
- If a user asks about async turn-based notifications, confirm push notification availability first. The source gives only a 'late 2026' target (T17675). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- When citing T17600, label the claim as indie and self-reported. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a character respawns, reapply server-applied cosmetics through the server-side PlayerAdded and CharacterAppearanceLoaded path. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 8:44](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=524)
- If a client requests an action tied to player state (sprint, ability, tool use), check server-side health or alive status before honoring it. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 6:06](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=366)
- If a remote is generated at runtime, confirm it exists before relying on it, and validate its input on the server. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 6:06](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=366)
- If a client needs transition UI before teleporting, fire a server-to-client RemoteEvent first. [indie, 2022-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 12:18](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=738)
- If a toggle action has start and stop states, use two remotes and let the server own the state. [indie, 2025-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 13:50](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=830)
- If giving tools or items to players, clone from a template rather than moving the source. [indie, 2021-01] -- [Emancyphur 2021-01 28:25](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=1705)
- If a visual change must apply to one player only, fire it from server to that client; do not expect other clients to see a local change. [indie, 2025-06] -- [ArTheDev 2025-06 12:24](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=744)
- If a client requests placement or item use, check player data, item type, and count on the server before acting. [indie, 2025-12] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 26:54](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1614)
- If credit or attribution relies on network owner, cross-check with server state before awarding. [indie, 2025-05] -- [GnomeCode 2025-05 5:41](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=341)
- If AI-generated code references RemoteEvents, confirm they exist in ReplicatedStorage before testing. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 6:06](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=366)
- If a feature grants a gamepass perk, grant it only on the server after PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished (player, gamepass ID, wasPurchased) or after a UserId-keyed ownership check. Never grant from the client. [indie, 2019-11] -- [TheDevKing 2019-11 11:25](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=685)
- Treat the 2019 gamepass advice as self-reported and verify against current official MarketplaceService docs before relying on it. [indie, 2019-11] -- [TheDevKing 2019-11 11:25](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=685)
- If a drop or pickup is client-owned for responsiveness, then have the server validate and grant the reward before any inventory or currency change. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 8:01](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=481)
- If a tool is held by a character, then do not set Anchored on it; rely on welds and network ownership instead. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 5:06](https://youtu.be/B409dMcW-pw?t=306)
- If a sources' advice conflicts with the server-authority rules already in the file, then keep server authority and treat the source as a performance tip only. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If a script accesses a non-main service directly and errors with 'not a valid member of DataModel', switch to game:GetService('ServiceName'). [indie, 2020-03] -- [AlvinBlox 2020-03 4:25](https://youtu.be/jOFrwMkl8LI?t=265)
- If shared status for all clients is needed, use existing replication rules (attributes or replicated containers set by the server) rather than new remotes; this is already covered in the knowledge file. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 10:24](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=624)
- Treat single-creator items marked low-advice as weak signals and do not add them as rules when they duplicate existing guidance. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)

## code-architecture-and-frameworks (coverage: covered)

- If writing a new class, then use a ModuleScript with Class.__index = Class, a new() returning setmetatable, colon methods and a destroy() method. [indie+devforum, 2022-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-oop-object-oriented-programming/1639499)
- If an object owns connections or instances, then store a Trove (or Janitor/Maid) on it and call a single Destroy in destroy(). [indie+devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061)
- If a feature needs to be shared by client and server, then place the module in ReplicatedStorage; if server-only, use ServerScriptService or ServerStorage. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/module)
- If a script grows past a few hundred lines or does more than one job, then split it into modules with one responsibility each. [devforum, 2023-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585)
- If you are tempted to inherit, then first check whether composition with has-a components works; use inheritance only for shallow families like UI buttons. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Duckable 2026-08 5:41](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=341)
- If a system must apply behaviour to many instances, then use CollectionService tags with GetInstanceAddedSignal and GetInstanceRemovedSignal, not hardcoded names. [indie, 2021-06] -- [B Ricey 2021-06 4:02](https://youtu.be/Vi2FckNJv9w?t=242)
- If a yielding API (TweenService) must be cancellable, then wrap it in a Promise with onCancel and cancel it on Destroying. [indie, 2022-06] -- [B Ricey 2022-06 2:44](https://youtu.be/TeMGAqzZrDc?t=164)
- If a helper reads no external state, then make it a pure function in a shared module. [indie, 2024-04] -- [B Ricey 2024-04 3:00](https://youtu.be/NK8VHcNHJG4?t=180)
- If a value is tunable or appears in several scripts, then put it in one config ModuleScript, not hardcoded. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/module)
- If you need a framework, then choose Knit only when you want service/controller structure and accept singleton coupling; otherwise use a simple bootstrap or loader. [indie, 2022-09] -- [B Ricey 2022-09 17:58](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=1078)
- If the project is small and the developer is new to OOP, use plain ModuleScript classes with one bootstrap per side and skip frameworks.
- If the same logic appears in several scripts with different values, abstract it into one function over a data table before adding more copies. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-abstraction)
- If an object changes behaviour over time, route every change through one set_state, return early on same-state, and reset flags there. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/state-machines-in-depth-tutorial/3157190)
- If two modules need each other, replace the require cycle with a signal exposed via a GetXSignal method. [indie+devforum, 2025-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661)
- If a system creates connections or waits, register them in a Trove or Janitor and cancel Promises on destroy. [indie+devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143)
- If you use CollectionService, connect GetInstanceAddedSignal before looping GetTagged and pass the instance into the handler. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GnomeCode 2026-02 3:50](https://youtu.be/3D0l-WiEhLI?t=230)
- If a name is referenced in several places (module, tag, ability, remote, UI part), grep every reference before renaming, because mismatches fail silently. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/intro-to-module-scripts)
- If a module is required by both client and server, assume its tables are not shared across the boundary and replicate data with remotes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events)
- If you see Knit, Nevermore or Aero in a codebase, treat it as valid but note the inference trade-off and singleton behaviour. [indie, 2022-09] -- [B Ricey 2022-09 17:58](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=1078)
- If a script is over ~1000 lines or a section is over ~100 lines, split it into modules by concept and keep dependencies one-way. [devforum, 2023-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585)
- If a request involves a large catalogue of static items, use one ModuleScript per item with an ID-keyed registry; keep global tunables in one config module. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Possible Panda 2026-01 0:37](https://youtu.be/AOAh-4Q9ARw?t=37)
- If a request involves access control, implement checks as predicate functions (Player -> boolean) and combine them with all/any helpers. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 3:00](https://youtu.be/Xi--YI0Hk7M?t=180)
- If a request involves UI state, create state inside the component function, and use scope-based cleanup if using Fusion. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 15:53](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=953)
- If the Fusion version is unknown, check it before choosing peek() or :get(). [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 13:50](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=830)
- If a request involves shared player state read by UI and logic, put it in an atoms module and use effects to react. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 1:05](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=65)
- If a request involves zones, pick one interaction approach, create one observer per game system, and return exit functions. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Possible Panda 2026-10 1:37](https://youtu.be/C0cOB7Xqc1M?t=97)
- If a request involves many downstream conditions, check the most restrictive first and return early. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-08 19:49](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1189)
- Do not copy-paste code blocks between scripts; extract a function or module instead. [indie+devforum, 2026-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842)
- Do not treat low-advice items as platform rules; present them as options. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Possible Panda 2026-01 0:37](https://youtu.be/AOAh-4Q9ARw?t=37)
- If a project has grouped module folders that may move, use ./ and ../ relative requires inside the group. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 1:51](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=111)
- If you are unsure of the exact string-require syntax, check the official docs rather than copying the source's example verbatim. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 1:51](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=111)
- If an orientation or rotation problem is present, reach for a tested quaternion utility module before writing custom math. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 8:17](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=497)
- If templates are needed at runtime, store them in a dedicated ServerStorage folder and clone them; do not leave them in Workspace. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 8:44](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=524)
- If a static catalogue has many entries of one type, use one table keyed by name; if each entry needs its own asset or script, use one ModuleScript per item. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Possible Panda 2026-01 0:37](https://youtu.be/AOAh-4Q9ARw?t=37)
- If code changes an item count or currency balance, call the single funnel function instead of writing to data directly. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GnomeCode 2026-02 10:54](https://youtu.be/HYBG2AtUBes?t=654)
- If you create a new file, apply the *.server.luau, *.client.luau or *.luau suffix based on where it runs. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Leif 2025-05 4:11](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=251)
- If you write a function that other developers will use, add a doc comment directly above it with no blank line. [indie, 2024-10] -- [Crusherfire 2024-10 0:51](https://youtu.be/1IzNDN6oZP4?t=51)
- If you need player, class or tag observation, use an Observers-style helper and return its cleanup into the main Trove. [indie, 2026-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-04 6:39](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=399)
- If you need a common helper, check a vetted utility library first before writing your own. [indie, 2026-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-04 19:01](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=1141)
- Treat HTML-tag formatting in doc comments as unverified and do not rely on it without testing in Studio. [indie, 2024-10] -- [Crusherfire 2024-10 0:51](https://youtu.be/1IzNDN6oZP4?t=51)
- If the user's project resembles one they have already shipped, suggest reusing their earlier framework before writing new core systems. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 2:43](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=163)
- Treat the time-saving claim as an indie's self-report, not a benchmark. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If an effect should run whenever a state value changes, attach a script to the value and react in its Changed handler. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 10:15](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=615)
- If a single world object needs setup and update logic, wrap it in a table module and require it from the main script. [indie, 2022-01] -- [GnomeCode 2022-01 14:08](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=848)
- If a random selection breaks a rule, re-roll by calling the selection function again; add a retry limit yourself, since sources do not specify one. [indie, 2022-10] -- [GnomeCode 2022-10 10:12](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=612)
- If several conditions all reject the same candidate, combine them with 'or' in one if block rather than separate checks. [indie, 2022-10] -- [GnomeCode 2022-10 12:59](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=779)

## combat-and-gameplay-systems (coverage: covered)

- For Roblox platform mechanics (tools, abilities, input, tasks, colliders, force fields, respawn, damage, teleport), follow the 2026-10 official rule first and cite it; check older DevForum advice against it before reuse.
- For combat design, split each attack into anticipation/contact/recovery and give every move a cost before tuning numbers. [indie, 2018-05] -- [Game Maker's Toolkit 2018-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/8X4fx-YncqA?t=163)
- If you write damage code, check hit.Parent for a Humanoid, clamp to humanoid.Health, and debounce per target. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits)
- If the game is multiplayer, put hit, fire, purchase and team checks on the server; use client events only for feedback. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/create-teams)
- If you use projectiles, prefer FastCast; otherwise use a per-frame predictive raycast, destroy and disconnect on hit, and use origin + direction when the raycast returns nil. [devforum, 2021-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507)
- If you build a spinner or gacha, make rarity weights sum to exactly 1 and convert duplicates to currency or remove them from the pool. [devforum, 2019-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895)
- If you build a shop, add stock limits and a recovery path so players who lose all money can still play. [indie, 2025-05] -- [RoDev 2025-05 10:51](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=651)
- If several servers write shared values, use UpdateAsync; if abilities conflict, declare Blocks/Stops/Suspends/ExclusiveGroup instead of writing manual checks, and give each ability a unique Name. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library)
- If a user asks for a number (damage, RPM, cooldown, tier chance), give the example value and label it as tutorial or sample data, not a recommendation.
- If a source is [indie, self-reported] and quotes revenue, CCU or visit numbers, flag them as unverified. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If melee hit detection is requested, use server-side magnitude or OverlapParams checks, not Touched. Use Touched only for anchored triggers such as landmines. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930)
- If a weapon is hitscan, use raycast with server validation, a per-weapon profile (range, rateLimit, isMelee) and a fire-rate timestamp check. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/hit-detection-with-lasers)
- If kill credit is needed, clear old creator tags, set the attacker tag before TakeDamage and expire it with Debris (about 2 s). [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912)
- If an anti-combat-log is proposed, first confirm that death carries a real penalty. If none, skip it. [devforum, 2020-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912)
- If ability or action gating is needed, route it through one state manager; store NPC state as character attributes, not player-keyed tables. [indie, 2025-07] -- [ArTheDev 2025-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/WImDkrf3sAo?t=0)
- If spam prevention is needed, combine a client cooldown flag, a server timed state and input buffering. [indie, 2026-10] -- [ArTheDev 2026-10 15:58](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=958)
- If weapon projectile numbers are needed, start from Creator Docs defaults (BulletSpeed 1000, ShotCooldown 0.1 s, StartupTime 0.2 s) and tune via config. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/weapons-kit)
- If a shared health pool or respawn cap is proposed, present it as a mode design option with its trade-off, not a default. [indie, 2024-06] -- [ByteBlox 2024-06 1:32](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=92)
- Mark low-advice items as unverified and give them lower weight than official docs or multi-source advice. [indie, 2026-08] -- [JustDucksey 2026-08 7:31](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=451)
- When indie figures conflict with official docs, follow the official docs and say so. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/personalize-the-game)
- If a tool fails to equip, check for a child named Handle before anything else. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 44:32](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2672)
- If you write level gates, use >= and keep requirements in one name-to-level dictionary. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 46:35](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2795)
- If you design loadouts, cap equipped slots (2 is the cited example) and require unequip before equip. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 2:34](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=154)
- If you tune stamina or ammo, test that dash, push and ranged attacks remain worth using. [indie, 2026-04] -- [LovJak 2026-04 6:11](https://youtu.be/1A_sIR4Uhig?t=371)
- If you design a boss, check fight length and that time-to-kill is not one-sided.
- If you build a respawn tool loadout, clone from ServerStorage and check for duplicates first. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 37:44](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2264)
- Treat [low-advice] and single-creator items as lower confidence than multi-creator rules. [indie, 2026-06] -- [LovJak 2026-06 6:17](https://youtu.be/ImcICq5XfL8?t=377)
- Prefer 2025-2026 creator advice over 2020-2022 items where they conflict. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 2:41](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=161)
- If a task involves team sizes, lobbies, or round player minimums, check the Server capacity setting (7 per N7421) before writing code or design values. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/107778070777162/)
- If a mode needs more players than the server capacity allows, reduce the team size or use multiple servers and say so. Do not assume a higher limit. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/107778070777162/)
- Label the capacity figure as a single official source and tell the user to verify it in Game Settings.
- If a task involves creating an NPC, first ask which role it plays (tutorial, companion, opponent or boss) and let that role set its abilities and intelligence level. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:13](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=793)
- If the NPC is a tutorial character, do not give it damage handling or combat tags. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:13](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=793)
- If the NPC is a companion, give it support or follow behaviour rather than opponent rules. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:13](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=793)
- If the NPC is an opponent or boss, apply the existing enemy AI and boss rules for behaviour variety and fight length. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Cumin 2025-05 5:47](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=347)
- If adding a new accessory to a character, first loop the character's children and destroy existing Accessory items before attaching. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 16:52](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=1012)
- If a generated or spawned weapon looks off, check that its handle is attached to the hand, not floating, before moving on. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 3:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=189)
