- If a block mechanic is being built, make a successful block fully negate damage; only explicit guard-break attacks should bypass it. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 12:49](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=769)
- If a QTE is being built, add a short pause and sound cue before the prompt, and keep one input type per prompt. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 14:58](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=898)
- If a central upgrade value is needed, query the central module and subscribe to changes instead of duplicating the value in each system. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Duckable 2026-10 14:02](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=842)
- If a boss relies on repeated stamina-draining actions, redesign the fight rather than adding more of them. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 9:03](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=543)
- If a low-advice item conflicts with an established rule in the knowledge file, prefer the established rule and note the conflict. [indie, 2026-08] -- [JustDucksey 2026-08 7:31](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=451)
- If a new tip only restates an existing rule, skip it rather than duplicating. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 0:53](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=53)
- For wall-stun after knockback, use a short forward raycast after the push ends rather than continuous collision events. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 22:45](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=1365)
- For accessory-caused Touched hits, resolve the character model before applying damage or tags. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 14:55](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=895)
- Before reading a dead enemy's position for loot, check that its root part exists. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GnomeCode 2026-02 13:50](https://youtu.be/HYBG2AtUBes?t=830)
- Give every dropped loot item a Debris lifetime so uncollected drops clear themselves. [indie, 2026-02] -- [GnomeCode 2026-02 13:50](https://youtu.be/HYBG2AtUBes?t=830)
- Prefer one shared pickup module for all collectible sources (map spawns and drops). [indie, 2025-06] -- [JustDucksey 2025-06 2:46](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=166)
- Clone weapon variants from a template tool and change only the model. [indie, 2025-06] -- [JustDucksey 2025-06 4:06](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=246)
- Label self-reported indie advice as such when citing it. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a gamepass or purchase should give a player a tool, clone the tool on the server into player.Backpack. [indie, 2020-03] -- [TheDevKing 2020-03 15:30](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=930)
- Do not trust a client request to grant a gamepass tool; verify ownership on the server first. [indie, 2020-03] -- [TheDevKing 2020-03 15:30](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=930)
- Treat T18416 as a single indie tip and confirm details against official docs. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 14:33](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=873)
- If a new rule is a single-mention tip from an indie creator with no formula, present it as an option to test, not a required pattern.
- If a number comes from a tutorial example (such as N8190), do not use it as a balance target; use it only as a worked example.
- If a tip is tagged low-advice, rank it below multi-creator rules when they conflict. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 9:25](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=565)

## common-mistakes (coverage: covered)

- If a user proposes a large first game, then recommend shrinking scope to one core loop and shipping early. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 46:44](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2804)
- If a user wants to buy ads for a game with poor retention, then advise fixing the loop first.
- If implementing purchases, then process via ProcessReceipt and server checks, never prompt-finished events or client subscription checks. [indie, 2024-07] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-07 3:40](https://youtu.be/xaBW50Xz7Vs?t=220) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If showing prices, then fetch them from MarketplaceService instead of hard-coding. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing)
- If designing rewarded ads, then make rewards deterministic, account-bound, developer products, with no RNG. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg)
- If designing UI, then check mobile layout, contrast, palette consistency and controller support first.
- If reading A/B results, then wait the full duration (about two weeks), segment players, and don't change content mid-test. [mixed, 2025-12] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-12 14:32](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=872)
- If third-party IP or AI-modified work appears, then advise licensing or removal; credit and fair use belief don't protect. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 2:57](https://youtu.be/2HotST5mF3A?t=177)
- If a feature has unbounded counts or referral rewards, then add server caps, one-time flags and cooldowns. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/referral-system)
- If quoting indie CCU or revenue claims, then label them self-reported; prefer official docs for mechanics. `x2` [indie, 2026-09] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-09 13:51](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=831)
- If code connects Touched on a Model, loop over child BaseParts and connect each one. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions)
- If the user wants performance gains, profile first; do not build a custom Humanoid or blame the default one without evidence. [devforum, 2022-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298)
- If the user asks to free memory, clear all references and call Instance:Destroy(); never recommend collectgarbage(). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables)
- If the user passes tables through a BindableFunction, use a pure array or pure dictionary with no nil values. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable)
- If the user plans ad spend, first confirm bugs are fixed and check CTR on a small test before scaling. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Duckable 2026-05 10:09](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=609)
- If the user's concept copies a top-earning game, flag clone risk and ask for one differentiating mechanic.
- If the user is about to set an asset to Open Use or grant a game permission, warn that it is irreversible. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/privacy)
- If player-visible text is broadcast, require the text filter call inside pcall before sending. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits)
- If the user cites PointsService, redirect them to leaderstats or their own data store. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 10:26](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=626)
- If the user asks for a whole game from one AI prompt or a free model they don't understand, advise building in stages and reviewing the code first. [indie+devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838)
- If the user's game uses a deprecated or migrating API (CameraAction, FilterDescendantsInstances, PlatformIntegratedChat, legacy PD), check the migration date first and recommend the replacement. [devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993)
- If the user's game uses Touched or cached AnimationTrack objects under rollback or Server Authority, switch to GetTouchingParts(), InputAction:GetState() and GetTrackByAnimationId. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority/techniques)
- If a reward is tied to fast completion or teleporting, gate it with server-side state checks rather than trusting the client. [indie, 2024-04] -- [ByteBlox 2024-04 3:08](https://youtu.be/7NRp4BEYrsQ?t=188)
- If the user asks about passwords, free Robux or Robux-to-cash sites, warn that these are scams and refuse to help with them. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page)
- If the user asks about launch timing, separate 'polish forever' from 'ship a thin game': recommend a minimum quality bar for the core loop. [indie+devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865)
- If the user proposes a clone or reskin of a hit, flag it and recommend changing the core concept while keeping the proven loop.
- If the user asks how to monetise, recommend designing anchor features first and avoiding gated core movement or camera.
- If the user copies audio, models or code, verify the licence and the source age before use; 'royalty free' is not a licence. [devforum, 2023-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/finding-free-music-for-your-games-both-in-and-off-platform/2179490)
- For production persistence, recommend a session-locked library (ProfileStore-style) and UpdateAsync for read-modify-write. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores)
- When a tutorial or library is more than a few years old, check current API names and deprecations before copying it. [devforum, 2024-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replica-server-to-client-state-replication-module/3216980)
- If a user wants to spend on ads or sponsorship, first check retention, bugs and the economy; advise against spend until they pass. [indie, 2026-07] -- [LovJak 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=0)
- If a user says a game is dead, audit execution and friction (polish, explanation, dead buttons) before advising to scrap the concept. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 6:09](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=369)
- If a user adds a script or Toolbox asset, advise reading it and measuring frame-rate impact before shipping. [indie, 2019-10] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-10 28:45](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1725)
- If a user is building systems with AI or copied code, check that the systems connect end to end. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=205)
- If a user plans a re-release, advise modernising matchmaking and progression rather than relying on nostalgia. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 0:40](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=40)
- If a user asks about events, advise unified, free progression and enough original content per objective. [indie, 2020-04] -- [ThatGuyEvan 2020-04 5:16](https://youtu.be/-4kisZLNwrk?t=316)
- If a user is targeting children, avoid aggressive FOMO and gotcha mechanics. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Cumin 2025-11 8:10](https://youtu.be/66n0P2NtBoQ?t=490)
- Treat indie figures as self-reported; do not present them as benchmarks without context. `x3` [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 16:26](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=986)
- If a user's concept is a clone of a top game, warn about copycat accusation risk even if they plan to credit the source, and recommend original mechanics and art. [indie, 2026-00] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026)
- If a user uses a clone kit or asset pack, flag the asset-flip backlash risk and recommend original character animation. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts)
- When reviewing a game's metrics, ask for monetization per hour by age cohort, not only DAUs and hours. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm)
- When a user wants external video or social feeds in-game, check the approved-link policy first and advise against it if unsure. [indie, 2026-08] -- [eGamers 2026-08](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/)
- When comparing Roblox financial figures, don't equate bookings-based and revenue-based margins. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Treat the Roblox Investor Relations items as platform ground truth and the RoWatcher and eGamers items as self-reported, low-advice sources. [indie, 2024-07] -- [Roox4 2024-07 8:17](https://youtu.be/OYfrdTxNdjA?t=497)
- If a user plans income from DevEx, check the latest announced end date for their region before advising. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- If moderation flags content, advise removing it for everyone, not per-user hiding. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 6:50](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=410)
- If the user builds open-ended creation, avatar, or roleplay features, advise design-level prevention before detection tooling. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 4:03](https://youtu.be/LPMDPxViGYI?t=243)
- If the user adds engagement counters, show only real metrics and warn against artificial counts. [indie, 2026-08] -- [AllThingsHow (guide) 2026-08](https://allthings.how/steal-an-egg-roblox-ban-why-it-happened-and-how-it-returned/)
- If the user designs a one-shot mission action, require clear signposting and no forced-restart punishment. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- If the user adds AI or NPC behavior, default to cheap rules and escalate only where needed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 1:22](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=82)
- Treat indie figures (GosuGamers, AllThingsHow, AlvinBlox) as self-reported; treat Roblox OFFICIAL items as platform ground truth. `x2` [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- Don't cite N7517's 40% as a measured result.
- If code calls ReserveServerAsync expecting a real server reservation, flag it and migrate to HttpService:GenerateGUID plus ReservedServerId before shipping. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- Treat [OFFICIAL] docs as ground truth over older creator advice when they conflict. `x2`
- If a release is under cadence pressure and core fun is unproven, cut scope rather than ship half-working systems. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Duckable 2026-10 24:23](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=1463)
- If a bug fix is ready, run regression tests on the touched systems before pushing. [indie, 2026-08] -- [MonarchDev 2026-08 3:26](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=206)
- If a user asks about home recommendations for near-copy games, state the claim is self-reported and direct them to official docs.
- If a user asks about AI disclosure effects on sales, note the Steam data is confounded and not Roblox-specific.
- If a funded team is planning scope, anchor it to demand and market evidence first. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 46:44](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2804)
- If a submission to a contest is requested, first verify it is Public, runs without errors, and is original work for that event. [indie, 2025-10] -- [Duckable 2025-10 0:29](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=29)
- If copying a setup function between scripts, verify the correct function is called and the prompt or feature works in a test run. [indie, 2025-05] -- [GnomeCode 2025-05 12:02](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=722)
- If writing scripts for others, add comments that explain the reasoning and design decisions, not only where to paste them. [indie, 2024-05] -- [Crusherfire 2024-05 13:38](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=818)
- If a reward is scarce, do not offer a paid bypass; keep the scarcity fair. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 9:46](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=586)
- If hidden geometry or scaffolding is out of view, prefer the simplest working method over perfect cleanliness. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 2:04](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=124)
- If a user proposes cosmetics as a revenue source in a single-player game, flag it as weak and suggest a social layer first. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 55:36](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=3336)
- If a user asks what to build next, rank candidates by ROI and cheap wins before big features. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 27:12](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1632)
- If a user plans to build for another person's game on a revenue share, recommend a fixed fee and a thorough playtest before signing. [indie, 2026-03] -- [VuukStudios 2026-03 5:44](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=344)
- If a user makes a kids' game and relies on viewer counts, flag the engagement threshold risk and check current platform criteria. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 8:04](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=484)
- If a user is about to playtest, remind them to confirm the testing server is not public. [indie, 2026-10] -- [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:10](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=370)
- If a user is about to launch, prompt them to check the server max player setting. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856)
- If a user builds harvestable resources, make sure they have despawn or respawn logic per server. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:58](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=898)
- If a user plans many games on one channel, suggest focusing on 1–3 games.
- If server lag appears with many physics-constrained moving parts, evaluate simpler scripted movement for those parts and profile before polishing content. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 13:16](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=796)
- If the output console shows repeated errors, fix the root cause line first, then re-test. [indie, 2024-04] -- [janisjanis01 2024-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=0)
- If a player reports an exploit or duplication, patch it quickly and re-test the fix before pushing. [indie, 2024-06] -- [janisjanis01 2024-06 1:02](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=62)
- If the game's core loop is weak, do not add manipulative mechanics or monetisation to compensate; fix the loop first. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203)
- If planning a monetisation change, announce it clearly before it goes live; never hide paid progression. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403)
- If using a plugin or generation tool, check community acceptance before relying on it for the shipped product. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 3:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=189)
- Before soft launch, set expected LTV, retention and crash rate by market and device so results are not a surprise. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 42:24](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2544)
- Before any holiday or large update, test save/load paths and each economy path end-to-end. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 6:41](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=401)
- When configuring product IDs, drops or eggs, verify each in Studio and confirm placed models are anchored and present. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 10:29](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=629)
- Treat backlash as a fast-moving risk; respond to community concerns early and transparently. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-07 40:03](https://youtu.be/jgqmsicPUYE?t=2403)
- If core gameplay occurs outside the defined play area, flag it as a risk before submitting to contests or judging. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 41:43](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2503)
- If a new item restates an existing rule about ads or marketing rescuing weak games, skip it rather than duplicating the rule. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 0:53](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=53) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**

## community-and-discord (coverage: covered)

- If a developer is pre-launch, then recommend creating a Discord about a month before release, with invites to friends and testers. [mixed, 2025-04] -- [Roblox Creators 2024-08 23:34](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1414) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If asked where to market, then pick one platform where the target audience already is and move followers to Discord. [official, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-07 4:15](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=255)
- If player feedback arrives, then filter it for fit and retention impact and implement the spirit, not the literal suggestion. [official, 2022-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-11 58:39](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=3519)
- If feedback is from trolls or non-actionable, then ignore or block rather than engage. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Raul 2026-07 6:36](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=396)
- If removing or changing a feature or a monetization bug occurs, then explain why publicly and compensate affected players. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 11:24](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=684)
- If planning an update, then tease it 10 days ahead with a hashtag and date/time and announce it on Discord and the group. [official, 2023-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 16:59](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=1019)
- If using community-submitted art, then ask permission, credit or pay, and rebuild it in the game's style. [official, 2023-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-06 0:48](https://youtu.be/MW-QvpIxO4I?t=48)
- If networking with top devs, then offer value first and do not expect free advice. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 25:34](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=1534)
- If citing indie CCU or growth numbers, then label them self-reported and unverified; prefer official statements for platform mechanics. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If the developer asks about measuring community, then use anticipation of updates and feedback volume rather than total plays, and discount Discord-event-inflated CCU. [mixed, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-07 15:25](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=925)
- If asked about social links on a game page, state the official limits: max 3 links, one per type, 16+ verified age, and 90-day deletion for group-owned games without a verified 16+ collaborator. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/social-media-links)
- If a user is building a group-owned game, remind them to keep at least one verified 16+ collaborator. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/social-media-links)
- If a user reports platform-wide crashes, advise filing a Roblox bug report rather than attempting a fix; if crashes are game-specific, use the community to corroborate. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes)
- If asked about influencer outreach, suggest business email for larger creators and small creators (1-10k subs) for free playthroughs. [indie+devforum, 2023-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If asked about merchandise, check community requests before committing. [devforum, 2025-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/expanding-how-commerce-can-help-you-earn-and-grow-your-communities/3651181)
- If asked about growth, recommend invite rewards with an in-game invite button, citing the friend-join evidence. [devforum, 2023-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Never advise dismissing accessibility feedback; treat it as ongoing work. [official, 2023-05] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-05 1:00:56](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=3656)
- Label indie figures as self-reported and do not present them as platform benchmarks. `x7` [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 16:26](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=986)
- Cite evidence IDs and prefer official 2026-10 guidance over older indie advice when they conflict. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- If a developer asks a Roblox coding question, first direct them to the Developer Hub, Stack Overflow, Lua.org and DevForum search (T5193, Q899). [devforum, 2019-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618)
- If the game has in-game text chat, use TextChatService and migrate any legacy chat (T7934, T10260). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/guidelines)
- If a bug report lacks steps, ask for exact trigger steps, extensions, and frequency for intermittent bugs (T8960, T8961). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feedback-guidelines)
- If the game is pre-launch and has an audience, post devlogs on one platform for about 2 months (T6487); if the team cannot sustain that, hold devlogs unlisted until release (Q984). [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 8:23](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=503)
- If a team conflict arises, move the discussion from positions to interests and use 'I' statements (T6934, T6932). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/collaboration-best-practices)
- If a small audience asks for a Discord, test interest in comments first (T6113, T6135). [indie, 2025-03] -- [janisjanis01 2025-03 21:25](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=1285)
- If sharing an unpublished build, use a private playtest link for up to 10 testers aged 9+ (T7834). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build)
- If a group collaborator breaks guidelines, check audit logs and act promptly (T7773, T8498). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups)
- If an application is pending, expect review within the stated cadence and do not follow up unless contacted (T7487, N3430).
- Remove personal information from any feedback before sharing it with others (T8963).
- If a player requests a ban appeal for an API ban, route them to the developer's own appeal process; Roblox support will not decide it. [devforum, 2024-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-ban-api-and-alt-account-detection/3039740)
- If a user asks how to bring players into a group from inside the game, use GroupService:PromptJoinAsync and mention that Manual Approval groups need server-side handling via Open Cloud. [devforum, 2025-12] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/prompt-players-to-join-communities-from-in-experience/4135599)
- If a user plans a Discord-based tester pool, set the feedback channel up before the first playtest. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences)
- If a user asks about moderator chat access during events, recommend read-only permissions and avoid code that reads message content. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/platform-integrated-chat)
- If a user shares a sudden follower or group spike, check for bot accounts and possible rollback before treating it as growth. [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/actioning-against-bot-accounts-on-roblox/4820656)
- If a user asks about growing a Discord, consider free resources and sharing in relevant community servers, and note that results vary by release. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Raul 2026-06 3:32](https://youtu.be/vnpCimQkHoQ?t=212)
- If a user wants commissions, suggest joining developer communities and posting clear listings with service, price, and DM contact. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Arow 2025-05 1:05](https://youtu.be/JeBDqH7INjY?t=65) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user requests personal information from players or asks for it in community rules, refuse and warn about identity theft and account takeover. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/intro-to-digital-safety)
- If a user asks about a booth or community post as a launch channel, note the low yield (8 players in 2 hours; 1 player from a post) and suggest tracking conversion. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Raul 2026-06 2:30](https://youtu.be/dAL8ohgzN28?t=150)
- If a user cites indie numbers, label them self-reported and prefer official Roblox docs for platform rules. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If a game has rank-gated features, build a deep ranked community; if not, use flat fan groups. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 1:26](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=86)
- If a community suggestion channel gets flooded with duplicates, move suggestions to a merging and voting tool. [indie, 2020-03] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-03 19:08](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=1148)
- If staff are being pinged constantly, disable @everyone and auto-delete staff-pinging messages. [indie, 2020-03] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-03 12:18](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=738)
- If a moderator is a friend but lacks judgement, do not appoint them to moderation; pick by suitability.
- If you cannot commit to a community long term, plan a handover to a fan-run unofficial server. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 7:01](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=421)
- If a monetisation or mission design choice is pending, run a Discord poll first. [indie, 2024-09] -- [CORSO 2024-09 6:40](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=400)
- If a player is harassing developers, redirect them to structured feedback channels. [indie, 2025-05] -- [CORSO 2025-05 4:45](https://youtu.be/O2LZcQG5PPQ?t=285)
- If the source is dated before 2021 for Roblox feature claims, verify before recommending. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 8:02](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=482) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If recommending a wiki, use wiki.gg rather than Fandom. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Cumin 2025-11 3:38](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=218)
- If planning pre-launch promotion, consider multi-platform audience building but keep one primary Discord hub. [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 8:23](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=503)
- If a developer asks whether to allow private servers, explain the fairness and economy tradeoff and recommend investing in anti-cheat instead. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Vindooly 2026-09 10:07](https://youtu.be/GSrXI-biGuA?t=607)
- When citing community or group size benchmarks, label them as indie self-reported and dated, unless they come from an official Roblox source. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If a question concerns moderation messages, advise giving an in-context reason for each block or removal (T17577).
- If a question concerns voice chat and abuse, mention subtle nudges for 13+ users as an option (N7444), and keep voice optional. [official, 2025-07] -- [Roblox 2025-07 15:51](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=951)
- If a question concerns support tickets or reports, first check whether the Creator Hub Player Support tool is available (T17660). Prefer it over a custom Discord ticket system for platform-related matters, and say that this is newer advice. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a question asks whether to keep the community on Roblox or move it to Discord, present both positions (Q1771 vs. the Discord-funnel rule) and do not pick one as settled. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475)
- Treat indie self-reported figures (N7495) as anecdotal, not benchmarks. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If a game has gone quiet for an extended period, recommend posting a status update before recommending new content, and do not wait for a firm release date. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 19:39](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1179)
- When advising on promotion sequencing, recommend community setup before paid acquisition, and paid acquisition before social SDK or commerce features. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:56](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=1076)
- Do not quote the Discord DAU or 30%+ figures as Roblox data; label them as self-reported, platform-wide or sourced from a single creator. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 16:47](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=1007)
- If a player asks how to show live server activity across servers, recommend MessagingService with PrivateServerId keys and a separate subscribing place, and flag it as single-source advice. [indie, 2022-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 40:48](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=2448) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a player asks about group-only perks, offer IsInGroup-based tiered tags as one option alongside existing group-role rewards, and do not treat it as a required pattern. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 4:16](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=256)
- When citing jam numbers, label them as self-reported from one creator and from a single event. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 5:35](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=335)
- If a new item is tagged low-advice or anecdotal, present it as the speaker's opinion, not as a proven strategy.
- When citing the Adopt Me concurrency figure, label it as a 2020 self-reported milestone. [indie, 2024-08] -- [VuukStudios 2024-08 4:22](https://youtu.be/3J7Vp6NgYf4?t=262)
- When a rule rests on one indie creator, say so and avoid presenting it as consensus. [indie, 2020-03] -- [TheDevKing 2020-03 17:36](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=1056)
- Do not add duplicate Discord-setup or core-audience rules; check existing rules first. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-10 13:45](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=825)
- If a community member's concept art, egg, or pet design is used in a game, credit that artist by name and keep a record of the attribution. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 14:54](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=894)
- If a new item repeats an existing community rule (feedback channels, polling, contests, community-sourced content), do not add a duplicate rule; only record the new detail, such as attribution. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 14:54](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=894)
- Treat janisjanis01's 2024 items as self-reported indie experience, not platform ground truth. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 21:12](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=1272)
- If a user asks about running a contest, cite the entry-limit and shortlist benchmarks but note they are 2020 indie self-reports.
- If a user asks about stream-driven growth, present the Mulch figures as a single self-reported stream snapshot, not a predictable rate. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 5:35](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=335)
- Treat all indie figures in this batch as self-reported and do not present them as platform ground truth. `x3` [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)

## core-loops-and-game-design (coverage: covered)

- If asked to design a game, first write the core loop as action, reward, upgrade, repeat, then prototype it with greybox before adding systems. [official, 2022-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-06 0:23](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=23)
- If a proposed feature does not feed the core loop, recommend cutting it or reshaping it; if it creates a parallel loop or opt-out path, flag it as a tumor. [official, 2022-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-06 38:48](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2328) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If progression feels slow, suggest front-loading early rewards (unlock every few minutes, low early XP) and a goal within 10-15 minutes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding)
- If designing a simulator, tycoon or clicker, include currency, multiplier upgrades, rebirth, pets and rarity, and also add depth, skill or non-numeric challenge. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 5:07](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=307)
- If adding RNG, roll from the rarest tier, store weights in a config, and decide whether rarity is hidden until reveal. [indie+devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401)
- If the economy inflates, add sinks, caps or drains; use a separate event currency for events. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/economy-events)
- If asked about quests or season passes, tie tasks to the core loop, avoid Robux-gated tasks, keep dailies inside one session, and release missions daily. [official, 2021-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-10 0:05](https://youtu.be/1stxDQeHhr8?t=5)
- If a game is solo or prone to empty servers, add AI/practice modes and social hooks (trading, visible progress, parties). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox)
- If retention is low, fix core gameplay before monetisation and check funnel drop-offs; prefer official statements over indie self-reported metrics. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- If tuning movement or combat, adjust friction/acceleration, trigger on input Begin, telegraph attacks, and avoid solved strategies. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start)
- If a design involves automated or AI-driven actors, then restrict them to defined events and an explicit allowlist of actions. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227)
- If a core system (health, combat, inventory, rewards) is involved, then implement it in deterministic game code, not in dynamic behaviour. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227)
- If an AI needs quick reactions, then use the human-like agent model. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 21:21](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1281)
- If an AI needs long-range planning or simple puzzle logic, then start with behaviour trees and add human-like agents only if needed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 21:21](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1281)
- If a design relies on automation, then keep the designer in control of normal play and let automation act only in unusual situations. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 3:47](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=227)
- Cite only the official Roblox sources for these points; no indie creator evidence exists yet for this topic.
- If designing zones or areas, then gate each one behind a visible speed or progression tier, and check thresholds against current income. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Mulch 2026-09 1:36](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=96)
- If a playtest shows players rushing content, then add an unlock gate before launch. [indie, 2026-10] -- [LovJak 2026-10 2:04](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=124)
- If one solo tester can finish the whole loop alone, then fix that before adding any new system. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:32](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=152)
- If a loop has no pending missions or goals, then add one before shipping. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 12:31](https://youtu.be/3bUysm7IFxg?t=751)
- If an optional activity has a reward that is small relative to its time cost, then raise the reward or cut the activity. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 5:23](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=323)
- If a secondary mini-game is taking over playtime or income, then move income back to the core loop. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 10:06](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=606)
- If the loop has only one optimal action (for example fill-to-max), then add a trade-off that creates real choice. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 4:17](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=257)
- If a player dies, then respawn at spawn by default unless the design needs otherwise. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 14:29](https://youtu.be/9sTCUPeaJqM?t=869)
- If inventories grow large, then add sell-all and equip-best buttons. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Mulch 2026-09 12:06](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=726)
- If camera control is needed for a custom view, then set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable and restore Custom afterward. [devforum, 2021-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966)
- If a mechanic is meant to create pressure, verify it has a drain, cost, or consequence; if none, add one. [indie, 2025-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 0:40](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=40)
- If designing a sell loop, add a storage cap plus a paid upgrade to create a sink. [indie, 2022-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 36:01](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=2161)
- If designing turn-based combat, tick statuses at end of the unit's turn and use one gate per action. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 12:06](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=726)
- If designing multiplayer turn-based combat, default to team-wide turns to cut downtime. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 16:12](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=972)
- If asked for numbers, present demo values as starting points and explain they are self-reported.
- If horror is the genre, prefer sanity or speed mechanics over monsters for pressure. [indie, 2025-03] -- [BloxxedDev 2025-03 4:37](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=277)
- If spawns cause unwinnable rounds, target spawns near players before tuning difficulty. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 6:42](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=402)
- If a design proposes a paywall, first check whether players can sample that content type for free. [indie, 2022-11] -- [GameMakers 2022-11 10:11](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=611)
- If you add a new power or stat, schedule a balance and nerf review in the next playtest cycle, not as optional follow-up. [indie, 2026-10] -- [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:52](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=412)
- If a bonus references another object, test the feature with that object absent. [indie, 2026-10] -- [janisjanis01 2026-10 6:52](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=412)
- If a mechanic is passive walk-and-collect, consider an active interaction step before adding more rewards. [indie, 2025-01] -- [janisjanis01 2025-01 9:15](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=555)
- When tuning difficulty, prioritise playtester feedback over assumptions.
- Treat the indie figures (18.5k, 8 bags, 31 highlight limit) as examples, not benchmarks; they are self-reported. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- If the user edits a drop table or egg weight, remind them to verify observed rates in play before shipping. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 4:17](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=257)
- If a resource drains over time, compute time-to-empty versus time-to-find and flag any case where players run out first. [indie, 2025-10] -- [janisjanis01 2025-10 15:56](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=956)
- If a multiplier compounds with a count such as rebirths, test it at the maximum expected count before recommending it. [indie, 2024-06] -- [janisjanis01 2024-06 0:43](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=43) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the core loop is not yet fun, advise against adding psychology hooks, retention mechanics or polish. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 3:23](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=203)
- If the game shows loading screens between loop actions, recommend removing or shortening them to keep flow. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 2:41](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=161)
- If a spawn or wave loop has no end state, add a game-over or termination trigger before adding other features. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 2:32](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=152)
- If you build a weighted random table, give every entry a numeric weight (use 0 for never-random entries) and use the cumulative-sum roll. [indie, 2022-10] -- [GnomeCode 2022-10 16:15](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=975)
- If weights include fractions, convert them to integers before using an integer random roll; do not assume fractional totals work with math.random as written in the source. [indie, 2022-10] -- [GnomeCode 2022-10 16:15](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=975)
- If the map is very small, prefer a build-to-earn or key-hunt progression before adding distance-based zoning. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 36:14](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2174)
- If you generate layouts procedurally, test at least 100 samples for loops and dead ends before accepting the generator. [indie, 2022-10] -- [GnomeCode 2022-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/QLoF9Er8Oo8?t=0)
- If you use legacy examples (wait(2), 2020-era tutorials), verify them against current API docs before copying. [devforum, 2018-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677)

## cross-platform-and-console (coverage: covered)

- If the experience targets any non-PC platform, then ensure touch works: tappable prompts (T7399), readable UI and smooth performance on phones (T6868), and treat console and mobile optimization as recommended (T9527). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/standout-games)
- If you define gameplay inputs, then use Input Action System actions with multiple bindings and do not hardcode keys (T7087, T7754). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/gamepad)
- If the UI needs to adapt to input, then read PreferredInput (T7084, T7755); if it is insufficient, add GamepadConnected/GamepadDisconnected or GamepadEnabled checks (T7090). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/gamepad)
- If the player can switch input mid-session, then test UI updates when a controller connects or a trackpad switches to mouse (T9701). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware)
- If you build an Android TV experience, then design D-pad-friendly controls (T6935, T6937), test with TV Remote simulation and hardware (T6936), and use the 1920x1080 emulator with the TV Remote Controller Emulator if no hardware (T8204, N3371). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/android-tv-pilot)
- If you detect a TV remote, then check Gamepad1 for no Thumbstick1/2 and ButtonCenter support, not keycodes alone (T8198, T8199); map ButtonCenter to ButtonA, ButtonBack to ButtonB, and D-pad keys to DPad (T8200). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/micro-gamepad)
- If nav and gameplay share a remote button, then use separate input contexts (T8201). If you hide default touch controls, then set GuiService.TouchControlsEnabled=false only when you supply your own (T8194). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers)
- If the mouse is locked, then read InputChanged MouseMovement Delta instead of position (T7759). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mouse-and-keyboard)
- If you build VR, then configure OpenXR before launching Studio (T8292), test each hand separately (T8630), and use Reset Positions when stuck (T8631). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/testing-modes)
- If you override default controls or a gameplay cue relies on grass motion, then override only for specific needs (T9391) and add a non-motion cue for Reduce Motion players (T7259). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/terrain)
- If the target includes VR, first test whether default player scripts work, then add custom VR code only where needed. [devforum, 2023-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-is-coming-to-meta-quest/2465291)
- If building custom VR hands, disable controller models and the laser pointer first, then drive hand positions from UserCFrameChanged. [devforum, 2021-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966)
- If building VR, anchor HumanoidRootPart when the default character should not move or show. [devforum, 2021-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966)
- If building mobile UI, place controls in a 'Mobile Controls' ScreenGui and enable it only when TouchEnabled is true. [indie, 2023-09] -- [VuukStudios 2023-09 0:37](https://youtu.be/dcJtZnan7i8?t=37)
- If using gestures, gyro, or accelerometer input, check AccelerometerEnabled, GyroscopeEnabled, and gesture support first. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mobile)
- If the UI depends on orientation, set separate emote bar positions and listen to ScreenOrientation changes. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/mobile)
- If the experience needs Voice Chat, keep max players at 100 or fewer and restart servers after lowering the limit. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/voice-chat)
- If the target includes console or VR, plan a fallback for the age check flow. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091)
- If referencing default PC keybinds, mark them as beta and read bindings at runtime instead of hardcoding them. [devforum, 2026-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904)
- When writing new bindings, prefer the Input Action System over ContextActionService with KeyCodes. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input/micro-gamepad)
- If code uses mouse-only click events for buttons and the project targets multiple platforms, switch to Activated-style events. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 12:12](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=732)
- If an experience is planning console or mobile support, check current concurrency and analytics first; do not treat the ~10-player threshold as official. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091)
- If citing platform split or VR share numbers, state that they are self-reported and dated (2021), and prefer current data. [indie, 2021-12] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-12 8:52](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=532)
- If a user asks how players should join, recommend the Details Page URL or direct link and note Web Play is Chrome first. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- If a user is planning cross-platform release, use one codebase, confirm cross-play, and gate differences by input type. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If a user asks about launch platforms, prefer the Nov 2026 official guidance (support all platforms at launch) and flag the staged-rollout advice in the existing file as older. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- If a user targets mobile, include low-end Android testing before launch. [indie, 2026-05] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/)
- Do not state offline solo play as available or confirmed; cite it as a forecast only.
- Do not claim Web Play supports browsers other than Chrome; sources only cover Chrome.
- Do not plan console, mobile or VR launch scope around T17674 or T17684; both are general multiplayer or solo-mode roadmap items with no platform input changes. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- If a task depends on turn-based or async multiplayer notifications, verify current availability in Studio and the official Newsroom before building on it, since the schedule was announced as planned. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a request asks whether to ship one cross-play codebase or separate pure-play builds, note the disagreement (T17948 vs the existing cross-play rule) and ask for the target audience's device mix before recommending. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 15:36](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=936)
- If citing the 56% mobile login figure (N7709), label it as self-reported and from a single Discord community, not an official platform statistic.
- Do not treat single-mention indie claims (T17948, T17972) as established best practice.
- If a planning decision depends on the console install base or a pre-order attach rate, cite the figure as self-reported (N8159, N8160) and verify it independently before relying on it.
- Do not treat indie-creator market estimates as platform ground truth; [OFFICIAL] items take precedence. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)

## data-persistence (coverage: covered)

- If a DataStore call is written, then wrap it in pcall, check success, and warn(err) on failure. [indie+devforum, 2024-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastore-tutorial-for-beginners/733973)
- If the load pcall fails, then use defaults, mark the profile errored so it never saves over real data, and tell the player. Prefer this over kicking. [indie+devforum, 2021-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/data-storingpresenting-using-profileservice-and-replicaservice/1464984)
- If a player can be loaded on more than one server, then use a session lock, preferably ProfileStore/ProfileService, and store game.JobId with os.time timestamps. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/player-data-purchasing)
- If you hand-roll a session lock, then treat sessions older than 30 minutes as dead, keep the lock during autosave, and release only on leave when the JobId matches. [devforum, 2020-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/session-locking-explained-datastore/846799)
- If writing production data that another server might change, then use UpdateAsync and not GetAsync+SetAsync. Use SetAsync only for demos. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/hash-map)
- If the server is shutting down, then save all players in parallel inside BindToClose, using a counter, within the 30 s shared budget. Decrement the counter on every early return. [indie+devforum, 2024-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421)
- If a player is leaving, then confirm data is loaded and the player is still in Players before saving. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/player-data-purchasing)
- If progress updates every event, then keep it in a local table and save on an autosave timer (30-120 s with random offset) and on leave. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/save-data)
- If storing Vector3/CFrame/Instances, then convert to X,Y,Z numbers, number arrays, or property-name dictionaries. Never pre-JSONEncode before SetAsync. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/save-player-data)
- If data is temporary (queues, daily or monthly boards), then use MemoryStore with expiration; if it is permanent progress, then use DataStoreService. If it is a numeric leaderboard, then use OrderedDataStore. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores)
- If the game needs player data across multiple servers, use ProfileStore; do not write raw GetAsync/SetAsync without a lock. If hand-rolling a lock, expire it with MemoryStore, not os.time. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/player-data-purchasing)
- If a load still fails after retries, kick the player with a 'progress is safe' message; never save default data over real data. [indie, 2023-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 20:22](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=1222)
- If a write depends on the current value, use UpdateAsync with a transform function; for new keys or independent values use SetAsync. Always pass metadata. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores)
- If a save approaches the size limit, measure with HttpService:JSONEncode length first, then integer-scale, flatten and comma-join; compress only as a last resort. [indie, 2023-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 28:20](https://youtu.be/ykWkDov_x-8?t=1700)
- If migrating from DataStore2, use the official migration tool; do not start new experiences on DataStore2.
- If a write must reach a player locked on another server, use ProfileStore:MessageAsync or DataStore:Queue; do not write the locked store directly. [indie+devforum, 2024-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543)
- If testing, use a separate test version and index; never enable Studio API access on the live experience. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores)
- If user data must be deletable, define RTBF templates and use User_{UserId} keys before launch, then verify deletion end-to-end. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/right-to-be-forgotten)
- If you need real-time cross-server leaderboards, use MemoryStore; use OrderedDataStore for stored leaderboards with a few seconds of lag. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores)
- On shutdown, save all players in parallel within 30 s, then wait about 3 s before the server closes. [indie+devforum, 2024-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421)
- If starting a new project with player data, use ProfileStore. For an existing ProfileService project, do not migrate for this reason alone. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Possible Panda 2025-12 0:00](https://youtu.be/0rmh0FpHBYM?t=0)
- If a handler touches player data and the profile is nil, return early; do not create a new profile on the fly. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 5:18](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=318)
- If you change profile.Data that is shown in leaderstats, update the leaderstats value in the same code path. [indie, 2023-08] -- [Amora 2023-08 11:43](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=703)
- If you add a new saved field, add it to the template with a default and rely on Reconcile to fill it for existing players. [indie+devforum, 2024-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543)
- If you define a saved integer, set its min and max in the template and clamp on load. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 1:12](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=72)
- If scoping a save system, prioritize currency, unlocks and loadouts before cosmetic or secondary systems. [indie, 2026-06] -- [studio rook. 2026-06 0:43](https://youtu.be/WCIBNClvUrQ?t=43)
- If a player's load fails, kick them rather than letting them play unsaved. [indie, 2023-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 20:22](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=1222)
- If testing DataStores in Studio, confirm the Studio API access setting is on first. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/save-data)
- If built-in session locking ships, evaluate it against the ProfileStore and hand-rolled lock rules before changing them. Do not delete the existing lock rules until the official docs confirm the new behavior. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208)
- If GetHistogramAsync is not in the API reference yet, do not generate code that calls it. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation)
- For batched leaderboard reads, use OrderedDataStore BatchGet now and handle missing entries. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208)
- When citing roadmap items, state the date you read them, because shipped items disappear after six months.
- If a task involves teleporting players between places or servers, check that secure state is persisted to a DataStore or MemoryStore and not held only in local or teleport data. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If a task involves reserved servers, store the reserved server ID keyed by session or party ID so rejoin works across sessions. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- Treat these two items as official ground truth from the 2026-10 Creator Docs. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If saving a CFrame, store position and look vector as numbers and rebuild it with CFrame.new(pos, pos + look). [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 2:46](https://youtu.be/PHpJQn2LvSo?t=166)
- If testing saves in Studio, do not rely on PlayerRemoving alone. Verify with the autosave loop. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 8:14](https://youtu.be/9sTCUPeaJqM?t=494)
- If setting up production autosave, use about 60 seconds. Use 3 seconds only in test builds.
- If a player's character is moved to a saved position, do it after CharacterAdded. [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 3:31](https://youtu.be/9sTCUPeaJqM?t=211)
- If a new saved system is added, verify persistence by leaving and rejoining before moving on. [indie, 2026-10] -- [LovJak 2026-10 6:17](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=377)
- If a cache holds player objects, use a weak table (__mode 'k' or 'v') so entries can be collected. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-09 17:43](https://youtu.be/Ef2tcqNENSk?t=1063)
- If a user cites a DataStore budget or throttle, confirm it against current official Roblox docs before using it as a constant. [indie, 2022-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 0:40](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=40)
- On a failed load or save, retry with capped backoff first and kick only after retries fail; do not follow the 2022 kick-immediately pattern. [indie, 2023-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 20:22](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=1222)
- Before writing a key, check the ~6 s same-key cooldown and coalesce changes in memory. [indie, 2022-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-06 0:40](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=40)
- Detect reserved servers via PrivateServerId and PrivateServerOwnerId before running DataStore logic. [indie, 2022-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 25:53](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=1553)
- Do not change a store name unless the user confirms a deliberate data wipe. [indie, 2024-05] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/KQMdyljRh28?t=0)
- For new projects, recommend ProfileStore over ProfileService or DataStore2. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Possible Panda 2025-12 0:00](https://youtu.be/0rmh0FpHBYM?t=0)
- When debugging DataStore errors in Studio, first confirm Studio API access is enabled. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/save-data)
- When using a DataStore module, review its changelog before updating; treat major version bumps as breaking. [indie, 2023-06] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-06 0:15](https://youtu.be/4rNva5qXj-c?t=15)
- Use WaitForChild with a timeout where a script depends on folders created by another script. [indie, 2022-12] -- [LovJak 2022-12 1:22](https://youtu.be/wJdljYuxApU?t=82)
- If a task grants gamepass or purchase-based perks, add an ownership check in the PlayerAdded path in addition to the purchase handler. [indie, 2019-11] -- [TheDevKing 2019-11 24:07](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=1447)
- If a purchase rule and a perk-on-join rule conflict, keep the purchase handler for new purchases and the join check for existing ownership. [indie, 2019-11] -- [TheDevKing 2019-11 24:07](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=1447)
- If shop or pack state looks stale or stuck, first check which data source is authoritative, then check the order of refresh calls. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 7:48](https://youtu.be/B409dMcW-pw?t=468)
- If an item is destroyed or consumed, confirm its open or award action has finished first. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 6:18](https://youtu.be/B409dMcW-pw?t=378)
- Treat these shop and pack tips as single-source and self-reported; prefer official docs when they conflict. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)

## development-process-and-speed (coverage: covered)

- If the user is a beginner, then recommend a simple, finishable game (simulator, obby, hot potato) and scope to under ~1 month. [mixed, 2024-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-01 16:56](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=1016) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If asked what to build first, then define a single core loop MVP and defer extra features to post-launch updates. [mixed, 2023-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-11 22:07](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1327)
- If a mechanic is risky or physics-heavy, then recommend a narrow Studio prototype (1-2 weeks) before full development. [official, 2023-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a game has no traction after 1-2 weeks, then suggest pivoting or shelving; if it is a live game with an audience, advise continuing updates.
- If planning a live update, then apply a 2-3 week cap, use existing systems, put it behind a config flag, and run a retro afterward. [official, 2025-12] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-12 1:04](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=64)
- If giving schedule estimates, then use 6 productive hours per day and add buffer (solo ~2x). [mixed, 2026-01] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 27:41](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=1661)
- If a deadline looms, then cut features, fix critical bugs, and ship. [mixed, 2025-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-07 18:34](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1114)

## discovery-algorithm (coverage: covered)

- If advising on discovery, base mechanics on the official 2026 signals (PTR, first-play bounce, play days, playtime, spend, co-play, 28-day retention), not the older qPTR-only model. [mixed, 2026-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-10 5:54](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=354)
- If a game gets no Home impressions, check genre, custom thumbnail and Exclude from Recommendations first. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools)
- If bounce rate is high, align the icon with the first 30-180 seconds and fix onboarding before spending on ads. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery)
- If using ads, spread small spend over 2-4 weeks, watch for Home impressions, and stop when organic sustains the game. [indie, 2026-07] -- [VuukStudios 2026-07 3:31](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=211)
- If no Home recs after 7-14 days of ads, recommend reassessing or moving on, and label it as indie heuristic. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=490)
- If a user wants longer sessions, note the 60 min/day cap and avoid addictive loops. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery)
- If Home impressions fluctuate after a surge or update, advise waiting for the new baseline and checking seasonality and competitors. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq)
- If the user targets under-16 or Select, check HEP counts against the current threshold and the 25 maintenance floor. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2026-09 15:30](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=930)
- If quoting indie CCU or visit figures, mark them unverified; prefer official statements on conflicts.
- If the game is niche, suggest search-niche targeting and YouTuber outreach as seeds.
- If advice refers to 7-day RFY or week-one retention, rewrite it using the 28-day window and the day 1 / day 2-7 / day 8-28 checkpoints. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- Use 30-90 seconds as the first-play bounce window (official, July 2026), not 30-180 seconds. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery)
- Report play-through rate, first-play bounce, session quality and spend separately; never blend them into one score. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-07](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026051059/ex991-robloxq22026earnin.htm)
- Label indie-reported figures (HEP 250 threshold, under-13 monetisation penalty, age-based session preferences, growth test) as unverified. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- Do not use chart positions, CCU or player counts as ranking inputs; they are single-day snapshots. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics)
- For US under-13 audiences, flag short-term monetisation as a possible impression risk and recommend a before/after impression check. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- When discussing new games, cite the top-10 share drop and the ~40% recent-content figure as evidence of room, not as a guarantee. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- When an official [OFFICIAL] source conflicts with an indie source, prefer the official source and note the conflict. `x3` [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- Keep recommendations tied to retention and first-play metrics; do not suggest thumbnail-only fixes.
- If a claim comes from a single indie source and is not confirmed by official Roblox material, mark it as unverified.
- If a feature is dated 'late 2026' or later, check its current status before advising a developer to depend on it. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the 7-14 day ad decision rule conflicts with the 28-day window, follow the 28-day window and say so. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=490)
- Do not treat CCU, visits or active-player spikes as ranking inputs, even when they appear in case studies.

- When advising on discovery, prioritise CTR (title and thumbnail) before feature work, since impressions only feed ranking through clicks. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 0:41](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=41)
- Do not present self-reported figures (40-50% retention, 50-player gate) as official Roblox thresholds. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=490)
- If a game was launched or changed less than about a week ago, do not conclude the algorithm rejected it. Say the data window is still open.

- If a game has good retention but low CCU, check Home recommendation pickup before recommending a gameplay change. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 7:28](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=448)
- If the user asks when a new game will be picked up, answer about one week, with two weeks as the older range, and note these are self-reported indie figures. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 20:20](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1220)
- Label the one-week and 7-day window claims as creator-reported, not official Roblox guidance. [indie, 2026-06] -- [rasdev 2026-06 3:23](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=203)

## events-and-seasonal-content (coverage: covered)

- If designing event rewards, then require a challenge or skill action rather than a join-time giveaway. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-09 7:04](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=424)
- If making a limited-time item, then show the countdown and frame it as something to lose, and don't secretly change odds. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 2:42](https://youtu.be/M-GCMK1Tryk?t=162)
- If scheduling an event, then use the Events and Updates API (PATCH to reschedule) and fixed launch times. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/standout-games)
- If planning a sale, then use Schedule Sale up to 30 days ahead. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace)
- If an update is major, then align it with a seasonal moment and promote a sign-up pad beforehand. [indie, 2026-02] -- [janisjanis01 2026-02 12:37](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=757)
- If an event uses sounds as triggers, then add fallback logic. [devforum, 2022-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697)
- If adding match-based events, then hook cleanup to MatchEnd. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/ending-matches)
- If a player-count or revenue claim comes from an indie source, then call it unverified. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 50:29](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=3029)
- If a source cites Live Ops, then treat it as outdated and defer to experience events. [indie, 2019-05] -- [anomalousDucky 2019-05 3:23](https://youtu.be/w_oAK9htLJY?t=203)
- If the event is long or spans many places, then keep it connected with a narrative rather than spreading it thin.
- If planning a collab or celebrity event, forecast a short spike followed by a sharper drop, and schedule follow-up content before the event ends. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-the-brainrot-trend-dying-what-ccu-data-from-three-top-games-reveals)
- If a new event has no history, do not promise sustained collectible value; use exclusives and scheduled return events to bridge the gap. [indie, 2026-08] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-08](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/)
- If a battle pass is monetized, always provide a free grind path that reaches the same rewards. [indie, 2026-00] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026)

- When citing the concert CCU figure (N7542), treat it as official platform ground truth.
- When citing RIA award counts (N7503, N7514), label them as indie-reported, self-reported figures. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 16:26](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=986)
- If asked about The Hunt: Roblox 20, state that it started 2026-09-17 and is past as of 2026-11-01.
