## first-session-onboarding (coverage: covered)

- If a player can't act within ~10-30 s of spawning, remove loading, cutscenes or tutorial steps until they can. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 6:03](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=363)
- If mechanics are non-obvious, build a short interactive tutorial with beams/arrows and a first goal; otherwise rely on environment cues and contextual hints. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 7:42](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=462)
- If no onboarding data exists, instrument funnel steps first (join, first action, first loop completion), then fix the largest drop-off. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 8:22](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=502)
- If you are unsure between tutorial variants, A/B test with Experiments rather than guessing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 15:51](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=951)
- If text is the main instruction, replace it with icons, visuals and visible consequences; cap dialogue at 3-4 taps. [mixed, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-08 32:27](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1947)
- If the game has many features, gate them behind progression and trigger contextual tutorials when relevant. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=868)
- If designing the economy, give starter currency/items and an easy first win; avoid steep early scaling. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 8:21](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=501)
- If UI is built, test on phone, tablet, console and gamepad emulators and avoid hover-only cues. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters)
- If planning ads, first confirm onboarding funnel, bugs and age-rating settings. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-10 13:45](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=825)
- If the lobby is long or new players meet veterans, shorten the lobby and segment new players. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 21:36](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=1296)
- If a player's first session starts with an age check, count that time as pre-play friction, not as part of the game's FTUE window. [official, 2026-01] -- [Roblox 2026-01 5:06](https://youtu.be/m5vqnNSCFCI?t=306)
- Do not claim the age check is skippable, optional, or required for all users. The source gives only the duration.
- When analysing early drop-off, check for the age-check step before attributing abandonment to the game's tutorial or first minute. [official, 2026-01] -- [Roblox 2026-01 5:06](https://youtu.be/m5vqnNSCFCI?t=306)
- If a mechanic can punish the player on a first attempt, telegraph it in a safe instance before the penalty applies. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 7:42](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=462)
- If the target audience skews young, make early progression milestones easier to reach than the default. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 12:19](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=739)
- Do not add a separate tutorial for mechanics already explained by the core-loop-first and contextual-tutorial rules; only telegraph hazards that are non-obvious. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 7:42](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=462)
- If the game has teams, configure Teams (AutoAssignable, unique TeamColor) and spawns (Neutral off, AllowTeamChangeOnTouch on) before wiring team-switching UI. [indie, 2021-01] -- [Emancyphur 2021-01 1:39](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=99)
- If the game has checkpoints, make them one-way after touch, then verify in playtest that players cannot reactivate earlier ones. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 22:36](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1356)
- If the first room has non-obvious controls, prefer level layout and props over pop-ups; add a persistent input prompt only if the layout cannot teach it. [indie, 2025-03] -- [BloxxedDev 2025-03 7:20](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=440)
- If a spawn shows a forcefield or protection effect, remove it and rely on layout or spacing for safety. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 3:35](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=215)
- If the game has abilities or battle moves with costs, show their effects in the menu and battle UI before the player commits. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 7:21](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=441)
- If setting starting currency, start near 10 coins as a benchmark, then adjust so the first purchase is reachable within the first session. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement)
- If sources conflict on pop-ups versus prompts, use a short intro with a persistent prompt, not large modal pop-ups. [indie, 2025-03] -- [BloxxedDev 2025-03 6:12](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=372)
- Mark items from 2021 (Emancyphur) and low-advice items (ByteBlox, BloxxedDev) for verification before applying as firm advice.
- If a progression system is unlocked at an NPC, place an in-world sign there rather than a pop-up (T18557). [indie, 2025-01] -- [janisjanis01 2025-01 4:22](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=262)
- If you ship a next-objective pointer or info button, test it on every spawn point and target platform before launch (T18619). [indie, 2025-12] -- [janisjanis01 2025-12 11:52](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=712)
- Treat janisjanis01 items as single-creator, self-reported evidence. Do not present them as proven conversion lifts. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- If a new item only restates an existing onboarding rule (for example T18599, which repeats 'Clear goals and next steps'), do not add it as a new rule.
- If a reward type exists that a new player might not notice, add particles and a notification on drop for it. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 0:56](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=56)
- When authoring a tutorial, make each step a separate data module so it can be changed without touching the rest of the flow. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 11:12](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=672)
- If a tutorial cannot be followed by a six-year-old persona, reduce steps or wording before adding new ones. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 11:12](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=672)
- Keep the first tutorial to one complete core loop; defer other mechanics to later contextual prompts. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/creating-engaging-experiences)

## game-ideas-and-genres (coverage: covered)

- If a user asks for a game idea, propose a proven loop plus a ~20% twist and a one-sentence pitch.
- If the idea needs more than one sentence, simplify before proceeding. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=0)
- If the user is a beginner, steer to obby, tycoon, simulator, incremental or quiz scope finishable in weeks.
- If the genre looks saturated (garden, simulator, brainrot, tower defense), require a mechanic-level differentiator. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Cumin 2025-08 5:08](https://youtu.be/wdf6cfH3rnA?t=308)
- If the user wants a clone, ask which loop elements change and push toward combining 2-3 inspirations.
- If validating, check Roblox charts for growth, search for competitors, and other platforms for proof. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Tizzy 2026-07 13:39](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=819)
- If the idea is niche, realistic FPS, plain RPG or scary horror, warn about audience size and quality bars. [indie, 2026-06] -- [rasdev 2026-06 3:26](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=206)
- If asked about official guidance, cite RPG/strategy/shooter/puzzle underservice and that genre isn't a ranking factor. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart)
- If the user's passion conflicts with demand, suggest checking demand and talking to target players. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=0)
- Flag all CCU and revenue claims as unverified self-reports.
- If a user proposes an RPG, strategy or shooter game, flag the underrepresentation signal (T17593), then check competition and quality bar before recommending it. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If an idea needs multiple rooms or long progression, first recommend the one-room minimal version (T17693). [official, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2026-09 25:47](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=1547)
- If a user says the game is only for friends, state the expected reach (~4 players) and confirm whether reach is a goal. [official, 2026-04] -- [Roblox 2026-04 21:18](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1278)
- If a user wants a mash-up, require at least one avatar, social or cross-platform layer on top of the known mechanic (T17594). [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- When discussing visuals, suggest one distinctive style choice (Q1779) without recommending licensed IP unless it fits. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- Treat GamesBeat and other indie-sourced signals as self-reported; weight official Roblox signals more heavily. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- Never present an underserved-genre signal as a guarantee of success.
- If a proposed idea's core mechanic already has many near-identical clones on the platform, don't build a copy; add a durable differentiator first. (T17980) [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 14:23](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=863)
- If the design is an extraction or collection loop, add item trade-offs (weight, fragility) before adding more content. (T17826) [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 5:23](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=323)
- If planning long-term retention, design endgame unlocks that change how play works, not just stats. (T17985) [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 42:58](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=2578)
- If making a sequel to a hit, change one clear thing rather than cloning the original. (T17796)
- If a candidate title is a generic single word in a saturated genre, require a more specific, searchable name before building. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 0:50](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=50)
- If an idea is unproven or unusual, run a 48-hour jam-style prototype to test fun before scoping the full game. [indie, 2025-10] -- [Duckable 2025-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=0)
- If a top-grossing game's mechanic is the core of the idea, copy the mechanic and change the theme or add a twist. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 14:20](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=860)
- If a mobile ad game is the inspiration, search the Roblox catalog for existing remakes first and skip it if one is already widely copied. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=0)
- If time is limited to about a week, pick a simple stud-style concept with one reward loop. [indie, 2026-03] -- [VuukStudios 2026-03 1:46](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=106)
- If scripting skill is low, cap the scope at one small system such as a revive system. [indie, 2026-03] -- [VuukStudios 2026-03 12:17](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=737)
- Treat indie success claims (VuukStudios, rasdev, janisjanis01, GameMakers) as self-reported and not as proof of demand; validate demand separately. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If a user wants to ride a trend, first verify it is still growing on current Roblox charts or Discover. Do not rely on 2025 examples as proof of current demand.
- If a user wants to remix a front-page hit, keep the existing ~20% change rule. Do not copy the setting or structure wholesale, which conflicts with 'copy principles, not assets'. Treat T18924 as low-advice. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 14:20](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=860)

## genre-mechanics (coverage: covered)

- If designing horror, avoid large pure-dark areas, build tension gradually, and make objectives visible with neon. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 26:57](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=1617)
- If adding projectiles, use Raycast by default; GetPartsInBox only for large linear projectiles. [indie+devforum, 2024-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332)
- If implementing knockback or moving platforms, use constraints, not BodyMovers. [indie+devforum, 2024-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332)
- If NPCs path or climb, disable unused Humanoid states and keep obstacles under ~3.5 studs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If building a tycoon, include build/upgrade choices beyond collect pads. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Cumin 2025-08 3:30](https://youtu.be/wdf6cfH3rnA?t=210)
- If building PvE, scale difficulty by stage, vary enemy behaviours, and make the first boss teach mechanics. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 8:11](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=491)
- If building tower defense, add target priority modes and audit tower usage. [indie, 2026-06] -- [janisjanis01 2026-06 4:36](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=276)
- If gating features by age/safety level, follow official per-genre guidance. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust)
- If combat game spans platforms, consider mobile-only lobbies or balancing. [indie+devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865)
- If citing indie revenue or visit numbers, label them unverified.
- If a request involves a viral or meme-themed concept, plan retention and depth systems alongside the hook. Cite T17463 and T17471. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/top-10-roblox-games-by-players-march-2026-data-breakdown)
- If a request is for a competitive shooter, default to touchscreen-first calibration of controls, hitboxes and matchmaking. Cite T17477. [indie, 2026-05] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/)
- If a request is for horror, add a survival loop before adding scares, and keep jump scares rare. Cite T17484 and the existing jumpscare rule. [indie, 2026-05] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/)
- Verify the current status of named example games before using them as evidence. [devforum, 2018-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677)
- If a chase or escape sequence has route choices, use visible cues to show wrong paths and never rely on invisible walls alone. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 6:21](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=381)
- If a map has many enemies, cap the count and give each enemy a distinct role before adding more. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 11:14](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=674)
- If a game has a single starting area, plan additional areas in the roadmap rather than treating the first zone as the full experience. [indie, 2026-10] -- [LovJak 2026-10 2:46](https://youtu.be/YXcV0Ba0RJc?t=166)
- If building an obby, sequence challenges by increasing difficulty and consider cooperative mechanics as a distinct layer. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 5:46](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=346)
- Treat all sources here as indie, self-reported advice unless verified against official Roblox documentation. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)
- If a proposed obby uses swinging mechanics, then require a swing physics and momentum playtest before adding it to the progressive obby library. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 4:20](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=260)
- If a genre needs effectively endless levels, then build procedural assembly from a small piece library instead of hand-authoring each level. [indie, 2025-06] -- [JustDucksey 2025-06 3:26](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=206)
- If citing procedural or swing advice, then note it comes from a single self-reported indie source. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)

## influencer-and-creator-marketing (coverage: covered)

- If asked how to get YouTubers to play a game, then recommend a first message with screenshots, a trailer, a direct playable link and a concrete perk. [indie, 2026-02] -- [Cumin 2026-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=41)
- If a creator already features the game, then DM them first, starting with the most-viewed video. [indie, 2021-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-08 13:47](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=827)
- If the budget is a few hundred Robux per video, then warn of slow replies and low views, and suggest fairer rates. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Erthlord 2026-01 10:55](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=655)
- If hiring creators via a marketplace, then recommend SocialSeed checks: verified account, price filter, average views, reviews, past videos, due date. [indie, 2024-07] -- [TheDevBacon 2024-07 1:22](https://youtu.be/_XJVDRr2ZuA?t=82) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If evaluating a campaign, then compare organic installs before, during and after, and look at 365-day value, not only day 30. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:46](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=946)
- If early results are weak, then don't cut the creator yet; check long-tail tracked links. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 14:23](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=863)
- If running a giveaway, then gate rewards behind the tracked link and let the creator choose prizes. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 18:27](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1107)
- If targeting non-English markets, then suggest local agencies with regular strategy meetings. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 31:30](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1890)
- If asked about affiliate links, then cite the official rules: anchor text, FTC disclosure, 4M+ MAU, 65% like score, age 13+, up to 50% share, and note pilot status. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/creator-affiliate)
- If the game is weak or unreleased, then prioritize game quality and pre-launch hype over Discord or TikTok building; treat indie numbers as unverified. [indie, 2026-07] -- [VuukStudios 2026-07 5:49](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=349)
- When planning creator deals, treat reusable cutdowns and short-form clips as a deliverable to negotiate, not a bonus. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:39](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1059)
- Label reuse-rights advice as single-source and self-reported (T17950) when presenting it to the user. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:39](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1059)
- If judging a creator or vertical on early results, wait for at least 10 videos and about 500k combined views per vertical, and 3-4 months for a creator with a weak start. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 39:03](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2343)
- If planning a creator budget, define audience demographics first, then pick verticals and creators. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 40:25](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2425)
- If reporting influencer performance, include CPI, store ranking, DAU and retention alongside direct installs. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 4:50](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=290)
- If a developer asks about cutting creator spend, warn that installs may decline faster once spend stops and suggest a gradual reduction. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 0:05](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=5)
- If advising a creator's own channel, recommend 1-3 focused games and a fixed weekly cadence, judged over 30-60 days. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 1:26](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=86)
- Label all figures from these sources as indie, self-reported. `x2` [indie, 2019-11] -- [TheDevKing 2019-11 8:41](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=521)

## localization-and-international (coverage: covered)

- If starting localization, set the source language first, then add source text, then invite translators. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization/work-with-translators)
- If the game earns money or is large, then recommend human or native translators and treat auto-translation as temporary. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 5:32](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=332) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If strings aren't in the table, then use Studio playtest capture (1-2 min) rather than waiting for ATC. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization/add-source-content)
- If text shows in the wrong language despite a correct table, then check per-language Automatic Translation and verify in-game. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/auto-translation-overrides-manual-translations/4958947)
- If a string has several meanings, then set Context via CSV, not Location. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization/add-source-content)
- If text is dynamic or player-generated, then use real-time translation with player.LocaleId or parameter formats. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization/auto-translate-dynamic-content)
- If UI text is in images, then move it to TextLabels or localize the image via asset IDs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/immersive-narrative)
- If choosing languages, then rank by player country data first. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 15:09](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=909)
- If the user asks about Notable Translators, then say it is deprecated and advise independent vetting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/notable-translators)
- If expanding to a new country, then run a paid UA test before always-on campaigns. [indie+devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/about-our-upcoming-global-compliance-system/461447)
- If a game has grind-gated top items and targets East Asian markets, then propose a regional day-one spend path and track high-value player churn in those regions. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 40:18](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=2418)
- If conversion or ARPU data is available by region, then analyze each region separately before changing global monetization. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 42:19](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=2539)
- If the game is not pay-to-win-leaning, then treat the US vs China conversion gap as a hypothesis to test, not a benchmark. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 42:19](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=2539)

## luau-language-and-types (coverage: covered)

- If writing a new script, then start with --!strict, annotate parameters and returns, and cache services and module requires in top-level locals. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732)
- If the code needs a delay or pause, then use task.wait(n) or task.delay; never use wait() or spawn() in new code. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-game-behavior)
- If waiting for a condition, then use event:Wait() or a connection after checking the flag first; don't loop with wait(). [devforum, 2019-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015)
- If calling an instance method, then use colon syntax; reserve dot calls for functions that do not take self. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/functions)
- If a value may be nil, then guard it with if/and/or, early return or assert, and re-cast to a non-optional local when the checker still warns. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/plant-reference-project)
- If the checker warns about a value you know is correct, then cast it with :: only at one localized place (or a single helper), not across call sites. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/type-checking)
- If a metamethod re-enters the same table, then use rawget/rawset/rawequal inside it; if an arithmetic metamethod is defined, return a fresh table and call setmetatable on each result. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables)
- If a cache holds instances or tables, then use a weak __mode table and don't depend on when entries are collected. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/metatables)
- If a function is in a hot loop and captures per-instance values, then pass values as arguments rather than creating closures each iteration. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Crusherfire 2025-06 14:24](https://youtu.be/oUjrs6Apd7A?t=864)
- If you need to compare numeric strings or validate numbers, then convert with tonumber() and check nil, and use math.floor(x) == x before integer-only APIs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/strings)
- If code uses wait(), spawn() or delay(), replace with task.wait, task.spawn, task.defer or task.delay; use spawn for immediate, defer for next-in-frame. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/scheduler)
- If a loop must not block, run it in its own thread with task.spawn and stop it with coroutine.close or a cancel flag. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 1:24](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=84)
- If a table is mutated while iterated, iterate in reverse for removals and never insert during pairs. [indie, 2024-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2024-08 35:07](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=2107)
- If annotating a class, use intersections and wrap constructors with casts; set the child metatable after defining the child type. [devforum, 2022-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-type-checking-with-oop/1997394)
- If a field may be nil, mark it optional; otherwise initialise it and annotate on the declaration. [indie, 2023-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2023-08 11:53](https://youtu.be/eRVDL7xlN8g?t=713)
- If a callback handles a specific class, type its parameter to that class instead of Instance. [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143)
- If many optional parameters are needed, pass one info table. [indie, 2026-09] -- [ArTheDev 2026-09 9:04](https://youtu.be/fIwzczfIu2U?t=544)
- If native compilation is requested, apply @native only to hot server functions and annotate arguments. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/native-code-gen)
- If async work is independent, run it concurrently (Promise.all) and cancel with onCancel on exit. [devforum, 2023-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661)
- If an edit-time type error is suspected, remember typing does not change runtime behaviour; validate untrusted runtime input separately. [indie+devforum, 2023-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175)
- If a task binds one handler to many UI buttons, use a GetDescendants loop with an IsA check. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 14:56](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=896)
- If a task defines player data or registry shapes, export a type and cast the data template or entries to it. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 1:47](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=107)
- If a task updates state from a read-only value, table.clone it before changing it. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Possible Panda 2026-09 7:13](https://youtu.be/gjUtuovH41w?t=433)
- If a source is marked low-advice or is older than 2024, weight it below newer sources. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/is-the-brainrot-trend-dying-what-ccu-data-from-three-top-games-reveals)
- If a user asks why a Connect handler runs immediately or never runs, check for parentheses after the handler reference first (T17780). [indie, 2026-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 12:52](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=772)
- If a binding is never reassigned, suggest const; if its table contents must also be immutable, suggest table.freeze on top of const (T17843). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 4:53](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=293)
- If the user asks for performance on a whole script, you may suggest --!native; for a single hot function, suggest @native instead. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 0:57](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=57)
- When writing a dictionary loop, do not claim ipairs-like stop behavior for for k, v in tbl; use ipairs only when stopping at nil matters. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 18:05](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=1085)
- Format large literals with underscores when they exceed about six digits for readability. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 16:26](https://youtu.be/5k4MJW277qY?t=986)
- If a conditional value can be false or nil, do not use and/or as a ternary; use an if-expression or an explicit if block. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 11:18](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=678)
- If you need a whole-number result from division, use //. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 16:10](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=970)
- If a loop value is unused, name it _. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 4:01](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=241)
- If module state is internal, prefix it with _ and expose it through functions. Treat this as a naming convention, not enforced privacy. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 2:41](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=161)
- If you annotate function parameters, add a doc comment above the function so hover shows both type and purpose. [indie, 2024-10] -- [Crusherfire 2024-10 0:51](https://youtu.be/1IzNDN6oZP4?t=51)
- If you place an object relative to a surface, use PointToObjectSpace on the surface-normal CFrame and read the components. [indie, 2024-01] -- [B Ricey 2024-01 15:13](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=913)
- If a new source item restates an existing rule (for example Event:Once or Event:Wait for yielding on events), skip it rather than adding a duplicate; only add the frame-timing distinction between RenderStepped and Heartbeat (T18515).
- If a handler is wired to exactly one event, inline it as an anonymous function inside Connect (T19005). [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 7:52](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=472)
- If a handler is shared across several events or reused, define it as a named local and pass the reference to Connect (T19005). [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 7:52](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=472)
- When writing an if or while condition, use == or ~=, never a single = (T19006). [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 8:28](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=508)
- Treat the existing 'pass function references to Connect' rule as still in force; inline functions are an exception only for single-use handlers (T19005). [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 7:52](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=472)

## matchmaking-and-teleport (coverage: covered)

- If a TeleportAsync call is needed, then run it from a server Script; if the code is client-side, then move it to the server via RemoteEvent. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If TeleportAsync throws or fails, then retry inside pcall up to 5 times with a 1 second delay; if TeleportResult is Flooded, then wait about 15 seconds first. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If TeleportResult is any value other than Failure or Flooded, then treat the teleport as invalid and do not retry. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If a reserved server is needed, then generate its ID with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) and reuse that ID to route players to the same server. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If you need to test teleports, then publish the game and test in the Roblox client; do not rely on Studio playtesting. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If you are building custom matchmaking, then start from default signals, define player and server attributes first (max 5 each), and add at most 2 custom signals. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/customize-matchmaking)
- If you change matchmaking weights, then preview with Preview and Test (3 mock servers) and inspect hover scores before applying to live places; use only non-negative weights and tune by ratio. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/customize-matchmaking)
- If a lobby is underpopulated or players wait too long, then consider bots or fill logic, and raise priority for players who have waited longer. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/queues)
- If a place shows no Current Configuration, then it uses default Roblox matchmaking (Friends weight 15 dominates). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/analytics)
- If you need access control or a server browser, then use private servers with invite links or usernames (mobile must be invited directly); for cross-server features, use MessagingService and a 1 minute refresh, not DataStore polling. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/educator-onboarding/4-running-classrooms)
- If a game has multiple servers queuing players, elect one host per mode via a DataStore key before any teleport is confirmed. [devforum, 2023-11] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983)
- If a player asks to join a queue, use a manual request; do not auto-queue players. [devforum, 2019-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142)
- If the goal is to let friends join a player, call GetPlayerPlaceInstanceAsync and pass the job ID as ServerInstanceId; do not also set a reserved or access-code field. [indie, 2023-10] -- [XTB7 2023-10 1:21](https://youtu.be/o0S_8fFCsfA?t=81)
- If the goal is an unjoinable matched group, use ReservedServerAccessCode or ReserveServer plus TeleportToPrivateServer, and check that each player is still in the game first. [indie+devforum, 2024-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983)
- If a teleport is within one place, move the HumanoidRootPart CFrame instead of calling TeleportService. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GnomeCode 2021-06 2:25](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=145)
- If a queue becomes empty, cancel the countdown coroutine and reset UI; if a player leaves, remove them from the queue. [indie, 2024-08] -- [PineappleDev 2024-08 12:27](https://youtu.be/5wudgfIoTwo?t=747)
- If tuning custom matchmaking, change only a few signal weights at a time, and expect a new custom signal to start at weight 0. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/customize-matchmaking)
- If the Voice Chat signal is in use, confirm voice chat is enabled; otherwise its weight is 0. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/attributes-and-signals)
- If an error occurs during teleport-related async calls, handle it with pcall and Enum.TeleportResult rather than only the TeleportInitFailed event. [indie, 2023-10] -- [XTB7 2023-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/o0S_8fFCsfA?t=0)
- Prefer 2025 to 2026 official and DevForum guidance over 2019 to 2021 indie advice when they conflict. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- If a group PvE mode has a player minimum above a drop-in server's capacity, then use a per-mission queue or lobby, not a shared server pool, so one player leaving doesn't abandon the run (T16235). [indie, 2025-07] -- [Cumin 2025-07 13:59](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=839) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a player asks to start a multiplayer room, then allow others to join a visible room with a chosen dungeon/difficulty rather than requiring invites first (T16396). [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 22:08](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1328)
- If players report waiting in mixed-skill lobbies, then add level-bracketed lobbies (e.g. 1+, 30+, 60+) before adding matchmaking complexity (T16646, N6658). [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 4:47](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=287)
- If a region has very few servers, then check ping and empty-lobby rates before launch; low population forces high-ping servers and drives players away (T16179, N6384). [indie, 2025-06] -- [Cumin 2025-06 3:15](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=195)
- If a group needs to form before a teleport, then stage players in a bare queue server and teleport the whole group once the threshold is met (T16647). [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 5:28](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=328)
- If cross-server group search is needed, then treat it as significant work; use MessagingService (existing rule) or a library such as Muster rather than building from scratch (T16648). [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 5:28](https://youtu.be/kjLtxvB_iyk?t=328)
- If a design sets a large group size (e.g. 15-player campaigns), then size the queue, wait-time and fallback behaviour for that threshold; do not reuse 4-player PvE assumptions (N6407). [indie, 2025-04] -- [PineappleDev 2025-04 4:39](https://youtu.be/KTS-9A9DLvY?t=279)
- If a server size cap is set, then remember it limits the teammate pool (e.g. 40 cap leaves 39 possible teammates) and balance it against population per region (N6655).
- If a task involves party size, queue capacity, or reserved-server grouping, check the game's configured server cap first; do not assume a fixed value. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/119958473240233/)
- If code calls Teleport() on the client, migrate it to server-side TeleportService:TeleportAsync before changing Access Control. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If a teleport targets a player-purchased VIP server, set VipServerId on every teleport in that session. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If a teleport targets a developer reserved server, use ReservedServerId and never set a second routing field. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If the player must not fall back to a public server, set VipServerId explicitly rather than relying on plain teleports. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If a teleport fails after the call returned, handle TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed and retry with the original TeleportOptions. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If the result is Flooded, increase the backoff before retrying. [unsourced, ] -- 
- If you need to know a server is reserved on arrival, check PrivateServerId ~= "" and PrivateServerOwnerId == 0, not JobId. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers)
- If the place is a test place, a progression-gated place, or reserved-server-only, prefer Secure within universe only. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If a game has console players and crossplatform is off, do not assume one reserved server ID equals one server; track each platform group separately. [indie, 2022-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 26:33](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=1593)
- If a lobby or party has level or host restrictions, surface the join conditions to players before they try to join, rather than failing silently. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 9:23](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=563)
- Treat the 100-player figure as a self-reported example, not a platform guarantee; check the game's configured cap. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/119958473240233/)
- Do not treat the 2-player start, 50-second round cap, or 5-10 second intermission as matchmaking defaults; use them only as examples of round-based timing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/matchmaking/customize-matchmaking)
- If a request is about queue start thresholds, rely on the existing rule to start countdowns at minimum players and tune the threshold per game. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/core-scripts)

## mindset-and-strategy (coverage: covered)

- If user is a beginner, then advise scoping a small game, shipping, and expecting flops
- If a user asks how to succeed, then recommend volume of simple games plus iterating
- If a user wants passion vs money advice, then present both camps and suggest funding via simple games then passion projects
- If a game has no traction by ~14 days, then suggest diagnosing and possibly moving on, noting late exceptions
- If a game has traction, then recommend continued updates and retention work
- If the user is burnt out, then suggest a break or switching projects [official, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-09 36:43](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2203)
- If user blames algorithm or players, then redirect to diagnosing own metrics
- If quoting income or CCU figures, then label them self-reported and unverified [devforum, 2026-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606)
- If monetization is asked, then put retention first, then spending breadth [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 24:03](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=1443)
- If user relies on commissions or does brand work, then outline an income ladder and define success metrics and long-term updates up front [official, 2023-09] -- [Roblox 2023-09 9:12](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=552) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user asks how to get discovered, prioritize official merit-based guidance (Q710) and update/event cadence (T3570) over luck. [devforum, 2024-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user is chasing one metric (CCU, visits), redirect them to replayability and multi-signal measurement (Q777, T3842). [devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/boost-your-discovery-by-building-games-people-want-to-play/4779042)
- If the user plans a schedule, check for realistic cadence and rest; flag burnout warning signs (T4574, N1681).
- If the user asks about monetization, mention whale concentration (T3343) but note it is dated and should be verified.
- If the user asks about Robux strategy, present the 2022 advice (T3454) as dated and recommend checking current economics. [devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user is building an audience, suggest journey framing and consistent community work (T3834, T3838). [indie, 2026-03] -- [Naavik Gaming Podcast 2026-03 35:01](https://youtu.be/mykMEsgoi5E?t=2101)
- Label self-reported figures as indie-sourced; do not present low-advice items as authoritative. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If the user is discouraged by early failure, reinforce the expectation of flops and continued shipping (Q703, Q763).
- If the user asks about team or solo development, note both are viable if the user balances game and community work (T3838). [indie, 2026-06] -- [DitchyDevelopments 2026-06 3:25](https://youtu.be/cfLbW2j2VLs?t=205)
- Do not treat luck as a substitute for quality; combine preparation with merit-based fundamentals (T3553, Q710). [devforum, 2024-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user plans many parallel projects, recommend focusing on one game first, and note that older advice favoured volume. [indie, 2026-07] -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 12:10](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=730)
- If a game flops, diagnose thumbnail, then core loop, then mechanic before advising abandonment. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 4:05](https://youtu.be/Df5ebFGEOXY?t=245)
- If a user asks about cloning, present the trade-off: clones can earn short-term but originals are the long-term top earners. [indie, 2025-10] -- [bubDEV 2025-10 10:39](https://youtu.be/N-u2gI4DUyw?t=639)
- If a user sets a CCU or subscriber target, benchmark against ~200 active players rather than 10K. [indie, 2025-08] -- [bubDEV 2025-08 6:51](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=411)
- If a user asks about AI tools, encourage doing the hard problems themselves and warn against over-configuring the setup.
- If a user faces hostile comments, advise ignoring hostility and staying focused on building.
- Label indie figures as self-reported and Roblox 2018 figures as dated speaker estimates. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-07 16:26](https://youtu.be/qJx27G0N9kE?t=986)
- Do not present any single creator's viral claim as a general rule. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/plugins)
- If a user asks how long until success, give a range and cite that most releases fail and many attempts are normal; do not promise a timeline.
- If a user's game has no recommendations or algorithm pickup after about 14 days of fixes, help them diagnose discovery versus stickiness before recommending a stop. [indie, 2026-08] -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 17:49](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=1069)
- If a user is planning their first game, scope it small, frame it as training, and steer toward a finishable project.
- If a user asks for CCU targets, offer a staged ladder and note these are aspirational creator claims. [indie, 2026-10] -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 16:44](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=1004)
- If a user claims easy money from AI or quick builds, respond with caution and ask for their own measured data. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Erthlord 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=0)
- If a user asks about hiring or leading, separate leadership skill from coding skill.
- If a user is stuck on code, ask them to frame a specific question and suggest a short break before iterating.
- If a user plans a budget, recommend saving before funding development and multiply their time estimates. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks about income, distinguish pre-recommendation revenue from post-traction revenue and cite that early earnings are small. [indie, 2026-06] -- [VuukStudios 2026-06 18:52](https://youtu.be/catPKgFIZbQ?t=1132)
- If a source is tagged low-advice or is a single self-reported claim, label it as anecdotal and cite the date. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If the user is starting a first project, recommend small template edits or experiments before a large game. [indie, 2020-02] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 1:25](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=85) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user plans full-time work on a side project, flag burnout risk and suggest a weekly hour cap around 10-15 hours. [indie, 2021-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-08 29:14](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=1754)
- If the user is in a long team build, recommend scheduled days off. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 5:32](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=332)
- If the user is planning UGC income, present a ~12-month payback horizon as a self-reported benchmark and not a guarantee.
- If the user reports losing enjoyment or feeling only obligation, suggest a motivation check and consider handoff. [indie, 2023-12] -- [Last Level Studios 2023-12 6:40](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=400)
- If the user asks about finishing vs cutting a failing game, present both views (RoyalDev vs the existing cut-after-14-days rule) and advise diagnosing first.
- Label low-advice items as lower-confidence and cite them alongside higher-confidence sources. [indie, 2024-09] -- [BubuTheDev 2024-09 2:48](https://youtu.be/zc7vWpLOejE?t=168)
- Do not treat indie figures as platform ground truth; no official sources were provided for this batch. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Skip any new item that repeats an existing rule. Only merge new rules, numbers or gaps. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 0:53](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=53)
- For items tagged low-advice or indie self-reported, record them as single-mention and do not promote them to multiple-creators strength without corroboration. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)
- Treat concurrency and top-chart observations as signals to study rather than as guaranteed durability, consistent with existing guidance on peak CCU and retention.
- If the user asks about using AI to build Roblox games, recommend giving the AI a verification method (tests, checks, playtest) before relying on its output. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 27:38](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1658)
- If the user asks about team size or agent-team scale, note that the 30-agents-per-person figure is a hypothetical and not a benchmark.
- If the user asks about Roblox's age or history, state it as a milestone (20 years, 2026) and do not present it as evidence of success.
- Do not treat novelty-focused guidance as new; link it to existing rules on original concepts and building unique games.
- If a user asks whether AI can build their whole game, say no. Explain that they need to direct, test, and understand the code. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 11:32](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=692)
- If a user wants to learn Roblox quickly, recommend building in Studio actively instead of only watching videos. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:06](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=126)
- If a user plans a second game, suggest reusing assets and systems from a niche they already know before choosing a new genre. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 4:03](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=243)
- If a user plans UGC income, suggest at least two product groups (for example avatar items and emotes). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 1:23](https://youtu.be/WIkCweVdd88?t=83)
- Mark failure counts and income claims from these creators as self-reported, not verified. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 4:24](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=264)
- Do not present luck versus merit as settled; note that creators disagree.
- Repeat existing rules only when the user asks; don't add duplicate advice.
- If the user asks about focus or productivity, offer the 15/5 block cycle as one option to try, and label it as an unverified creator suggestion. [indie, 2021-02] -- [Emancyphur 2021-02 5:22](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=322)
- If the user asks about learning motivation, prioritise projects they personally find interesting over external pressure. [indie, 2021-02] -- [Emancyphur 2021-02 3:18](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=198)
- If a claim about AI tools is mentioned, separate virality from game quality when giving advice. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 12:11](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=731)
- Treat claimed play counts and self-reported experience levels as unverified; do not use them as benchmarks without caveats. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 13:21](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=801)
- If a learning resource has many concepts, advise the user to take short notes per resource so concepts stay manageable. [indie, 2021-02] -- [Emancyphur 2021-02 3:18](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=198)
- If an ad creative underperforms, stop tweaking it and shift budget to the winners. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 35:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2158) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a game or content theme loses novelty, switch concept rather than forcing it. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 35:17](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2117)
- Before scaling spend or live ops, verify retention and CPI-efficient conversion. [indie, 2022-08] -- [GameMakers 2022-08 19:42](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=1182) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Choose monetization intensity that your runway can support.
- Judge creator growth only after roughly 30-60 days of consistent output. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 1:26](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=86)
- Stay in one content niche until an audience exists; then branch out. [indie, 2024-11] -- [SmartyRBX 2024-11 18:14](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=1094)
- Don't rely on algorithm tricks alone; prioritize content and game quality.
- Count experiment learnings as a return on the spend that produced them. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 12:14](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=734)
- If a developer asks whether to drop a stalled game, first check data for the bottleneck and apply the 14-day fix window; prefer finishing and iterating on one game (rasdev 2026-05) over restarting, unless the fix window has failed. [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=163)
- If a developer proposes a larger paid push, check organic signals and retention first, and frame the spend as a risky bet with a clear kill criterion (T18796). [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 50:16](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=3016)
- Treat janisjanis01's 114-day and 1,000-users-in-7-days figures as personal goals, not benchmarks, and say so when citing them.
- Treat Deconstructor of Fun poll results as audience sentiment, not market data; do not use them to justify planning decisions.
- When a user asks for a goal, pair it with a plan and milestones instead of stating the goal alone (Q1940).

## monetisation-devproducts (coverage: covered)

- If a product is repeatable (currency, boost, potion, hint), then make it a developer product, not a game pass. [indie, 2026-09] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-09 13:51](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=831)
- If designing a perk like 2x offline earnings, then sell it as a consumable prompt at collection time. [indie, 2026-07] -- [MoseyTube 2026-07 4:36](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=276)
- If a player fails to afford an item, then show the error first and offer the shop/exact amount, opening the shop directly on repeat attempts. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Tizzy 2026-07 18:56](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=1136)
- If adding a starter pack, then show it after the tutorial or first milestone with a 24h timer and low price, and A/B test variables. [indie+devforum, 2023-12] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If writing purchase code, then use a single server ProcessReceipt that returns PurchaseGranted only after granting; never rely on PromptProductPurchaseFinished. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/shop)
- If building the shop, then include 20-30 tiered products, best-value tag, higher tiers above the best seller and several entry points. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Tizzy 2026-06 0:50](https://youtu.be/zoz3PSMqets?t=50)
- If planning sales, then keep them to a few per year, after confirming healthy metrics. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines)
- If the economy has hard currency, then keep it scarce and use event-specific currency for events. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-01 0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0)
- If asked for revenue claims, then label indie figures as self-reported, unverified. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If enabling Managed Pricing or Shop listing, then script prices dynamically, add icons and fix ProcessReceipt issues first. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing)
- If the task involves ProcessReceipt or purchase grants, follow the official rules (T7474, T9844 to T9846) over older indie guidance.
- If a user asks for a specific price, cite the creator's price as an example only and recommend an Experiments A/B test rather than copying it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing)
- If a random or crate product is proposed, require disclosed odds and a guaranteed or high-tier alternative; flag misleading 'secret' framing. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Yann 2026-05 4:48](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=288)
- Treat [low-advice] items (T6230, T6231, T6308, T6626, T6515) as anecdote; do not present their numbers as benchmarks.

- Omit N2956 (no data provided).
- Always create a new product per bundle for tracking (T7808). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles)

- Keep product IDs in one shared module even when they are generated automatically, so ProcessReceipt routing still uses a single ID table. [indie, 2025-09] -- [PineappleDev 2025-09 2:30](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=150)
- If hub or offer revenue stays near zero after a test period, do not add more offer types; first diagnose prompt-to-purchase conversion. [indie, 2026-08] -- [MonarchDev 2026-08 4:06](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=246)
- If asked whether developer products can be gifted on Roblox, state that these sources do not cover gifting and that it must be verified in official docs.
- Cite all figures from this batch as indie self-reported, not as platform benchmarks. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- Do not treat a creator's personal single purchase (e.g. 1,000 Robux goat) as a market benchmark. [indie, 2026-06] -- [LovJak 2026-06 6:17](https://youtu.be/ImcICq5XfL8?t=377)
- When recommending a bulk action, also enforce quantity limits server-side in ProcessReceipt-linked grants. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Mulch 2026-09 9:22](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=562)
- When a limited item is about to be removed, advise planning communication, since sales spike before removal. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/intellectual-property)
- If ProcessReceipt finds the player is nil, return NotProcessedYet and do not grant; grant only on the later callback after rejoin. [indie, 2019-12] -- [TheDevKing 2019-12 11:36](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=696)
- Only return PurchaseGranted after the reward has been applied and saved. [indie, 2019-12] -- [TheDevKing 2019-12 11:36](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=696)
- When planning re-engagement, target churned and existing players, and measure in-app purchase rate against new creator-acquired users before scaling. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 23:58](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1438)
- Treat the 6x re-engagement figure as a self-reported single case; validate with your own test before budgeting around it. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 23:58](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1438)
- Use Studio test purchases (not charged) to validate the grant flow, then confirm in the live game before launch. [indie, 2023-08] -- [SimTek Game Development 2023-08 14:08](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=848)

## monetisation-gamepasses (coverage: covered)

- If designing a pass, then make it a permanent, optional benefit; if repeatable, use a developer product. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization)
- If a pass would block core progress or remove challenge, then redesign or remove it.
- If the game is competitive or for young players, then default to cosmetics/convenience; treat pay-to-win as a risk and flag it. [mixed, 2022-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-11 0:43](https://youtu.be/2kUFhwfuZ-w?t=43)
- If the user has more than about 6 passes, then recommend consolidating or bundling; never exceed 15.
- If a pass's multiplier could break the economy (auto-collect, infinite currency), then warn and suggest limits or a cheap convenience price. [mixed, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-02 10:00](https://youtu.be/RfpMZZFy8Zw?t=600)
- If conversion is low, then improve shop visibility, add a starter pack and contextual upsells, and enable the shopping bag icon.
- If ARPPU is low, then add higher tiers and consumables, and check for overpowered one-time passes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization)
- If a pass doesn't sell, then check per-pass stats and rework its value or make it free. [indie, 2026-09] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-09 7:55](https://youtu.be/ZradA-cygP0?t=475)
- If implementing a pass in code, then check UserOwnsGamePassAsync on PlayerAdded and handle in-session purchases. [indie, 2024-03] -- [Crusherfire 2024-03 4:44](https://youtu.be/kDCLGPMpLl0?t=284)
- If building a season pass, then include free and premium tracks and retroactive premium rewards. Quote indie revenue numbers as unverified and prefer official statements on mechanics. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes)
- If a purchase prompt fails with 'third party sales disabled', enable Allow Third Party Sales in Game Settings > Security. [indie, 2024-02] -- [SimTek Game Development 2024-02 16:16](https://youtu.be/V65oLE0Wbuc?t=976)
- If a pass shows as owned but gives no item, treat it as a bug and fix the grant path before launch. [indie, 2026-09] -- [janisjanis01 2026-09 8:45](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=525)
- If a repeatable item is proposed as a gamepass, suggest a developer product or a limited-time offer instead. [indie, 2026-07] -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 4:41](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=281)
- If the design blocks core progression or the first-time experience behind a Robux prompt, do not implement it. Follow official guidance. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/learn-explore-sort)
- If a boost pass is proposed in a grind-heavy game, it may sell well, but check it does not block core gameplay or create pay-to-win. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 8:54](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=534)
- If a pass is sold, verify ownership at join with pcall and on live purchase before granting benefits. [indie, 2020-03] -- [TheDevKing 2020-03 13:27](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=807)
- If a game is acquired with existing passes, replace them so revenue goes to the new owner. [indie, 2025-09] -- [janisjanis01 2025-09 8:44](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=524)
- If the source revenue figure has unclear units (N2763), do not use it as a number without checking.
- If a player asks to build a gamepass, first check whether it speeds up a grind or gives cosmetics; avoid making a paying player dominant. [indie, 2026-10] -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 6:40](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=400)
- If a pass removes a tedious task, make sure the core loop is still playable without paying; do not make it a blocker. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 6:06](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=366)
- If the user asks about a stat or booster pass, note the conflict with the existing rule against one-off stat boosters and recommend a repeatable product or a speed-up instead. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 4:40](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=280)
- If planning revenue, compute buyers x price x players and apply the 30% cut before reporting numbers. [indie+devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493)
- If the user runs or plans ads, check that at least a few passes are live first. [indie, 2026-05] -- [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=0)
- If the user asks about pass setup, confirm 'Allow third party sales' is on and that passes are tested in the published client. [indie, 2024-02] -- [SimTek Game Development 2024-02 16:16](https://youtu.be/V65oLE0Wbuc?t=976)
- If the user designs a prize wheel, require showing odds before the spin. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 6:34](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=394)
- Report 2024-2026 pricing, not 2019 pricing, unless the user asks for history; flag speculative revenue figures (Blox Fruits, Brookhaven) as unverified.
- If a pass is cosmetic or convenience, price it in the 59–99 Robux band and test before going lower. [indie, 2024-12] -- [BubuTheDev 2024-12 1:16](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=76)
- If content was previously free, do not move it behind a gamepass. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Cumin 2025-05 16:13](https://youtu.be/2auVezIcNAE?t=973)
- If pass sales are low, check in-game visibility and prompts before cutting the price. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Mulch 2025-06 2:45](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=165)
- If a game earns from passes, measure pass vs dev product revenue separately before choosing which to emphasise. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Rapstrike GFX 2026-10 4:05](https://youtu.be/6EoiHRT4dAA?t=245)
- If the pass is a powerful admin or sandbox tool, price high and allow multiple admins. [indie, 2024-09] -- [CORSO 2024-09 9:12](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=552)
- Treat [low-advice] items as anecdotes, not benchmarks. [indie, 2024-11] -- [SmartyRBX 2024-11 6:04](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=364)
- Do not cite ThatGuyEvan's VIP and total revenue estimates; they are unreliable speaker figures and were excluded.
- Prefer the most recent (2026) source when older guidance conflicts, and say so. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- If the game is a competitive shooter, then monetise cosmetic skins and avoid any power or advantage item. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)
- If the monetisation plan relies on selling passes across experiences, then flag it as unavailable since 2026-07 and redesign for in-experience passes. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- If pricing a power or admin pass in the thousands of Robux, then warn about pay-to-win backlash and offer an earnable equivalent. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts)
- If a permanent luck or stat boost is proposed for a grind game, then check the existing caution on capped stat boosters before listing it as a core pass. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 4:40](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=280)
- If setting multiplier pass prices, then use a steep ladder with an x2 entry near 99 to 149 Robux and cap the top tier against the economy. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/114697347887839/)
- If setting cosmetic pass prices, then use a ladder from about 19 to 199 Robux with bundles up to about 700 Robux, rather than only 59 to 99. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/114697347887839/)
- If citing any price, then date-stamp it (2026-11 snapshot) and note that listings change by version.
- If citing RoWatcher or RoTrends numbers, then mark them as self-reported and lower weight than Roblox API listings.
- If a pass count is being planned, then benchmark against 5 to 13 active passes seen in most snapshots and treat 46 as an outlier.
- If the listing shows a high-priced pass, then do not treat presence on sale as evidence it sells well; test conversion first.
- If a source proposes gating a class or character's starting item behind Robux, flag it as conflicting with the core-progress and cosmetic-only rules, and recommend only a cosmetic variant. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- If a pass free trial is available in the dashboard, note it as a one-session trial before buying, and do not invent a different trial length.
- If a pass or product fixes a player loss (revive, retry, lives), only offer it when a free alternative still lets the player progress. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 6:57](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=417)
- If a game has collection or capacity mechanics that grow over time, sell expansions as repeatable dev products rather than one-off passes. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Mulch 2026-09 6:32](https://youtu.be/U2IjSOyNTB8?t=392)
- If a booster affects leaderboard values in a competitive game, flag it as a fairness risk and check the intended behaviour before launch.
- Plan where monetisation appears during development, but do not gate core progression behind it at launch. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Duckable 2026-10 20:12](https://youtu.be/EOUSVENUkr0?t=1212)
- Keep all numbers from Mulch and MonarchDev labelled as indie, self-reported when reporting them. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)

- If adding a VIP cosmetic tag, show it only when ownership is confirmed by UserOwnsGamePassAsync for that pass ID. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 3:36](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=216)

- Treat 2022-2023 snippet-based guidance as older. Check current API and dashboard paths before shipping. [indie, 2024-02] -- [PolarBear 2024-02 0:41](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=41)

## monetisation-other (coverage: covered)

- If designing a new game, then define monetisation hooks (shop, passes, subscription, Premium perk) in the design phase, but build the fun loop first.
- If a paid item gives a gameplay edge, then limit it to progression speed or consumables, never core power or content access. [indie, 2025-08] -- [PineappleDev 2025-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/tsmsTtrlT8Y?t=0)
- If adding random paid items, then show numerical odds (100% total), disclose indirect purchases, and gate with PolicyService.GetPolicyInfoForPlayerAsync. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-random-items)
- If running an event, then use a separate event currency and limited-time (not stock-capped) items; keep earned non-consumables permanently. [mixed, 2026-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-01 19:12](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=1152)
- If adding rewarded video, then place it at natural breaks, state the exact fixed reward, and never prompt mid-fight; confirm ~100k DAU eligibility. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/shooter)
- If you want Premium income, then add a Premium perk and PromptPremiumPurchase; don't gate the game or promise Robux. [indie+devforum, 2020-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-premium-payouts/455347) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If adding a subscription, then follow the platform rules (same benefits across platforms, full term, no extra tasks, distinct non-tiered benefits); create separate Robux and local-currency products. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 25:59](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1559) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If enabling paid access, then give the full game, weigh player-count loss, and don't combine with private servers. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-local-currency)
- If revenue drops, then break it down by source, platform and country and compare payer conversion and ARPPU to benchmarks. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition)
- If self-reported indie numbers conflict with official mechanics, then trust the official docs and label the indie figure as unverified. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If the user asks about selling UGC in the Marketplace, first confirm ID verification and Roblox Plus or Premium 1000/2200 eligibility. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- If the user plans a bundle, set priceInRobux on each included item so the discount is visible. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles)
- If the user wants an urgency offer for new players, use singleUse with a 15-minute window and never gate gameplay with it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles)
- If the user asks about a moment-of-need offer, recommend OneTime bundles triggered at the moment of need, not as pop-ups. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles)
- If the user asks about random or paid results, generate and grant them on the server only. [devforum, 2019-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895)
- If the user asks about creator payouts, point to Creator Rewards and note the Creator Affiliate program is deprecated. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 7:27](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=447)
- If the user quotes Robux revenue, label it self-reported and convert using the current DevEx rate, not the speaker's implied rate.
- If the user is building a school-facing game, check school-hours restrictions before enabling purchases.
- If the user licenses IP, enforce one license per item and a maximum of 100 items per license, and track the 14-day offer windows. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/creators)
- If the user relies on low-advice figures, do not present them as benchmarks.
- If the game's average session is under about 15 minutes, prioritise retention before recommending paid traffic or Premium-focused monetisation. [indie, 2024-08] -- [VuukStudios 2024-08 3:20](https://youtu.be/3J7Vp6NgYf4?t=200) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a game is DevEx-eligible, forecast at $0.54 per 100 Earned Robux for age-checked 18+ US spend; treat $0.38 figures as outdated. [indie+devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091)
- For tipping or donations, use the Transfers API (PromptRobuxTransferAsync, BindReceiptHandler), not passes or DevProducts; verify the post-May 29 status. [devforum, 2026-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/disabling-cross-game-sales-of-passes-and-dev-products-and-introducing-the-transfers-api/4618396)
- Do not recommend paid ads or traffic until organic conversion is proven. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Matt The Method 2026-08 3:17](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=197)

- Never recommend spend-based odds, silent duplicate swaps or pressure purchases; keep offers optional. [indie, 2025-08] -- [PineappleDev 2025-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/tsmsTtrlT8Y?t=0) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
