## optimization-and-streaming (coverage: covered)

- If a game is slow on phones, then check Performance Summary and MicroProfiler on a low-end client first, not Studio; if frame bars are consistently tall, fix a per-frame cost, and if they spike occasionally, look for bursty work (loops, large remotes, instance floods). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/use-microprofiler)
- If memory is climbing, then use Developer Console memory tab and Luau heap snapshot diffs, then destroy unused sounds, animations and clones. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/use-microprofiler)
- If the game has many instances on join, then enable StreamingEnabled and set Persistent or Atomic for scripted or always-needed models. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques)
- If a model breaks under streaming, then flatten nested Persistent/Atomic hierarchies and avoid client-side reparenting or Clone into Workspace. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques)
- If a part does not move or need collision, then anchor it, set CanCollide/CanTouch/CanQuery off, and use Box CollisionFidelity. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345)
- If a part is small or cosmetic, then use Box collision, Automatic RenderFidelity, and disable CastShadow. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If many transparent layers stack, then merge them into one union or reduce layers; do not place decals on fully transparent objects. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience)
- If you see wait() in a loop, then replace with task.wait and spread heavy work across frames or use parallel Luau for DataModel-free compute. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If remote traffic is high, then batch into fewer large packets, use small data types, and keep incoming traffic under ~50 KB/s. [devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-best-practices-how-to-keep-your-games-ping-low/1913475)
- If server memory approaches 50%, then plan capacity with 6.25 GiB + 100 MiB × peak players and keep MaxPlayers lower if needed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/identify)
- If a project needs memory or join-time gains, enable Instance Streaming and keep StreamingMinRadius 64 and StreamingTargetRadius 1024 before tuning. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345)
- If a frame goal is missed, first classify the bar colour (orange, blue, red) and only then pick a script, render or physics fix. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler)
- If a fix improves average FPS but not spikes, keep profiling; spikes are what players feel. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler)
- If advice comes from a 2018–2021 DevForum post and conflicts with 2025–2026 official docs, follow the official docs and say the older advice is superseded. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- If a suggested streaming radius is smaller than the official default, verify memory need on a low-end device before adopting it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/frustum)
- If many server-owned parts are tweened, replace with client-side animation playback or client-only visuals. [indie, 2024-10] -- [Suphi Kaner 2024-10 20:29](https://youtu.be/ZPviBfpPloE?t=1229)
- If Open Cloud or HTTP request volume approaches limits, batch and back off; do not just retry. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service)
- If a claim is self-reported with no measurement or is marked low-advice, label it unverified in recommendations. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Do not recommend Atomic or Persistent on every model; use Atomic for parts that must load together and Persistent sparingly. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming)
- Treat Studio FPS and Studio memory as non-representative; ask for physical low-end device measurements before concluding. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 34:09](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2049)
- If a project targets mobile, check memory against the 400-500 MB full-game guideline before tuning rendering. [indie, 2021-01] -- [TechSpectrum 2021-01 1:08](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=68)
- If a mobile crash appears, check memory usage first. [indie, 2022-01] -- [Last Level Studios 2022-01 30:42](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1842)
- If the user wants custom streaming, first suggest native Instance Streaming; only add a frame-time-throttled reparent queue if native streaming cannot meet the need. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-08 5:51](https://youtu.be/0RRGneqSZRY?t=351)
- If a vehicle or enemy has per-wheel raycasts running every frame, replace them with one center raycast plus orientation constraint. [indie, 2025-09] -- [Roox4 2025-09 7:31](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=451)
- If large maps show fractional transparency, set 0 or 1 and confirm the effect in MicroProfiler. [indie, 2025-06] -- [BubuTheDev 2025-06 5:47](https://youtu.be/0UYtSJvYe4U?t=347)
- If a light is shadowed, prefer SpotLight over PointLight where the look allows. [indie, 2021-01] -- [TechSpectrum 2021-01 11:27](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=687)
- If SLIM is considered, verify which geometry is SLIM with a Highlight in Workspace and test known defects first. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Last Level Studios 2025-11 11:16](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=676)
- Treat indie figures as self-reported; prefer measured data from MicroProfiler and Developer Stats. [indie, 2026-00] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-00](https://rowatcher.com/news/fish-it-reels-in-270k-players-inside-roblox-s-most-addictive-casual-game-of-2026)
- Give newer advice (2025-2026) precedence over 2020-2021 advice where they conflict, and say so. [indie, 2024-11] -- [Possible Panda 2024-11 0:41](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=41)
- Treat [low-advice] items as lower-confidence; do not present them as authoritative. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Possible Panda 2026-01 0:37](https://youtu.be/AOAh-4Q9ARw?t=37)
- If the client instance count is approaching about 150,000 with streaming on, reduce instances (deparent, stream, pool) before adding more features. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 21:08](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=1268)
- If a module or model count climbs steadily during play, run the Script Memory reset and check unparented instances before tuning rendering. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 8:35](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=515)
- If a boat-scale or prop mesh is well above about 5,000 polygons, move the extra detail into a normal map; never exceed the 20,000-polygon import limit. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 28:14](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=1694)
- If client and server instance counts match, treat zone culling as broken and investigate before optimising anything else. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 16:52](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=1012)
- Do not tune gameplay around late-2026 features (adaptive radius, path pre-fetching, 500-stud draw distance floor) until they are confirmed live. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208)
- When targeting low-end Android, test both current and possibly raised draw distance behaviour, since the minimum may rise to 500 studs. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284)
- Treat indie figures (GamesBeat T17599, N7476) as self-reported, not platform ground truth. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- Use the 1-second-per-10,000-instances figure as a rough estimate; always confirm with a measured join on a real low-end device. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 21:08](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=1268)
- If a game's performance degrades as players progress (tycoon, idle, upgrade-based games), test the maximum-progression state, not only the start state, on a low-end device. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 10:49](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=649)
- Treat large FPS drops tied to progression as a content-scaling problem: profile the MicroProfiler and count visible parts and draw calls per upgrade tier before changing rendering settings.
- If a task involves spawning or cloning many items at once, batch or stagger the spawns across frames instead of doing them in a single frame. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 9:49](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=589)
- If a task involves load-in or loading-screen flow, test with a full-inventory account and consider a skip fallback. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 9:25](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=565)
- Treat the 100-clone FPS figure as a self-reported indicator. Confirm with a profiler on a low-end device before quoting it. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 34:09](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2049)
- If a game is a small, linear, discrete-section space, you may suggest instant section swaps as an alternative to gradual streaming, but label it as an unverified indie suggestion. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 34:09](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=2049)
- If a section swap is proposed, require a frame-time measurement on low-end mobile before recommending it. [indie, 2022-01] -- [Last Level Studios 2022-01 30:01](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1801) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Do not replace the existing streaming rules (PauseOutsideLoadedArea, ModelStreamingBehavior, streaming radii) with this tip for open-world games.

## physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai (coverage: covered)

- If code uses any Body* mover, then migrate to AlignPosition, AlignOrientation, LinearVelocity, VectorForce, LineForce or AngularVelocity per the official mapping (T7837-T7842). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/mover-constraints)
- If a part must hold a constant speed, then use LinearVelocity or AngularVelocity with a MaxForce/MaxTorque high enough to reach the target (T9684, N3837). If only a one-time speed is needed, set AssemblyLinearVelocity or AssemblyAngularVelocity (T9738, T7899). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/create-moving-objects)
- If a hinge must turn by itself, then set ActuatorType to Motor (or Servo for a target angle), anchor the stationary base, and for player-loaded vehicles set MotorMaxTorque to inf with one motor per rotating assembly (T8177, T7785, T8179, T8180). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/create-spinning-objects)
- If a constraint rotates about the wrong axis, then align both attachments' Axis, enable Show Constraint Details and recheck (T8519, T7787). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/constraints/universal)
- If a door will not move, then check the frame is anchored and the door is not, and set limits to -90/90 using the orange axis for limits (T10015-T10018). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/build-a-hinged-door)
- If an NPC never moves, then delete its Humanoid and drive it with constraints or CFrame; keep AI as simple as possible (T5843, T5175). [indie+devforum, 2026-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333)
- If an NPC must follow a path, then use Humanoid:Move per waypoint, handle Path.Blocked only for waypoints ahead, wait until grounded before ComputeAsync, and use finite material costs (100 or 1000) rather than math.huge (T8948-T8950, T8951, N3612). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding)
- If a player-facing outcome or distance/LOS check is involved, then run it on the server; keep only presentation math such as wave formulas on the client (T8582, T5437). [indie, 2025-07] -- [Crit 2025-07 5:49](https://youtu.be/hqWLpWsWF00?t=349)
- If generated AI logic is used, then playtest and hand-tune it before shipping (T6630, Q1055). [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 6:06](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=366)
- If a mechanism is unstable (tank-like), then consider a fixed timestep; otherwise keep adaptive stepping (T8049, T8266). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve)
- If building suspension, default to raycast VectorForce springs (T12940) for control; use Cylindrical plus Spring only for constraint-based builds. [devforum, 2025-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628)
- If PathfindingService returns a status other than Success, handle it explicitly and do not leave the NPC idle. [indie+devforum, 2025-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450)
- If a waypoint Action is Jump, set humanoid.Jump = true before MoveTo. [indie+devforum, 2025-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450)
- If an NPC should chase, first check range and line of sight; patrol alone does not detect players. [indie+devforum, 2022-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360)
- If NPC count is high, throttle think Rate and distance-based path updates, then test at target player count. [devforum, 2022-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360)
- If a door or platform must carry players, use a physics hinge or physics platform, not a tween. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 9:05](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=545)
- If a platform must hold weight against gravity, set ServoMaxForce high enough (example 10000) and Restitution 0 on limits. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/create-elevators)
- If a slow assembly unexpectedly sleeps, use an actuated joint or an impulse to wake it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/sleep-system)
- Do not use client-reported positions or physics for gameplay decisions. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/security-tactics)
- Before shipping a 2026 beta feature (ImprovedAnimationConstraint, Server Authority), verify its current status and phase.
- If an NPC walks between waypoints with Humanoid:Move, keep using the existing Humanoid rules. If it is physics-driven (enemy, drone, rolling body), use simple parts with custom movement instead. [indie, 2025-09] -- [Roox4 2025-09 0:50](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=50)
- If an NPC must chase or navigate barriers and mazes, use a pathfinding module first. Fall back to raw PathfindingService only when custom control is needed. [indie, 2025-07] -- [Crit 2025-07 0:37](https://youtu.be/hqWLpWsWF00?t=37)
- If you create a PathfindingLink handler, assign it without parentheses and return true at the end, or pathing stops. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Crit 2025-08 2:11](https://youtu.be/Om2vAKMW1Pw?t=131)
- If you tune NPC chase, change config values after restoreDefaults, then set shortest-distance, target offset and direct-move timer explicitly. [indie, 2025-07] -- [Crit 2025-07 7:41](https://youtu.be/hqWLpWsWF00?t=461)
- If you write custom suspension or spring math, add damping. Prefer native Springs on constraints when they can do the job. [indie, 2025-09] -- [Roox4 2025-09 4:06](https://youtu.be/WNqGLvkf7hQ?t=246)
- If you aim a part at a target, use IK Look At on the server rather than a client script. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Roox4 2025-06 6:56](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=416)
- Treat [low-advice] IK and Roblox-version claims as lower confidence. Verify them against current official docs before shipping. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 3:42](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=222)
- Do not present indie-reported figures (teleport bound, offsets, timers, wait times) as official Roblox limits.
- If a turret or NPC must turn toward a target, use a rate-limited turn (rotateToward with a max angle per frame) rather than instant orientation, starting near 45 degrees per second and tuning from there. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 9:48](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=588)
- If a task needs to know when a part or character is in water, first check current Roblox Terrain APIs for water or material queries. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 9:28](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=568)
- If no built-in water query fits, use an invisible, anchored, non-collidable sphere over the water and detect entry through its touches. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 9:28](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=568)
- Treat the sphere-sensor advice as a single indie, self-reported tip, not official guidance. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 9:28](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=568)
- When asked to add many NPCs, prefer one shared NPC module driven by per-type config over duplicating scripts per character. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 6:23](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=383)
- Treat the reusable-NPC-system advice as single-source and self-reported; confirm it against official docs or DevForum posts before relying on it for large projects. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 3:42](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=222)
- If a source suggests BodyForce or BodyVelocity, substitute the modern equivalent (VectorForce, LinearVelocity or an impulse) and retune the magnitude; do not copy legacy numbers. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 13:53](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=833)
- For instant knockback, use a one-off impulse and clean up any temporary force object after about 2 seconds. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 13:53](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=833)
- For enemy reactions, gate the lock-on on a distance check and return the enemy to its post afterwards. [indie, 2020-06] -- [GnomeCode 2020-06 24:16](https://youtu.be/Z74HM6xcsBE?t=1456)
- Verify any Humanoid.Sit-based stop on the current rig before shipping it. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 13:53](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=833)

## player-psychology-and-game-feel (coverage: covered)

- If the user asks for combat or attack feel, then combine anticipation, a short contact hold, heavy recovery, a camera nudge, recoil and layered audio rather than a single effect. [indie, 2018-05] -- [Game Maker's Toolkit 2018-05 13:53](https://youtu.be/8X4fx-YncqA?t=833)
- If a player action has no visible or audible response, then add immediate feedback (sound, particle, state change) before adding anything else. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques)
- If the user asks for randomised rewards or gacha, then show odds, guarantee that good results still happen, and flag the ethical and policy check before recommending compulsion-style tactics. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 1:21](https://youtu.be/M-GCMK1Tryk?t=81)
- If the user asks to make the game more frustrating for retention, then recommend challenge without punishment by default and only suggest frustration-driven retry loops for opt-in competitive modes. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/edu-templates/edu-lesson-plan-template)
- If the user asks about anti-cheat or kicking players, then prefer unobtrusive checks and feature gating over hard rejection; write a custom message if rejection is needed. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust)
- If the question is about platform mechanics (damage, ForceField, physics, chat, rejection, speech), then answer from Roblox Creator Docs (2026-10) and override older community advice.
- If the user asks for numeric values (frame counts, transparency, blend time, firing settings), then quote them as examples from the named source, not as standards, and note their age.
- If the user cites revenue, CCU or engagement figures from these creators, then label them as unverified indie self-reports.
- If the user is starting a new game, then define target audience and motivation first, then design for multiple player types, then run the Xbox test on gameplay footage. [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 6:32](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=392)
- If the icon or title promises a feature, then verify gameplay delivers it or exceeds it before publishing. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 7:27](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=447)
- If building camera feel from scratch, start with smoothing about 16 and FOV 60, then tune one value at a time. [indie, 2025-01] -- [PineappleDev 2025-01 4:07](https://youtu.be/hHKQEohHqwU?t=247)
- If a player-visible value changes, tween HUD visuals about 0.5 s and keep the one-shot audio immediate. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters)
- If a random reward has a guarantee, show the pity counter and keep the published odds fixed. [indie, 2025-02] -- [ByteBlox 2025-02 13:37](https://youtu.be/o-8V99horro?t=817)
- If a player leaves or disconnects, do not apply a penalty beyond losing the current fight. [devforum, 2020-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912)
- If designing horror, keep scenes readable, limit jumpscares, and place safe rooms after high-tension beats.
- If a combat timing window is added, use about 0.1 s and pair it with a stun on success. [indie, 2025-07] -- [ArTheDev 2025-07 29:32](https://youtu.be/Pi8rQ9gGTKE?t=1772)
- If choosing easing, prefer Exponential or Circular for fast-start, slow-stop motion; avoid Linear for feel. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters)
- Prefer OFFICIAL Creator Docs (2026-10) over indie figures for APIs and platform behaviour; treat indie numbers as starting points. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If a numeric value is cited from one creator, verify it in your own play-test before shipping. [indie, 2025-08] -- [bubDEV 2025-08 4:53](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=293)
- Do not change drop odds based on past outcomes; that pattern is labelled manipulative. [indie, 2025-02] -- [ByteBlox 2025-02 25:43](https://youtu.be/o-8V99horro?t=1543)
- If designing a boss or heavy enemy attack, then add a visible windup and a vulnerability window before finalising it. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 5:16](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=316)
- If a game has a chasing enemy, then add escape options before tuning its speed. [indie, 2020-09] -- [Emancyphur 2020-09 5:42](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=342)
- If a reward is earned, then show an on-screen gain animation and avoid stacking several reveals at once. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-09 2:56](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=176)
- If core activity requires long waits early on, then redesign the early loop rather than relying on later payoff. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Cumin 2025-06 12:34](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=754)
- If designing higher difficulty, then change behaviour or layout rather than only raising damage. [indie, 2026-02] -- [Cumin 2026-02 6:44](https://youtu.be/ROo_Xpms4ok?t=404)
- If setting death penalties, then keep carried-currency loss small and avoid long walk-backs. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 10:36](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=636)
- If tuning respawn timers, then keep them at a few seconds for shooters and no more than about a minute for MOBAs. [indie, 2025-07] -- [Cumin 2025-07 4:57](https://youtu.be/jXogdTgtlXE?t=297)
- If a rule conflicts with the existing random-rarity rule, then weigh player preference for earned rewards and document the trade-off.
- If a source is tagged low-advice, then treat it as a hypothesis to test rather than a settled rule. [indie, 2021-11] -- [GameMakers 2021-11 4:49](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=289)
- If a design includes idle or offline income, do not add a penalty for absence (T17470, T17476). [indie, 2026-05] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/)
- If a design uses scarce collectibles, prefer timed map-wide spawns over a permanent egg or shop (T17496). [indie, 2026-06] -- [GosuGamers 2026-06](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78614-grow-a-garden-2-has-launched-on-roblox-here-s-everything-new-added-in-this-sequel)
- If a design uses pets or mounts as speed multipliers, treat that as official guidance (T17563) and check the tier-split rule. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- If a source is indie and self-reported (T17470, T17476, T17496), do not present its claims as measured benchmarks. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- If absence punishment and urgency conflict, use scarcity windows for urgency, not loss framing for non-return.
- If a player action produces a hit or impact, verify the reaction shows immediately before adding further juice.
- If the task involves AI-generated design, balance or feel values, label them as a draft and recommend a playtest-and-replay pass before shipping. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 6:09](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=369)
- Do not claim a design is fun based only on AI evaluation; recommend human playtesting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 26:18](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1578)
- If a design includes an instant-death timer, add a visible warning of at least about 2 seconds before the lethal outcome. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 12:37](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=757)
- If a design includes a stalker or chaser enemy, add an audio or visual cue and keep the enemy physically visible or trackable. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 13:37](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=817)
- If a level sequence has several threats in a row, insert a calm gap or safe space before the next threat. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 12:37](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=757)
- If enemies are AI-generated, do not ship them without a balance pass on health, damage and speed. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 6:09](https://youtu.be/QwdZRB6xRKk?t=369)
- If the camera is locked to a rail or track, cap the turn range near 100 degrees so the player can keep aim readable. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 7:42](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=462)
- Treat self-reported figures (2 seconds, 100 degrees) as starting points and verify them in playtests. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 6:06](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=366)
- If a player-facing difficulty change is proposed, check it against real death and session data before accepting it as balanced. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 5:44](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=344)
- If a GUI has buttons created at runtime, connect hover and click sounds through DescendantAdded, not a one-time loop. [indie, 2025-03] -- [PineappleDev 2025-03 1:21](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=81)
- If a threat is meant to escalate, add it in steps and avoid introducing several new threats at once. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 5:44](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=344)
- If the game is advertised for multiple players, verify solo matchmaking works or state clearly that it is unsupported. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 16:53](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=1013)
- Use ambient sound and light cues to guide players before resorting to on-screen markers. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 10:45](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=645)
- If a proposed paid item gives any gameplay advantage, flag it as pay-to-win risk and recommend a cosmetic or time-gated alternative. [indie, 2022-11] -- [GameMakers 2022-11 25:03](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=1503) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If monetization changes are proposed, favour transparent, published rules over hidden mechanics, since players who feel manipulated drop off. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 20:36](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=1236)
- If premium currency is being designed, gate it behind time or progress rather than making it instantly purchasable. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 29:16](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=1756) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a difficulty spike is proposed for revenue, schedule it as a rotation and check retention impact rather than making it permanent. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 4:32](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=272)
- If external events are stressing players, consider adding calm content before pushing high-intensity challenge.
- When designing cosmetic or status items, cover achievers, self-expressers, speculators and competitors explicitly. [indie, 2024-08] -- [GameMakers 2024-08 37:49](https://youtu.be/gp_hDijjC4U?t=2269)
- Treat indie-sourced monetization figures and anecdotes as self-reported and unverified; corroborate with official platform data before committing to them. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- If a player action completes an unlock or purchase, add both a sound and a visual payoff (particles, confetti, shake or animation). [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 15:09](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=909)
- If a return or login reward is designed, frame the message around what the player stands to lose by not returning. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=121)
- Treat loss-aversion hooks as optional polish that depends on the audience; do not add them where they would punish absence in a way that feels manipulative. [indie, 2026-04] -- [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/yFKY2FlwHjE?t=121)

## playtesting-and-feedback (coverage: covered)

- If the build is a rough prototype, then playtest it with friends or a Discord server now before polishing. [mixed, 2025-04] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19)
- If testers report bugs, then fix them first before triaging feature feedback. [mixed, 2026-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-04 1:16](https://youtu.be/NAwQr9sTy74?t=76)
- If a feedback item appears once, then log it; if multiple players repeat it, then act on it. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-09 28:28](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=1708)
- If feedback comes from friends or fans, then discount it and seek real critics or target-audience players. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 18:15](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=1095)
- If players leave within about 5 minutes, then survey them and review onboarding and the core loop. [mixed, 2026-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-07 35:50](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=2150)
- If shipping an update, then test it first and avoid last-second changes. [mixed, 2026-08] -- [Roblox 2024-09 22:31](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1351)
- If a change is risky, then release to engaged players or A/B test it with a 50/50 split for about a week. [official, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2026-09 10:39](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=639)
- If the game is social or multiplayer, then test both solo and group sessions. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2026-09 25:41](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=1541)
- If you lack devices or testers, then use the Studio emulator and recruit via Discord, QA Central or paid testers. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 52:21](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3141)
- If using AI-generated changes, then playtest each yourself. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2018-07 26:03](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1563)
- If the game needs a comprehension or fun check, run a small group playtest with friends, family or teammates before sharing more widely. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies)
- If you must check controls and UI for all players, recruit disabled and varied testers. [official, 2023-05] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-05 1:00](https://youtu.be/K1pEI6LVJJA?t=60)
- If you are choosing a public test format, use a time-boxed weekend or week test to get retention data; open early only if you can triage a bug flood. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies)
- If you are tuning hints, set the delay from completion data and show each hint once only after most players finish the step. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/onboarding-techniques)
- If you run a large rollout test, use a 50/50 split against control unless the change requires multiple variants. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments)
- If player segments may differ, use conditional configs in experiments to compare outcomes per segment. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs)
- If you receive an AI feedback summary, verify it against raw comments, especially downvote comments, before prioritising. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/insights)
- If KPIs are not met after a change, repeat the cycle of analytics, cause-finding through community, change, and retest. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies)
- If telemetry shows a behaviour but not its cause, run a small interview or observation sample before redesigning. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Yann 2026-05 4:46](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=286)
- If a game is about to receive paid traffic or public launch, first confirm it loads and is playable with real testers. [indie, 2026-05] -- [janisjanis01 2026-05 7:18](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=438)
- If a question concerns long-term retention or progression, plan a multi-week diary study or a longitudinal cohort instead of one-off sessions.
- If testing multiplayer, include long-lived servers, rejoins and degraded network profiles before shipping. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project)
- If running a classroom or lesson playtest, apply a 1-2 minute time box and a single goal per check. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-4)
- If changing progression or tuning, test both too fast and too slow, including late waves. [indie, 2026-09] -- [janisjanis01 2026-09 6:27](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=387)
- If a fix or config change is pushed, playtest again immediately; config errors can block progress.
- If feedback comes from peers, request observations plus one recommendation rather than open-ended opinions. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding)
- If the user asks which beta type to use, recommend closed access via a Roblox group first, then open a Beta-tagged beta once the game is playable end to end on all target platforms. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences)
- If the user asks about early testers, confirm Early testing availability and the 10 age-checked friends cap before recommending it; otherwise use Invite early testers. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the user asks about balance values, say they are educated guesses and recommend changing one value at a time with a playtest after each. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script)
- If the user is preparing a bug-report flow, require device info, tester identity and screenshots or console logs at report time. [devforum, 2023-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838)
- If the user has just released or updated a game, tell them to check update ratings for dislike spikes and Create > Analytics feedback within the first days. [indie, 2026-08] -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 3:23](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=203)
- If the user is running paid ads, advise joining their own server to see where players leave, and shipping fixes between ad days. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 7:22](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=442)
- If blind testers cannot progress, recommend changing the design before blaming the tester. [devforum, 2023-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Never recommend a release until a full retest pass shows no critical issues. [devforum, 2023-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- When indie advice conflicts with 2026 official guidance on beta type, state the conflict and prefer the official guidance. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- Do not treat stream viewers or public beta testers as a substitute for closed, engaged testers when the game is unpolished. [indie, 2025-03] -- [PineappleDev 2025-03 6:37](https://youtu.be/IEAr0I_sUV4?t=397)
- If a featured or front-page surge is coming, then verify saving, plots and core progression before the traffic arrives. [indie, 2020-02] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 11:47](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=707)
- If a suggestion does not map to an observed problem, then log it and defer it. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 2:39](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=159)
- If public criticism is vague, then convert it into named tasks before publicly reacting. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 2:11](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=131)
- If attack telegraphs or boss cues are hard to see, then widen the camera to a fixed view. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:49](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=109)
- If a Team Test is planned, then run only committed scripts and keep drafts in solo sessions. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Emancyphur 2021-07 5:34](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=334)
- If a concept draws widespread negative reactions in pitch or prototype tests, then pivot before building further. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 8:51](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=531)
- If art feedback is blunt or unclear, then rewrite it as reference images of the wanted style. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 2:04](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=124)
- If you need an unbiased view of new-player experience, then ask an experienced outside developer to play live on a call. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-09 2:16](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=136)
- If running NPC testers, start with batches of about 50 and revise prompts before scaling beyond that. [official, 2026-04] -- [Roblox 2026-04 27:00](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1620)
- If the goal is early human testing before publish, invite no more than about 10 age-checked friends of similar ages. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a test build does something unexpected, play it through before filing a fix, and decide whether it is a bug or a feature. [official, 2026-09] -- [Roblox 2026-09 19:26](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=1166)
- If a player drops mid-match and matching skill is needed, consider NPC fill-in at matching skill level. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:13](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=793)
- Do not treat NPC test results as a replacement for real-player testing; use them as a first pass. [official, 2026-04] -- [Roblox 2026-04 27:00](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=1620)
- If a game with purchases is not ready for full release, consider a time-limited demo as the testing vehicle. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 21:00](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1260)
- If playtesters report dead-ends or unmarked fail states, treat the build as under-tested and run a full-loop pass before adding features. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 17:02](https://youtu.be/rjPW4GeeyFg?t=1022)
- If the target audience uses low-end hardware, include low-end device checks in every playtest round. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 12:19](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=739)
- If a playtest involves teleporting groups of players into a map, enforce a group-size cap and verify it before the session. [indie, 2025-07] -- [JustDucksey 2025-07 10:25](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=625)
- If a tester never dies, flag difficulty as possibly too low and check it against progression data. [indie, 2023-11] -- [Roox4 2023-11 5:44](https://youtu.be/kKiPL7wc8Rc?t=344)
- If a survival or resource loop feels bad in playtest, adjust drain and spawn rates first, then retest before changing other values. [indie, 2025-07] -- [JustDucksey 2025-07 9:45](https://youtu.be/s5nooZlK5Pc?t=585)
- If judging many submissions, use one fixed rubric and set cutoffs before scoring. [indie, 2025-10] -- [Duckable 2025-10 1:41](https://youtu.be/x7VmLQ9dBAI?t=101)
- Treat the cutoff percentages and game counts as examples from one self-reported contest, not benchmarks to copy exactly. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365)
- If a survey or test offers currency as a reward for completion, then remove or replace the reward before trusting the data. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 16:33](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=993)
- If playtest feedback describes a problem, then map its possible causes in a logic tree before choosing a fix. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 16:33](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=993)
- If a feature preview is needed for qualitative signal, then use an NDA'd fan panel of about 80-100 people rather than the general public. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 11:41](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=701)
- If a bug appears during friend playtests, then fix it and retest rather than halting the project. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 3:28](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=208)
- If asked to evaluate a game for funding or investment, then recommend playing it thoroughly yourself on mobile first. [indie, 2020-05] -- [TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks how long their game should take to finish, suggest measuring a fresh account and cite the ~25-minute indie reference only as a rough comparison. [indie, 2024-04] -- [janisjanis01 2024-04 5:42](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=342)
- If a stage or hazard is reported as impossible, recommend slowing the hazard first and retesting before redesigning. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 4:53](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=293) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user plans to scale paid acquisition, recommend a small single-channel soft launch first and check for engagement signals before increasing spend. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 37:46](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2266) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks who should test onboarding, suggest non-developers such as marketing staff, and have them log funnel friction and confirm tracking events fire. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 44:27](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2667)
- If a game has a survival, base-defence or resource-drain loss condition, add a playtest step that triggers the fail state in a real run and records the time to failure (T19015). [indie, 2022-01] -- [GnomeCode 2022-01 26:06](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=1566)
- Treat a single indie source as a prompt to test, not as a proven benchmark. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)

## pricing (coverage: covered)

- If a game has no pricing data, then start from comparable items and time-to-earn, launch, and iterate (lower if few buy, raise next version). [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 16:58](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1018)
- If the user hard-codes prices, then switch them to MarketplaceService lookups (InfoType.Product or GamePasses) before any optimization or regional pricing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/price-optimization)
- If the game has about 60,000 or more transactions per 30 days, then recommend Price Optimization with all products included; if fewer, then recommend manual A/B tests. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/price-optimization)
- If designing a shop, then include a very cheap entry tier (about 25-100 Robux, even 35), mid tiers, and a few high or whale tiers; add value-per-Robux labels and highlight the best bundle. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/monetization)
- If an offer is poor value or aimed at new players in the first 10-30 minutes, then make it cheap or hide it. [mixed, 2026-06] -- [Roblox 2025-09 21:55](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=1315)
- If setting a default price, then remember regional prices run 30-100% of it; choose it with the 70% max discount in mind. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization)
- If the user wants a sale, then make it genuine, time-bound, event-tied and infrequent; never label a permanent price 'on sale'.
- If pricing subscriptions, then use at least 49 Robux or an approved local tier; warn that changes are limited to once per 60 days and increases need 30 days' notice. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/subscriptions)
- If a creator-claimed number (conversion rate, sales) is cited, then label it self-reported and unverified; prefer official docs on platform mechanics. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If pricing UGC, then decide between the floor (volume) and above the floor (higher revenue share), and note the advance recoup math. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 18:23](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=1103)
- If a user asks about launch pricing, recommend a half-price first-week test to measure conversion and ARPPU, labelled as a self-reported method (T6538). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/experiments)
- If conversion is stable at a cheap price, suggest a controlled test upward rather than staying at launch-level prices (T6508, Q1032). [indie, 2026-05] -- [Yann 2026-05 2:44](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=164)
- If a user asks about a guaranteed minimum sale price, explain Amount Above Price Floor plus Do Not Price Below (T9593, N3803). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace)
- If a user prices an avatar item above floor, use the progressive share table and note in-game sales stay at 30% (N3512, T8663). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Where The Money Went 2026-10 8:15](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=495)
- If a user prices an animation pack, enforce the 300 Robux marketplace floor (T9771). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/subscription-design)
- If a user prices a plugin, enforce the $4.99 minimum (N3179). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-products)
- If a user asks whether a specific Robux price is 'good', compare to other examples in the same genre and label examples as self-reported and dated (N2408, N2487, N2652, N3260).
- When citing indie figures (dev share, failed game totals), label them as one creator's estimate, not a benchmark (N2946, N2655). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- Do not mix cloud/Extended Services budgets into Robux pricing answers (T8149, N3352-N3360).
- If the user's question is about regional pricing or price floors, cross-check with the existing regional and bounds rules before answering. [indie, 2025-07] -- [Arow 2025-07 1:09](https://youtu.be/KsaRtStNO3s?t=69)
- If the player country mix is mostly lower-income regions, test halved gamepass and developer product prices against current prices before committing. [indie, 2026-10] -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 3:42](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=222)
- If the audience is mostly US or Canada (higher spend), higher prices are viable; check current conversion data first. [indie, 2026-10] -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 3:42](https://youtu.be/4vuXAQt0PuQ?t=222)
- If pricing a game pass versus a developer product, price the pass higher. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 13:11](https://youtu.be/qLP05zvMsW0?t=791)
- If modeling revenue, use the current DevEx rate from DevHub and net out the ~70% share before comparing. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/vbzFbOUGERA?t=0)
- If a game targets 18+ DevEx users, expect ~42% more per Robux but fewer players; choose only if content fits. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 4:04](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=244)
- If a traffic spike or big promotion is planned, raise prices temporarily and set a reversion date. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 5:26](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=326)
- If pricing commissions, start with hourly rate x hours x (1 + complexity premium), then adjust the rate by observed job volume. [indie+devforum, 2026-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437)
- If a bestseller is high-volume, test a higher price on bulkier items before assuming current pricing is right. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Perstile 2025-05 2:14](https://youtu.be/ET3fRqaFmFc?t=134)
- If a timed or gated feature is planned, budget platform costs (uploads, publishing fee, private servers) before shipping. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/video-frames)
- Do not cite 2019 or 2024 DevEx figures as current; they conflict with each other and with 2026 data. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/vbzFbOUGERA?t=0)
- If asked to change a VIP or private server price that already has sales, warn that existing subscriptions will be cancelled and advise keeping the price fixed. [indie, 2024-06] -- [Mulch 2024-06 4:10](https://youtu.be/pqCXX06vlDw?t=250) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a game uses donation-style passes, recommend checking and likely disabling 'Use regional pricing', then verify the result in-game. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 3:02](https://youtu.be/skRRdrpf2F4?t=182)
- If asked about odd versus round price points, present the 199 vs 200 claim as an untested hypothesis from one creator and suggest an A/B test. [indie, 2024-02] -- [PolarBear 2024-02 2:02](https://youtu.be/nkmfdUbj4bQ?t=122)
- If asked about developer share by channel, cite the 70% vs 27% figure only as an unverified claim and recommend checking current DevEx terms. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Where The Money Went 2026-10 12:58](https://youtu.be/cJHug1Wnmhw?t=778)
- If asked about ad budgets, recommend a small test spend before a full campaign and cite the 8,000-Robux no-testing example as the cautionary case. [indie, 2026-06] -- [janisjanis01 2026-06 10:06](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=606)
- If asked about asset upload costs, advise turning off PBR in the generator to avoid extra Robux charges.
- If asked for current prices (ad credits, DevEx, gamepass norms), prefer newer sources and tell the user figures may be outdated.
- Treat all single-creator claims as hypotheses, not benchmarks. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 7:39](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=459)
- If a user asks what a gamepass should cost, pull or cite genre-level price clusters (75 / 99 / 149 and the VIP band of 175–199) instead of one game's min or max. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95082159892680/)
- If you cite an API price range, label it as a single-game snapshot dated 2026-11, and do not present it as a platform floor. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95082159892680/)
- If you model DevEx revenue, use $0.0038 per earned Robux (and $0.0054 for eligible 18+ US spend), state that these are effective June 8, 2026, and tell the user to confirm on the primary Roblox DevEx page. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/vbzFbOUGERA?t=0)
- If the user wants a VIP pass, suggest 175–199 Robux as the starting band. Only suggest a VIP+ tier if the game can deliver clearly distinct value, and never recommend changing VIP price after sales begin. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)

- Do not infer conversion rates or revenue impact from these price lists. They show what is listed, not what sells.
- If a user asks how to fund publishing, testing or ads with Robux, cite the wallet repurchase rate of $0.0038/Robux (N7522) and note tax applies. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- Do not treat the $0.0038 repurchase rate as the developer payout (DevEx) rate. Verify payout separately before modeling net revenue. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505)
- When estimating UGC avatar item test costs, include 750 Robux per test upload (N7545) and flag that the figure is lower-confidence. [devforum, 2020-12] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/upcoming-changes-to-avatar-catalog-upload-requirements/915265)

- If a source's price is a guess or refers to a non-Roblox product, exclude it from pricing benchmarks. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Cumin 2026-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/kYwCqstp9eA?t=0)

[indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- When suggesting a Robux shortcut for a grindable item, compare it against the item's grind cost first, and do not set a shortcut that makes the grind pointless. [indie, 2024-11] -- [Mulch 2024-11 1:23](https://youtu.be/axxmwooE-J0?t=83)

## retention-d1-d7 (coverage: covered)

- If D1 is below ~10%, then audit the first 5 minutes, the tutorial funnel and performance before adding rewards. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/funnel-events)
- If D1 is fine but D7 is under ~2%, then add visible short/mid/long-term goals, weekly events and content depth; don't just add daily rewards. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention)
- If asked for benchmarks, then cite D1 12-15%, D7 2-3%, session above 10 min and label them indie self-reported except the official 16% D1 90th percentile.
- If building a return hook, then stack daily reward + streak + playtime rewards + daily quests + offline progress, and use loss framing sparingly given Roblox's anti-pressure stance. [indie, 2026-07] -- [MoseyTube 2026-07 2:35](https://youtu.be/a170E9klbP4?t=155)
- If D1 drops suddenly, then segment by acquisition source and cohort before changing the game. [mixed, 2025-10] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-10 13:21](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=801)
- If launching, then plan a day-8 hook and weekly updates because spikes halve in ~3 weeks and the algorithm judges days 8-28. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 3:32](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=212)
- If a game is social, then add invite rewards for both sides and friend-play incentives. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 2:53](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=173)
- If using notifications, then restrict to 13+ opted-in users, max 1 per day, tied to meaningful events. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion)
- If ads bring players who leave fast, then fix the game, not the ad; use Beta mode to optimise out of home recs. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Poke 2025-12 12:27](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=747)
- If a source makes an unverified claim (90% event churn, quote signs raising playtime), then flag it as weak evidence. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Poke 2025-12 12:27](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=747)
- Do not treat platform-wide DAU (N7469) as a retention benchmark for an individual game; use cohort D1/D7/D30 data from the game's own analytics instead. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Q1768 supports the existing FOMO rule (Use FOMO hooks, but recognise the ethical trade-off); do not add it as a new rule and do not weaken the ethical caveat when citing it. [indie+devforum, 2026-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-technically-superior-game-isn%E2%80%99t-succeeding-on-roblox-and-why-the-algorithm-ignores-it/4461606)
- When a retention flow depends on teleporting (friend joins, return-to-game links), add a capped retry with a delay and a visible failure state, citing N7596's example values. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- Do not treat the SafeTeleport example values as fixed platform limits; confirm against current official docs before use. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If a game has a social-linking option (e.g. Discord social SDK), treat linking as a D28 retention feature and compare linked vs non-linked cohorts before crediting it. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 13:21](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=801)
- If a player has been inactive for a set period, route them into a win-back flow and measure their spend and return rate separately from new users. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406)
- If the game is an evolution or rebirth style, test how early rebirth is reachable against D1 and D7 cohorts before tuning it. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Mulch 2026-08 4:00](https://youtu.be/MMuXKVreLj0?t=240)
- Treat all figures from indie sources here (31% D28 lift, 1.5 extra play days, 30-minute sessions, 10-minute unlock wait) as self-reported and unverified; do not present them as benchmarks without caveats. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 13:21](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=801)
- If a game has no lobby, restart, or revive path after death, add one before tuning other D1 or D7 hooks. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 3:35](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=215)
- If a run takes longer than one sitting in playtests, shorten it or add natural stopping points before adding rewards. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 15:30](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=930)
- Treat the Cumin session-length figures as one self-reported data point; do not generalise them into a Roblox-wide benchmark. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=0)
- When assessing a core loop, playtest it for at least 30 minutes, since the reported drop-off point is 10 to 30 minutes. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 3:39](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=219)
- Prefer milestone letters or notes over constant on-screen dialogue when building re-engagement touches. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 22:27](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=1347)
- Cite the T and N IDs above when recommending run-length or lobby changes, and flag the figures as self-reported.
- If a player has been absent for more than a day, prioritise a return hook (login reward, update note, or event) over a long-term reminder. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 9:32](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=572)
- Do not treat YouTube watch-percentage targets (N7958) as in-game retention metrics.
- Skip adding new FOMO or update-cadence advice that repeats existing rules; cite the existing rule instead. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 0:43](https://youtu.be/HGobKizBHqQ?t=43)
- If asked for a day-1 retention benchmark, state that the sources give none (N8141) and do not invent a number.
- Treat the tripled session-time claim (N8080, Q1950) as an unverified self-report and say so when citing it. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 0:00](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=0)
- When recommending a daily login mechanic, offer both a reset-on-miss calendar (T18844) and a simple 24-hour crate (T18877), and say which fits a habit loop vs. a casual return. [indie, 2024-12] -- [janisjanis01 2024-12 14:29](https://youtu.be/Jc7yQTMFexk?t=869) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- When designing a daily reward ladder, check leaderboard or cohort depth data before going past day 10 (N8135). [indie, 2025-10] -- [janisjanis01 2025-10 15:07](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=907)
- Flag that all janisjanis01 figures are self-reported and from a single indie developer. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 0:31](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=31)

## roblox-platform-changes (coverage: covered)

- If a user asks about launching a new game, then warn about the 16+ gate until 500 highly engaged players and plan accordingly. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Tizzy 2026-06 2:39](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=159)
- If code uses RenderStepped/Stepped, then recommend PreRender/PreSimulation. [indie, 2025-03] -- [Crusherfire 2025-03 27:36](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1656) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If code uses HapticService, AvatarUnificationMode, or Avatar API V1-V3, then flag deprecation dates and migrate. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-26-30-2026/4966810)
- If a game's impressions drop, then check seasonality, competitors and algorithm changes before changing the game. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq)
- If asked about payouts, then cite Creator Rewards and not Engagement-Based Payouts; mention DevEx +8.5%. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts)
- If asked about all-ages release, then list 2FA, age verification, Premium/fee, Kids/Select review. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places)
- If tools modify the client, then warn about termination risk. [devforum, 2025-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/an-update-on-automated-action-against-modified-clients/3640609)
- If a date in the notes is before 2026-11-01, then verify whether the change already took effect. [devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211)
- If a feature needs chat or party play between kids and adults, flag it as blocked by the 2026-01 age-based chat restriction and check whether trusted connections has shipped. [official, 2026-01] -- [Roblox 2026-01 21:32](https://youtu.be/m5vqnNSCFCI?t=1292)
- If a feature targets all ages, design the chat or social layer so kids only see same-age peers. [official, 2026-01] -- [Roblox 2026-01 21:32](https://youtu.be/m5vqnNSCFCI?t=1292)
- If a platform threshold (trial count, Plus runway, age band) comes only from an indie source with no OFFICIAL tag, label it unverified and check official Roblox docs before advising. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 4:24](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=264)
- If performance drops after a release, check DevForum for reported platform issues before changing code. [indie, 2025-11] -- [janisjanis01 2025-11 16:47](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=1007)
- If a game is under the 16+ lock, track highly engaged 16+ users against the 500/60-day figure, and state that the figure is unverified. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 4:24](https://youtu.be/FpC7qnwDMDQ?t=264)
- When advising on ratings, use age bands from official sources, not the indie-reported bands alone. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- Do not present pessimistic claims about safety features (Q1920) as fact. Report them as one creator's opinion. [indie, 2025-09] -- [janisjanis01 2025-09 9:41](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=581)
- Before recommending a strategy pivot from reported algorithm changes, require impression and retention data that supports it (Q1908). [indie, 2025-12] -- [janisjanis01 2025-12 17:10](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=1030)
- Flag the Dec 1 and Dec 11, 2026 deadlines when they are relevant to the user's question. [devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-26-30-2026/4966810)

## scripting-and-performance (coverage: covered)

- If a feature grants currency/items, then implement and validate on the server and never trust client values. [mixed, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 33:38](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2018)
- If handling a purchase, then use ProcessReceipt with PurchaseId dedupe, return PurchaseGranted only after saving, NotProcessedYet if player absent. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 2:32](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=152)
- If writing DataStore code, then key by UserId, pcall, save on PlayerRemoving and BindToClose. [indie, 2018-08] -- [AlvinBlox 2018-08 5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312)
- If building a competitive or character-movement game, then recommend server authority mode and bind to simulation; replace old anti-fly or fake-projectile hacks.
- If optimising mobile, then check in-camera triangles (~500k), draw calls (~500), memory (~1.3 GB on 2 GB) on a real low-end device. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 4:32](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=272)
- If a script runs every frame, then convert to events or spread across frames, and avoid RenderStepped unless needed. [official, 2024-09] -- [Roblox 2024-09 25:25](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1525)
- If frame drops are reported, then triage: real hardware, frame rate, stats, Microprofiler, compare dumps. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 10:31](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=631)
- If many parts or NPCs exist, then use StreamingEnabled, anchor/disable idle NPC animators, avoid server-only attributes on parts. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 17:16](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1036)
- If asked about input hints, then use PreferredInput and input actions; if building React UI, use cleanup, dependency arrays and bindings. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 17:49](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1069)
- If a frame or CPU problem is reported, check the 16 ms budget on client and server first, then profile with MicroProfiler before changing code. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization)
- If a server crashes shortly after startup, look for uncleaned startup clones and memory spikes before anything else. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization)
- If a config or badge call is needed, run it on the server inside pcall with a fallback value. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs)
- If a config value changes, refresh the snapshot on UpdateAvailable rather than reading it once. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs)
- If error logs are noisy, strip unique IDs first, then add ordered Group or Ignore rules, testing each regex before saving. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report)
- If a VR build uses a custom camera, merge current VR camera fixes or replace the camera with the default. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines)
- If a rewarded ad or Robux transfer is involved, store the returned IDs and correlate them with ProcessReceipt. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/robux-transfers)
- If a UI looks wrong on phones, scale strokes and sizes with viewport height. [indie, 2025-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2025-04 0:19](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=19)
- If code uses task.desynchronize or ConnectParallel, check each API member's thread-safety tier and wrap every write in task.synchronize(). [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading)
- If a signal handler depends on a value set by a callback, set SignalBehavior to Deferred explicitly and yield before reading. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred)
- If a cache is keyed by Roblox Instances, do not assume weak tables collect them; hold a strong reference and release it on Parent=nil. [indie+devforum, 2024-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954)
- If a Touched or ProximityPrompt handler can fire repeatedly, add a debounce flag or attribute cooldown before doing work. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions)
- If you claim an optimisation helps, profile before and after with os.clock or the Script Profiler; keep it only if it shows gains. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/native-code-gen)
- If a DataStore or Open Cloud call happens in bulk, check GetRequestBudgetForRequestType, count UpdateAsync as two requests, and keep GetAsync caching on. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits)
- If a remote sends numeric data at high frequency, choose the smallest fixed-width type or a buffer, and send only fields the client needs. [indie+devforum, 2025-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-best-practices-how-to-keep-your-games-ping-low/1913475)
- If a source conflicts with 2026-10 official docs, follow the official doc and note that the older advice is superseded. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- If gameplay or reward logic runs on the client, move it to ServerScriptService and validate it server-side. [mixed, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 33:38](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2018)
- If many objects share one behaviour, use a CollectionService tag and one central script rather than duplicated scripts. [indie+devforum, 2025-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345)
- If code uses Motor6D C0, C1, Part0 or Part1 and the target place has AnimationConstraints, migrate to Attachments before editing.
- If writing a raycast with filters, use ExcludeInstances or IncludeInstances, not the older FilterDescendantsInstances. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575)
- If a remote fires many small events per frame, batch them into one per frame; keep unreliable remotes for event spam only. [indie, 2025-03] -- [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982)
- If an NPC or projectile needs collisions, use physics; if it only moves cosmetically on many parts, benchmark CFrame vs physics first. [indie, 2022-07] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 0:41](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=41)
- If a feature spends currency or grants rewards, add a server-side in-progress flag and validate every input. [indie, 2026-06] -- [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127)
- If a path is needed, wrap ComputeAsync in pcall, check Status, and fall back safely. [indie+devforum, 2022-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779)
- If a Store asset is being built, remove require(id), LoadAsset, LoadString and getfenv/setfenv. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages)
- If a claim is a single speaker's benchmark, treat it as unverified and measure in MicroProfiler on a midrange device before adopting it. [indie, 2024-11] -- [Suphi Kaner 2024-11 1:12:12](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=4332)
- If bulk-loading thousands of instances, yield on a time budget rather than after each item. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-08 21:38](https://youtu.be/0RRGneqSZRY?t=1298)
- If zones overlap, bind them to a one-at-a-time group and disconnect listeners on removal. [indie, 2022-07] -- [Emancyphur 2022-07 9:28](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=568)
- If an object is required, use WaitForChild with an explicit timeout; if optional, use FindFirstChild with a conditional. [indie, 2025-05] -- [LovJak 2025-05 1:12](https://youtu.be/M-o5hLZbjGY?t=72)
- If PathfindingService fails on stairs, add custom waypoints; precompute hot routes into a module. [indie, 2023-04] -- [Roox4 2023-04 3:15](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=195)
- If doors block NPC paths, build a PathfindingLink and return true from PathfindingLinkReached. [indie, 2025-10] -- [Crit 2025-10 4:00](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=240)
- If you need a world-wide scan every second, replace it with events or a central manager. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91)
- If you use FindPartsInRegion3, switch to GetPartBoundsInBox. [indie, 2023-07] -- [Amora 2023-07 3:57](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=237)
- If you need hit or projectile physics for many objects, prefer closed-form math on the server over replicated parts. [indie, 2025-06] -- [Roox4 2025-06 2:46](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=166)
