- If you need the character's CFrame, use character:GetPivot() in new code. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 3:31](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=211)
- Default to CFrame for orientation; introduce quaternions only when combining rotations or blending orientations. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-08 4:49](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=289)
- Do not copy tuned camera numbers (60, 12, -1, 3.25, 1.5) into other games; tune per game and check for end-of-zoom jolt. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 2:09](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=129)
- Keep camera and visual logic on the client; server-side code should not drive camera state. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 0:42](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=42)
- Treat low-advice sources (T17793, T17975) as optional guidance, not as required practice. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Possible Panda 2026-01 4:31](https://youtu.be/AOAh-4Q9ARw?t=271)
- If the task involves teleporting, use TeleportAsync inside pcall and do not suggest deprecated teleport methods. [indie, 2022-08] -- [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 7:18](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=438)
- If the task involves recursion, require a base case and a MAX_DEPTH counter before generating structures. [indie, 2023-10] -- [B Ricey 2023-10 0:53](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=53)
- If the task involves grid building, place snapping math in a shared ReplicatedStorage module and use // with one GRID_SIZE constant. [indie, 2025-12] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 4:51](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=291)
- If placement or occupancy checks are needed, use OverlapParams Include lists with a box shrunk about 0.2 studs per axis. [indie, 2025-12] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 32:21](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1941)
- If generated code is involved, test each system separately and profile FPS after each addition. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Duckable 2025-08 7:09](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=429)
- If an instance may exist at startup and also be added later, handle both paths. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 7:52](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=472)
- If writing per-frame smoothing, use alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt). [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 5:42](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=342)
- If a required value is missing, fail loudly with assert(condition, message) instead of continuing silently. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 7:55](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=475)
- If a pickup or Touched event is involved, set CanCollide false and award once with disconnect. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 12:28](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=748)
- If the source is indie, label figures as self-reported and verify against official docs before presenting them as platform fact. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Platform Aeronaut (transcript) 2026-07](https://transcripts.platformaeronaut.com/transcripts/RBLX-2Q26-transcript)
- If per-frame CPU work is heavy and cores are idle, use ConnectParallel inside an Actor with task.desynchronize; otherwise keep normal connections. [indie, 2024-05] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-05 6:53](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=413)
- Before enabling any parallel Luau, profile with the Microprofiler to confirm the bottleneck and the thread layout. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design)
- If a require path is built through variables, rewrite it as a literal full path so Studio tooling can resolve it. [indie, 2024-05] -- [Crusherfire 2024-05 3:32](https://youtu.be/yncfwrBAWVw?t=212)
- When calling UserOwnsGamePassAsync, wrap it in pcall and warn on failure rather than letting the error break the script. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs)
- After a live release, re-check memory and button logic with real players, since leaks can appear only under real load. [indie, 2020-02] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 27:52](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=1672)
- If an animation script for tagged parts does nothing, first check that the parts exist in Workspace and that the tag query returns them. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 2:46](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=166)
- If only the first tagged part moves, give each part its own thread. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 1:24](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=84)
- If a part gets animated more than once, add an active-animation table and skip duplicates. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 3:29](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=209)
- If item transfers can be duplicated, move the logic to the server and add dupe tests before launch. [indie, 2025-07] -- [janisjanis01 2025-07 9:21](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=561)
- If error counts spike, find the single repeating line before changing other code. [indie, 2024-04] -- [janisjanis01 2024-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=0)
- If pathfinding fails, start from a tested tutorial rather than adapting untested AI output. [indie, 2025-10] -- [janisjanis01 2025-10 6:44](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=404)
- If a Touched handler collects or awards something, resolve the player with GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent), return early on nil, and debounce. [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 5:02](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=302)
- If an animation plays repeatedly, look up an existing track by name in GetPlayingAnimationTracks() before calling LoadAnimation. [indie, 2022-01] -- [GnomeCode 2022-01 1:48](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=108)
- If watching one property, connect to GetPropertyChangedSignal instead of .Changed. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 2:42](https://youtu.be/paRHxtuk_Mc?t=162)
- If a loop over character children changes properties, filter with IsA('BasePart') first. [indie, 2020-04] -- [GnomeCode 2020-04 3:38](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=218)
- If a service, folder, or stat fails to resolve, check exact spelling and capitalisation first and confirm nothing was renamed. [indie, 2023-03] -- [Emancyphur 2023-03 0:56](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=56)
- If a leaderboard number is wrong, update the IntValue's Value rather than renaming objects. [indie, 2018-12] -- [AlvinBlox 2018-12 13:20](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=800)
- Before moving an object that must reset later, cache its original Position. [indie, 2021-03] -- [GnomeCode 2021-03 10:54](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=654)

## social-features-and-virality (coverage: covered)

- If designing a new game, then add at least one social hook (invite button, friends teleport, trading, co-op) before launch. [mixed, 2025-04] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 13:01](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=781)
- If the user asks about invites, then implement an invite prompt and check ReferredByPlayerID to reward both inviter and invitee; prefer play-together design over pure reward pop-ups. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 11:01](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=661)
- If servers fill up, then reserve a social buffer so friends can join. [official, 2018-07] -- [Roblox 2018-07 11:07](https://youtu.be/4rvzuYMtSlA?t=667)
- If a game is multi-place, then enable experience-to-place friend access and a Friends/Nearby teleport menu. [official, 2023-09] -- [Roblox 2023-09 28:26](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=1706)
- If planning promotion with little budget, then pitch small creators with a playable build and unique promo codes; do not pay big influencers early. [mixed, 2025-04] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:08:41](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=4121)
- If planning TikTok content, then post clippable funny moments, not plain 'play my game' posts; fix bugs first. [mixed, 2026-07] -- [Roblox 2024-09 23:57](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=1437)
- If adding competition, then consider friend leaderboards via ordered data store, but add cooperative mechanics for new players. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2026-10 19:34](https://youtu.be/LIKiUlkTqZc?t=1174)
- If new players face veterans, then use bots or new-player servers for first matches. [official, 2023-09] -- [Roblox 2023-09 16:16](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=976)
- If supporting streamers, then add streamer mode, HUD hide, kick/whitelist tools. [mixed, 2026-03] -- [Roblox 2023-09 0:41](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=41)
- If quoting indie numbers (Tsunami, Moments, singleplayer 99%), then label them unverified; prefer official statements for mechanics.
- If building invites, use Share Links with LaunchData instead of deep links. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion)
- If reading join data, poll GetJoinData for several seconds before assuming there is no LaunchData. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/invite-prompts)
- If awarding friend or group rewards, add bonuses for group play but never penalize solo players. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion)
- If the game is social, do not split friends into isolated server instances. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies)
- If publishing or saving clips, call CaptureService at key moments and obtain consent first. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 16:04](https://youtu.be/lUc3Xj8Ojd8?t=964)
- If citing Roblox platform-wide statistics, present them as context, not as benchmarks for a specific game.
- If the sources conflict on creator outreach, prefer targeted community outreach and playtest exchanges; use code-based offers to small creators only with care.
- If a source is dated before 2026-10 and conflicts with Roblox Creator Docs, prefer the Creator Docs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences)
- If implementing a referral system, make the reward banner description match the Studio implementation. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/referral-system)
- If a game is solo by design, flag the risk and propose co-op or divisible-task mechanics before adding social UI.
- If planning notifications, check the 1-per-user-per-day limit first and personalize with parameters and launch_data. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experience-notifications)
- If you need custom friend-click behavior, set teleportToFriend = false and handle FriendsLocator.clicked. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/friends-locator)
- If the game is for classrooms or education, disable voice chat, limit text chat, and use private servers. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/planning-for-educational-settings)
- If TikTok traffic claims conflict, prefer the 2026-10 rasdev figure but flag it as self-reported and mention the 2026-01 janisjanis fade. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 2:42](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=162)
- Mark [low-advice] items (comment-bait hooks, cross-server chat defaults) as unverified and recommend testing before committing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/cross-server-chat)
- Do not recommend reposting older content to game a new account without noting it can look like spam if overused. [indie, 2026-01] -- [janisjanis01 2026-01 7:33](https://youtu.be/IWzAHgHPhhU?t=453)
- Use Party Simulator to test party APIs before publishing any party-dependent feature. [devforum, 2026-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904)
- Use official [OFFICIAL] items over indie claims when they conflict. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools)
- If a user asks about voice chat, check server size against the 100-user cap and note the default-on rule for age-checked 13+ users. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/voice-chat)
- If a UI shows 'Connections' or 'Trusted Connections', rename the label to 'Friends' or 'Trusted Friends'. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/friends-are-back-expanding-trusted-friends-for-a-new-way-to-chat-and-play/4554217)
- If designing cross-age chat, do not assume it works in every country; check Trusted Friends availability. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/friends-are-back-expanding-trusted-friends-for-a-new-way-to-chat-and-play/4554217)
- If building an emote system, start with the EmoteBar module defaults and configure instead of accepting defaults when they don't fit. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules)
- If choosing where to spend item promo budget, prefer YouTube based on the most recent (2026-07) data, but test first. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Erthlord 2026-07 14:46](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=886)
- If planning an async turn-based game, plan for upcoming developer push notifications and check their rollout. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- Treat indie figures as self-reported. Do not generalize one creator's paid-creator or physical-promo result. [indie, 2024-03] -- [1Coal 2024-03 6:10](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=370)
- If the target audience includes under-13 players, do not rely on Discord or social buttons for community reach. [indie, 2019-10] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-10 16:22](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=982)
- If a game is for a broad audience, plan both a mid-size creator pitch and a large-creator pitch, and mark which is which.
- If a new short-form account gets no views, follow the cadence rule: one per day, space posts 30+ minutes apart, and add a second only after views grow. [indie, 2025-07] -- [#7Phantom 2025-07 11:59](https://youtu.be/xjZXsHxKerE?t=719)
- If a devlog underperforms on TikTok, test Instagram Reels with the same clip before deciding the platform is wrong. [indie, 2026-08] -- [studio rook. 2026-08 1:24](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=84)
- If a game has empty servers for solo players, prioritize fixing that before pushing friend-invite campaigns. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 8:10](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=490)
- If designing group rewards, pair a group-join reward with badges that name co-play (for example, Play With Friends). [indie, 2026-07] -- [LovJak 2026-07 2:05](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=125)
- If planning trading, gate it behind a progression milestone rather than making it available from the start. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Lnor Roblox 2026-09 7:47](https://youtu.be/gSFxStWqmgM?t=467)
- Treat low-advice and single-data-point figures (for example the 20,000 event peak and 501 devlog views) as examples, not benchmarks. [indie, 2026-05] -- [rasdev 2026-05 8:23](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=503)
- If a request concerns chat or voice features, check the official Roblox docs first and treat indie claims about rollout dates as unverified. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If the user asks about PvP design, propose stealing or conflict mechanics with counterplay and note that the sources do not cover moderation. [indie, 2026-02] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-02](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-brainrot-invasion-how-absurd-meme-games-conquered-roblox-s-charts)
- If the user asks for group-size rules, use the 4-player party cap as an indie-reported example, not a platform rule. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/119958473240233/)
- Label numbers from indie sources as self-reported and numbers from Roblox IR or public API as official. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- Do not treat the favorites figure as a benchmark without confirming its scope; the source does not say which experience it refers to.
- If a feature involves stranger chat or voice, default to filtered communication and gate unfiltered options behind trusted connections (T17573). [official, 2025-07] -- [Roblox 2025-07 12:37](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=757)
- If the user asks about friend-list naming, use Connections as the platform term and note the older Friends label as the prior rule (T17573). [official, 2025-07] -- [Roblox 2025-07 12:37](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=757)
- If the audience includes young players, prefer pre-set Quick Words over free-text coordination once available (T17676). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the user asks how to bring in players from outside Roblox, recommend Experience Details Page share links and the web-player join path (T17685). [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Newsroom 2026-09](https://about.roblox.com/newsroom/2026/09/rdc-2026-the-world-needs-more-play)
- If weighing safety against openness in social design, apply the official framing that both are measured together (Q1770).
- If citing creator reach, label N7511 as a self-reported indie figure. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)
- If a game targets players who use Discord, then recommend a social SDK integration, and verify its current terms first. [indie, 2019-10] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-10 16:22](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=982) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**

## sound-and-music (coverage: covered)

- If the task is new audio work, then use Audio objects (AudioPlayer, AudioEmitter, AudioDeviceOutput, AudioCompressor, AudioReverb, AudioEqualizer, AudioFader) and not Sound or SoundGroup. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials)
- If an existing project uses Sound or SoundGroup, then keep it working and translate to Audio object equivalents when you touch that system. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/sound/groups)
- If a sound has a world position (fire, NPC, waterfall, object), then use 3D: AudioPlayer plus AudioEmitter on the object, or a Sound parented to a part or Attachment. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials)
- If a sound is global (UI, background music, narration, omnipresent ambience), then use 2D: wire AudioPlayer to AudioDeviceOutput under SoundService. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials)
- If a sound covers a large area (rain, ocean, waterfall, city, room), then use a volumetric emitter, and first confirm the volumetric setting is Enabled in SoundService. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/design-dark-soundscapes)
- If background music is used, then set Looped, Volume about 0.2, and call Play() from a client script on join; never leave the game silent. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio)
- If a loop clicks or seams, then cut it at a zero crossing; if repetition is obvious, then layer loops of different lengths. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/sound-design)
- If priority sounds (dialogue, GUI, weapons) compete with music or ambience, then sidechain-compress the low-priority group under the high-priority one. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/design-dark-soundscapes)
- If TTS lines repeat, then cache them with AssetService:CreateAssetAsync() and keep Text under 300 characters; if speech must cut through other audio, then raise TTS Volume and vary VoiceID, Pitch and Speed. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-text-to-speech)
- If a build is about to ship, then playtest on device speakers and headphones, check Volume Mixer if Toolbox audio is silent, and tune compressor values by ear on the real source levels. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware)
- If a user's audio is flagged or privately restricted, first run the Audio Discovery plugin. Then replace the asset with owned uploads, licensed catalog tracks, or Pro Sound Effects. [devforum, 2022-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user needs radios or boomboxes, recommend a licensed-catalog picker instead of ID-based playback. [devforum, 2022-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a trigger can be re-entered, debounce it using AudioPlayer.Ended and call Play only when the sound is not already playing. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-3D-audio)
- If the user builds per-zone ambience, run it from a LocalScript and stop the previous zone before starting the next. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-3D-audio)
- If a sound is too loud, check Volume first (keep it around 2 or below for loud sources) before adjusting other properties. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/sound/objects)
- If a sound cuts off abruptly at max distance, raise RollOffMaxDistance for Inverse or Linear rolloff, or use a gradual DistanceAttenuation curve. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/sound/objects)
- If the user asks about accessibility, suggest per-category volume, per-trigger mute, distinct per-object sounds, directional captions and visual pairing. [devforum, 2021-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919)
- If the user asks about effects, recommend the newer Audio effects set for new projects and mention that dynamic SoundEffects are legacy.
- When quoting 2022 figures (catalog size, upload limits, privacy thresholds), state they are historical and must be verified.
- Prefer 2026 Roblox docs over 2021–2023 DevForum advice when they conflict on the same topic. [devforum, 2021-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507)
- If a key pickup, fight start, or escape is a story beat, trigger a music or stinger cue for it. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:31](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=91)
- If a climax track is louder than the ambience, keep its Volume near 2 or below and check the mix on device speakers and headphones. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware)
- Keep the quiet ambient base running underneath stingers and spikes so the mix does not go silent between cues. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:31](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=91)
- If a source is tagged [low-advice], verify the technique against official docs before recommending it as final. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 16:34](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=994) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If you implement random sound pools on legacy Sound, gate each pick on Sound.Ended; on Audio objects, gate on AudioPlayer.Ended. [indie, 2024-05] -- [Crusherfire 2024-05 12:58](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=778)
- If a random-selection tip is used, cache the child list once and only re-query it when the contents change. [indie, 2024-05] -- [Crusherfire 2024-05 11:34](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=694)
- If a sound search for a game element is taking long and no stock option fits, switch to creating or recording the sound yourself (T18564). [indie, 2025-01] -- [janisjanis01 2025-01 8:35](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=515)

## studio-business-funding-and-publishing (coverage: covered)

- If a user asks about funding, first check whether the project is educational (then Community Fund and Education Open Office Hours), large and ambitious (Game Fund), or small (Accelerator or private investors). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/getting-started-for-developers-education)
- If a user is pitching private investors, then require game progress, a monetization plan, a 1-2-3 month roadmap, a deal structure and an application listing role, use of funds, amount and timeline. [indie, 2020-04] -- [TheDevKing 2020-04 8:09](https://youtu.be/VOKFE8Qi16c?t=489)
- If a user needs a valuation, then compute total worth = assets + (day-one revenue x 7) and percentage = investment / worth, and warn that it is a rough, optimistic method from one indie creator. [indie, 2020-11] -- [The Letus Garden 2020-11 3:52](https://youtu.be/pAfbzNESkYk?t=232)
- If a user has no audience, then recommend building community before fundraising, citing the 2026 Naavik advice over the 2020 advice. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Naavik Gaming Podcast 2026-03 35:01](https://youtu.be/mykMEsgoi5E?t=2101)
- If quoting revenue, share or fund figures from indie sources, then label them self-reported and unverified; prefer official Roblox statements for program mechanics. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)
- If a user asks about Game Fund terms, then state the 2021 figures ($500,000 minimum, milestone-based release) and recommend checking current official documentation. [devforum, 2021-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-game-fund/1361477) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user offers a deal, then advise against lowballing, and note that reference points are 5-8% equity for proven teams and 10-15% fund revenue shares. [indie, 2023-12] -- [VuukStudios 2023-12 12:52](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=772)
- If a user is a solo beginner, then advise using free resources and friends first and adding partners as the game grows.
- If a user manages studio groups, then warn about the 90-day block and permanent loss from group removals, and mention the 100-group Premium limit. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/group-guidelines)
- If a user expects Jumpstart or similar support to be salary or publishing, then correct them: it is in-kind support. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart)
- If asked whether Roblox programs give equity or grants, answer that as of March 2026 they did not, citing T17604, and note that terms may have changed since. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- If asked about Roblox program support, describe it as mentorship, promotion, and possible future incentives, and flag that this is indie-reported (self-reported) rather than official. [indie, 2026-03] -- [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/)
- Do not present the 2026-03 characterization as current platform policy without checking newer official sources.
- If a user asks whether to self-publish or get a publisher, present the development-vs-distribution tradeoff from T17959 and say it is a single self-reported view. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 45:23](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2723)
- Do not treat this tip as a recommendation to sign with a publisher; it only suggests considering one.
- When a user asks about A/B testing or experimentation, first check their revenue stage against the GameMakers thresholds (N7943, N7944) before recommending heavy process. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 15:01](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=901) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Do not use the Liftoff or AppLovin valuation figures (N7988, N7989) as benchmarks for pricing a studio, game, or deal. They are low-advice, self-reported market context from February 2026.
- Label GameMakers stage thresholds (N7943, N7944) as self-reported indie figures, not platform policy or official guidance. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GameMakers 2025-02 15:01](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=901)
- If a user asks about the market for Roblox-adjacent companies, present the valuation figures as cited claims that need verification from primary filings.
- If a user asks about the scale at which an independent game competes with large publishers, cite the 100M+ revenue mobile threshold (N8037) as a mobile-market reference, and note it is a 2021 self-reported figure. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 10:46](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=646)
- If a user is pitching an early-stage company to investors, assume the investor lacks a track record to deploy capital with confidence (Q1937) and push them to supply concrete data alongside the story. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 29:17](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=1757)
- Do not present the EA cash-reserve figure (N8036) as guidance for indie funding decisions; it is context only.

## team-and-budget (coverage: covered)

- If the user lacks a skill (UI, modeling, scripting), then recommend outsourcing only that piece, starting with a small paid task. [devforum, 2018-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user wants to hire, then require portfolio, referrals or mutuals, plus a paid sample before the main contract. [devforum, 2018-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601)
- If drafting a hiring post, then include role, pay in Robux/USD, timeline and scope. [mixed, 2025-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-01 15:18](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=918)
- If the user pays a contractor, then recommend a written statement of work, pay on completion or weekly, and at most 50% upfront. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Tizzy 2026-09 7:03](https://youtu.be/E_tVm3bc5Xo?t=423)
- If a contractor misses deadlines, then give one warning and replace them. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Tizzy 2026-10 21:18](https://youtu.be/_JyiKfNNqls?t=1278)
- If the user is solo with no capital, then suggest commissions or partnering with a complementary builder/scripter, noting commissions are low leverage.
- If the user considers a brand deal, then warn about minimum guarantees and require a dedicated brand owner. [official, 2023-09] -- [Roblox 2023-09 27:26](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1646) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user considers selling or partnering, then advise own lawyer, own valuation, worst-case clause analysis and due diligence. [official, 2025-09] -- [Roblox 2025-09 15:23](https://youtu.be/Z3T33q4-Y_A?t=923)
- If the game is gaining traction and updates are stressful, then add specialists and a producer/PM rather than crunching.
- Always label indie cost and headcount figures as self-reported and unverified. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 40:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2432)
- If the budget does not cover every planned hire's quote, do not start; cut scope or delay hiring first (T3460). [devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578)
- If a project needs more than about 4 people, challenge the scope before adding headcount (T3786).
- If a candidate has not shipped a finished game, treat them as a test hire on a paid sample task, not a core contractor (T3463).
- If you cannot pay someone on a steady schedule, do not hire them; reduce scope instead (Q759). [indie, 2026-03] -- [DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 2:05](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=125)
- If you are considering debt to fund a hire, do not borrow; use only affordable spending (T3467). [devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578)
- If a team member's work is not reviewed against scope at checkpoints, add inspection before assuming delivery (Q695). [devforum, 2022-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578)
- Before selling, confirm government ID or age verification; a phone number does not count (T4554). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/creator-store)
- Before transferring group ownership, remove group-owned commerce items; the transfer is blocked otherwise (T4313). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/commerce-products)
- Before charging for paid access, confirm the account is at least 30 days old and ID-verified (N2082, N1902). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux)
- When a teammate needs to see a change, point them to Studio activity history and annotations, not screenshots (T3616). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools)
- If a task is a specialist skill (animation, modeling, scripting, music), find a paid candidate for it instead of doing it solo, and verify skill before assigning. [devforum, 2018-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601)
- If a bottleneck role blocks progress for more than one development cycle, hire for it rather than waiting on one person. [indie, 2025-06] -- [RoBuilder 2025-06 1:58:41](https://youtu.be/krMTcTvaj_8?t=7121)
- If outsourcing repetitive configuration work, require a recorded process before accepting delivery. [indie, 2026-02] -- [janisjanis01 2026-02 4:43](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=283)
- If setting a deadline, add buffer for contractor delays and never set one shorter than the estimated work. [indie+devforum, 2025-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601)
- If giving feedback, address the work and the fix, not the person; replace a contractor only when quality is clearly below standard. [devforum, 2018-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-cooperate-with-a-development-team/122601)
- If adding a Team Create collaborator, first confirm the age check is complete and that Edit is allowed (friend or group role); otherwise grant Play only. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration)
- If running a multi-developer game, move it into a group, set the group as package creator, and model profit splits before launch. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio)
- If two people might edit the same place, use previousDraftHash on publishes and avoid simultaneous edits from multiple devices. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/guide)
- If applying to the Incubator, check that the team is experienced and can commit to significant development time; do not present a solo beginner as the target. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/incubator)
- If citing budget figures, label them as self-reported unless they come from official documentation.
- If a contractor is paid by hour, track time and pay on a fixed monthly date; do not pay on royalty or per-assignment estimates. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475)
- If a candidate asks for a percentage deal, check the owner's track record (e.g. 2-3 front-page games) and require an upfront payment and a signed contract first. [indie, 2024-12] -- [VuukStudios 2024-12 10:04](https://youtu.be/xBPJnMgLoUE?t=604)
- If the group is used for payouts, add collaborators at least 14 days before the first payout and keep percentages at or below 100%. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 4:18](https://youtu.be/XzxoIlpBnJA?t=258)
- If a collaborator is listed as verified, still check history and identity; do not rely on the badge. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Raul 2026-09 1:38](https://youtu.be/GLmlPsYLsbM?t=98)
- If a commission offer is very low (e.g. 5K Robux for a full game), decline and wait for better posts. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Raul 2026-08 3:01](https://youtu.be/NE8OWWbAia8?t=181)
- If preparing a team transfer, verify email, move private assets, and set package ownership to the group before submitting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer)
- If the submitter is a team or group, confirm they are over 18. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/brand-developer-directory)
- If recruiting several developers in parallel, plan for at least one dropout. [indie, 2025-09] -- [Erthlord 2025-09 1:06](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=66)
- If a QA program needs testers, decide structure first, then advertise publicly and scale pay by revenue and bug volume. [devforum, 2023-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-effectively-qa-testing-your-roblox-experience/2199838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the team is visibly overloaded, hire for the specific weak area rather than generically. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475)
- If the project scope is small and the developer is solo, a solo build is a valid path; if the scope is large, plan team hires before progress stalls. [indie, 2026-08] -- [studio rook. 2026-08 0:35](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=35)
- If an artist asks for a revenue share instead of upfront pay, require a signed contract and modelled split math first, and flag that this conflicts with the older upfront-pay rule. [indie, 2024-12] -- [VuukStudios 2024-12 10:04](https://youtu.be/xBPJnMgLoUE?t=604)
- If a cheap commission (about $50-$120) is proposed, treat the delivery as a playable prototype and budget separately for further development. [indie, 2024-10] -- [BadGameDev 2024-10 5:44](https://youtu.be/eWkVkbbH9xs?t=344) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If asked about Team Create group-role editing, verify current official docs and Studio behaviour; do not rely on N6194. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Emancyphur 2021-07 0:28](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=28)
- If asked about DataStore limits, verify current official documentation; do not rely on Q1480.
- If choosing leaders, select by personality, maturity and trustworthiness and publish a promotion path. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 14:55](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=895)
- If the community is large and logistics are eating dev time, hire an events or community person. [indie, 2019-09] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-09 14:18](https://youtu.be/hnCm955Du8w?t=858)
- If a team shows mixed experience and repeated rework, rebalance roles or tighten vetting before scaling. [indie, 2020-03] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-03 7:57](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=477)
- If community-made maps or content are proposed, require closed testing before inclusion. [indie, 2021-12] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-12 5:22](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=322)
- Treat [low-advice] items (T16126, N6345-N6348) as lower-confidence and cross-check before recommending. [indie, 2024-10] -- [BadGameDev 2024-10 5:44](https://youtu.be/eWkVkbbH9xs?t=344) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user asks for a rough dev budget for a simple obby or single-loop sim, cite the $5,000-$15,000 agency band (N7232) and state it is unverified.
- If the scope includes a full economy, game passes, saves, or an update pipeline, use the $25,000-$75,000 band (N7233) as the reference.
- If the scope is large multiplayer with anti-cheat or LiveOps, state the ~$100,000 floor (N7234) and flag that it is an agency estimate.
- If a user wants to keep an original creator in the revenue split, prompt them to define the update-idea role and percentage in writing before launch. [indie, 2025-08] -- [GamesBeat 2025-08](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182)
- Do not present agency cost bands as market averages; label them as indicative and self-reported.
- If a co-op or multiplayer feature has no available testers, a bot or second test account can provide the second player for early checks. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 8:10](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=490)
- If testing with a bot, do not treat its results as proof of real player behavior. Schedule human playtests before release. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 8:10](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=490)
- If the user is hiring for a first role, first check whether the role can be covered by the owner or outsourced for a trial period.
- If a referral is proposed, still route the candidate through the standard interview pipeline rather than skipping steps. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 17:03](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=1023)
- If a candidate is from a large studio, verify specific contributions and use back-channel references before offering. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 24:53](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=1493)
- If runway is short, propose a fractional or one-month paid trial instead of a full-time commitment. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 33:55](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=2035)
- If a hire is proposed, plan at least two weeks of onboarding before judging fit. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 40:35](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=2435)
- If the user is working solo, advise disabling Team Create until a collaborator joins. [indie, 2026-04] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-04 4:47](https://youtu.be/XBi5o4JJNsw?t=287)
- Treat speaker-reported churn and process figures as anecdotal, not benchmarks.
- If a user asks about commission pricing, cite N7991 and N7992 as self-reported speaker estimates, not market data.
- If a user asks about outside investment, cite N7866 as a 2020 example and warn that current terms may differ.
- If a user asks how to get paid for services in USD, point to T18587 and recommend confirming each processor's fees and availability in the user's country.
- Do not treat the Robux-to-USD conversion as fixed; flag it as approximate.
- If a project has not yet passed soft-launch validation, do not hire a full marketing team; hire at most one internal marketer or use a trusted partner. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 35:44](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2144)
- If a soft-launch test has not reached a few thousand users through the funnel, treat the project as unvalidated and do not add headcount. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 35:44](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2144)
- If a data or dashboard task repeats across games, automate it with tooling before hiring for it. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 31:37](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1897)
- If creative output is the bottleneck, use AI for concepts and asset drafts, but keep final creative direction with a human. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 34:23](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2063)
- If scaling beyond the initial team, run an explicit buy-vs-build analysis before hiring. [indie, 2024-10] -- [GameMakers 2024-10 39:09](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=2349)
- If a required skill is missing (e.g. pathfinding, animation), source it early through a named community such as Hidden Devs. [indie, 2025-09] -- [janisjanis01 2025-09 1:45](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=105)
- When a bottleneck role blocks validated work, hiring early is acceptable; otherwise keep the team lean before validation. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 14:49](https://youtu.be/joeIJwwNCEw?t=889)

## testing-debugging-and-qa (coverage: covered)

- If a playtest needs an avatar or interaction, then use Test or Test Here, not Run. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/test-and-save)
- If the feature is multiplayer, friend-, SLIM- or voice-dependent, then test with 2 clients (3 Studio instances) or with friends or alts before shipping; a solo test is insufficient. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/testing-modes)
- If an 'attempt to index nil' error appears, then add WaitForChild before the access; if an event does nothing, then print inside the handler and check name capitalisation. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/selling-items)
- If a bug only appears mid-run, then use breakpoints (log-message or conditional) instead of editing code between runs; use Reload Plugin, not Save, while stepping. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Crusherfire 2024-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/vj55DK8aH3s?t=0)
- If replication, ownership or latency looks wrong, then use the Client/Server toggle, Network owners visualisation, and Network Simulator one condition at a time with a zero-value baseline. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/device-simulator)
- If DataStores fail in Studio, then publish first, enable Studio Access to API Services, and check Output for save errors; for lag, use the 2021 approach of a published build. - If an HTTP call returns 429, then retry with backoff; if 500 or 503, then treat as transient. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/errors)
- If the target is mobile, then test on at least one low-end Android device and run a 10-15 minute thermal session. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/test-on-hardware)

- If a loop or frequently hit line needs inspection, set a conditional breakpoint or logpoint before adding prints.
- If the error is 'attempt to index nil', check name spelling and case, missing returns, and WaitForChild before changing logic. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/intro-to-module-scripts)

- If measuring performance, use Manual graphics quality 10 with Performance Stats On and report device results separately from Studio numbers. - If testing race conditions or desync, add 0.5-1 s lag; for everyday baseline, use 50-150 ms. [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 0:52](https://youtu.be/OyPgQgBSIe4?t=52)
- If profiling, remove all breakpoints first, because they disable native execution. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/native-code-gen)
- If a shared ModuleScript fires breakpoints in the wrong context, set Trigger At to client, server or edit. [indie, 2023-12] -- [ByteBlox 2023-12 14:58](https://youtu.be/9YwYDuIuih4?t=898)
- If the code was agent-edited, playtest it and check Output before adding more changes. [indie, 2026-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 6:46](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=406)
- If a bug cannot be reproduced, record exact steps, try tiny variations, then fix; reproduce before editing. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 6:11](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=371)
- If debugging is taking a long time, treat it as the core skill rather than a sign of failure; budget more time for fixing than for writing.

- If a feature is broken and the cause is unclear, split it into sub-problems and search each one. [indie, 2022-10] -- [Emancyphur 2022-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/SdCmo91rsVc?t=0)
- Before publishing, read the draft/publish reminder and remove any :Mock() or test-only tracking. [indie, 2021-07] -- [Emancyphur 2021-07 3:51](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=231)

- If you need playtesting realism, prefer real testers over AI bots; use bots only for overload tests. [indie, 2020-02] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 27:52](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=1672)

## thumbnails-icons (coverage: covered)

- If a user's game has low CTR/PTR vs genre peers, then redesign thumbnails first and run multiple variants in an ad test. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Tizzy 2026-06 9:25](https://youtu.be/pu2o80prNV8?t=565)
- If designing a thumbnail, then use one big focal subject, high contrast, large text, and preview at phone size. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2024-09 22:23](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1343)
- If asked how many thumbnails, then give 3–5 for personalization and 10+ for ad tests, noting current Ads Manager caps should be verified. [indie, 2025-11] -- [VuukStudios 2025-11 7:45](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=465)
- If testing, then change one element, equal budget and duration, and promote the winner. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MoseyTube 2026-10 4:22](https://youtu.be/tao925_ypVg?t=262)
- If a thumbnail wins now, then keep rotating new candidates and refresh when QPTR declines. [mixed, 2026-06] -- [Roblox Creators 2024-11 6:19](https://youtu.be/I65eJ_uUsY8?t=379)
- If asked about clickbait, then advise against it citing QPTR and filtering.
- If shipping an update, then refresh icon/thumbnail with a variation and marker, max once a week, revert if dip. [official, 2024-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2024-11 7:15](https://youtu.be/I65eJ_uUsY8?t=435)
- If benchmarking CTR, then use <1.5% poor, 2.5% average, 3.5%+ great, labeled as indie self-reports. [indie, 2026-10] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-10 3:31](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=211)

- If a personalized game has fewer than 2 active thumbnails, add until there are 2–5 active; never run with one. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)
- If essential text or a logo sits in the bottom edge, move it up out of the player-count area. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)
- If the game icon is default auto-generated or ambiguous, replace it with a custom 512×512 square that shows theme and genre. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/experience-icons)
- If the footage contains voiceover, narration, lyric music, promo text, cinematic trailer styling or over-enhanced visuals, rebuild the video from real in-game capture. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)
- If three video thumbnail uploads have been used this month, defer further uploads until the quota resets. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/thumbnails)
- If a campaign creative underperforms its peers, disable it and test a replacement; keep the campaign at up to 10 creatives. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
