- Treat tool names and scores from third-party icon graders as unverified. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 2:01](https://youtu.be/fZJEDcibS_M?t=121)
- Label all figures from indie creators as self-reported; the only official-source evidence in this set is absent. [indie, 2026-11] -- [Roblox public API 2026-11](https://www.roblox.com/games/95168737547508/)
- When advice conflicts, say which source is newer (2026-07 rasdev over older rules) and name the conflicting creator. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 2:01](https://youtu.be/fZJEDcibS_M?t=121)
- Recommend a single clear focal point and capped effects (glow sparingly) when reviewing a thumbnail draft. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox 2024-09 22:23](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1343)
- Do not mention the user's email or account details in thumbnail or icon outputs. [indie, 2024-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-03 9:27](https://youtu.be/zMH1q2IzFYA?t=567)
- If a thumbnail or icon test is planned, compare against the existing ad creative baseline and record both CTRs. [indie, 2025-07] -- [janisjanis01 2025-07 16:47](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=1007) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If an event or update ships, schedule an icon refresh in the same release and keep the change recognisable. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 7:25](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=445)
- If a thumbnail wins on CTR, check retention and QPTR before scaling spend. [indie, 2025-09] -- [janisjanis01 2025-09 5:34](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=334)
- Treat numeric claims from single indie creators as self-reported and verify with your own A/B tests. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- Do not present the 9-in-10 icon win claim as a fact. [indie, 2026-06] -- [rasdev 2026-06 2:43](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=163)

## titles-descriptions (coverage: covered)

- If drafting a title, make it literal, genre-clear, ≤ a few words, third-grade reading level, with no repeated words or money lead. [mixed, 2026-05] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-12 12:17](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=737)
- If a title has more than 2 emojis or special characters, remove them. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places)
- If writing a description, put genre, unique feature, objective and primary keywords in the first ~160 characters, and keep total under 1000. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/descriptions)
- If the description contains hashtags, control lists, long FAQs or changelogs, remove or move them to tutorial/DevForum. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/descriptions)
- If choosing keywords, check Creator Xchange visits and competition, then test candidate names before committing. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Tizzy 2026-05 5:20](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=320)
- If the thumbnail or genre tags do not match gameplay, fix them before changing the title. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq)
- If a trend is saturated with clones, change title and thumbnail but keep the loop. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Tizzy 2026-06 1:54](https://youtu.be/dUmh6GAvJ-E?t=114)
- If the user wants to rename, warn about lost discoverability and prefer rare, deliberate renames.
- If targeting multiple markets, localize title, description and thumbnail text for top countries. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/demographics)
- If sources conflict on mechanics, prefer Roblox Creator Docs over indie claims. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 50:29](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=3029)
- If a title is a generic verb-noun phrase, check top charts for near-duplicates before committing and differentiate the noun or setting. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 4:33](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=273)
- If the title is hard to understand in about half a second, rewrite it as plain verb-noun or objective-plus-setting, not a clever name. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 4:45](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=285)
- If the genre has no search-friendly terms, use a hook title only when the gameplay clearly delivers on the hook. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 3:44](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=224)
- If you add a freshness bracket, use one tag ([NEW] or [BETA]) and remove it when the update is stale. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 5:25](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=325)
- If the game is based on an IP, use the official IP name as the primary keyword and secondary keywords for characters, places and objects. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ip-licensing/glossary)
- If the title or description changes, make sure the gameplay, thumbnail and icon still match it. [official, 2018-07] -- [Roblox 2018-07 24:16](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1456)
- If the description is written short, still lead with the hook and cover the core loop; stay under 1000 characters. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 3:07](https://youtu.be/tErJHe9wQn0?t=187)
- If a name or description is rejected by the text filter or review, fix the flagged wording before resubmitting. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace)
- Treat single-source claims (short descriptions, hashtags, reskinning) as tests to verify, not established rules. [indie, 2021-11] -- [GameMakers 2021-11 4:49](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=289)
- If a game is being transferred to a new owner, keep the title identical and do not rename it for search reasons. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer)
- If writing a game title, keep the existing emoji limit; use emojis only in the description, and only when they match the gameplay. [indie, 2026-06] -- [VuukStudios 2026-06 12:01](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=721)
- If drafting a description, use keyword-style verb statements built from the core loop. [indie, 2026-06] -- [VuukStudios 2026-06 12:01](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=721)
- If writing a short-form video caption for a game, include the exact game name. [indie, 2026-01] -- [Erthlord 2026-01 2:14](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=134)
- Treat the comparison promo tip (T13760) as low-advice; offer it as an option, not a default. [indie, 2026-08] -- [Raul 2026-08 4:16](https://youtu.be/VQHtqIE31Pk?t=256)
- Skip items about commission portfolios (T12794) and rewriting for brevity (T12071), since they repeat or fall outside game metadata.
- If a proposed title or description matches a common copied template in the genre, rewrite it with a distinctive angle while keeping the genre keywords. [indie, 2024-07] -- [TheDevBacon 2024-07 2:02](https://youtu.be/_XJVDRr2ZuA?t=122)
- If the group or brand has no clear niche, pick one theme and apply it consistently to titles, descriptions and visuals before publishing. [indie, 2025-12] -- [#7Phantom 2025-12 0:41](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=41)
- If the description promises a feature that only exists on an external wiki, add that information in-game or remove the claim. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Cumin 2025-11 6:42](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=402)
- Treat single-creator, self-reported advice as a hypothesis to test, not a platform rule; verify with your own A/B tests where possible. [indie, 2021-11] -- [GameMakers 2021-11 4:49](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=289)
- If a developer is choosing or finalizing a game name, recommend a name check before committing (T17623). [indie, 2026-06] -- [rasdev 2026-06 5:28](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=328)
- If a developer is publishing an update, suggest adding promotional text alongside the existing freshness tag (T17623).
- Do not treat promotional text as a replacement for keeping the title consistent; the title should stay unchanged across updates. [official, 2021-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-11 1:55](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=115)
- Cite T17623 only for the name-check and promo-text points; do not extrapolate exact limits or mechanics from it.
- If the game is in a saturated genre such as space, do not rely on the genre word alone. Add a specific fantasy or setting to the title and check keyword competition before committing (Q1856, existing rule on keyword research). [indie, 2025-02] -- [GnomeCode 2025-02 8:51](https://youtu.be/-oZIdFe8qHk?t=531)
- If the niche is crowded and the icon is strong, you may test a distinctive or odd but memorable name against a literal one; otherwise keep the literal default. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 3:24](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=204)
- If the audience (kids, teens, etc.) is undefined, define it before writing the title or marketing posts. [indie, 2023-02] -- [SmartyRBX 2023-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/Wh2B4cQn4Zs?t=0)
- Always check that a distinctive name is still readable and pairs with the icon; drop it if it confuses players. [indie, 2026-07] -- [rasdev 2026-07 3:24](https://youtu.be/9_cHgdOo7i0?t=204)
- Treat T18696 as the newest guidance when it applies to naming with icons; keep metadata accurate.

## trailers-and-video-marketing (coverage: covered)

- If asked to make a trailer for the Roblox game page, then use only real current gameplay, no voiceover, narration, promo text, lyrics music or cinematic scenes. [indie+devforum, 2026-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gameplay-videos-on-home-help-your-games-get-discovered/4842601)
- If the user has a YouTube-embedded game video, then tell them embeds were removed September 30, 2026 and to upload a file via Creator Hub > Configure > Places > Videos. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4880336)
- If making an external trailer, then use 60-90s, a 5-10s action hook, features in 15-20s, title card with CTA, and on-beat music. [indie, 2026-09] -- [rasdev 2026-09 10:07](https://youtu.be/IyEqyPOjEvU?t=607)
- If the game is weak or unfinished, then advise improving the game before heavy trailer investment, though devlogs and rough clips are fine for early awareness. [indie+devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838)
- If planning social content, then recommend TikTok/Shorts, a first-seconds hook, and a consistent cadence; track videos over 1,000 views and repeat the common element. [indie, 2025-12] -- [#7Phantom 2025-12 0:59](https://youtu.be/peTkfSG79rM?t=59)
- If the user needs cinematic screenshots or footage, then give the freecam shortcuts and settings (hidden UI, FOV, roll, Stiffness, post-processing, Player Lock). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera/free-camera)
- If quoting indie view or subscriber figures, then label them self-reported and unverified. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If measuring creator or video campaigns, then use at least a 7-day click window. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 15:05](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=905)
- If the game has a notable update, then submit a Today's Picks nomination with a trailer and core-mechanic footage. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/standout-games)
- If the audience is mainly non-English, then make content in that language. [indie, 2026-10] -- [VuukStudios 2026-10 7:17](https://youtu.be/0ENzqNB_EUo?t=437)
- When advising on Moments, prefer the official Roblox Investor Relations update (T17551) over indie claims, and say which source is official. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If a creator asks how to get into Moments, cover the Experience Details Page route (T17534) and the preview and clip quality rules before any platform-specific tactics. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- Do not invent retention percentages. The only Moments retention evidence is qualitative.
- When a creator's clip is for Moments, check that the first seconds show the game itself, not only creator commentary. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- When writing promo copy for YouTube, X, or Discord, include the game's Details Page URL so viewers can play directly (T17662). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- When advising on Moments, state that the feature is announced and verify rollout status first (T17681).
- When recommending Details Page or Moments clips, lead with gameplay because Moments clicks go to play (T17681). [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Newsroom 2026-09](https://about.roblox.com/newsroom/2026/09/rdc-2026-the-world-needs-more-play)
- If the task is launch promo timing, then schedule creator videos 1-2 weeks before launch and reserve full promo tempo for the weeks near release (T17806). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 20:20](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1220)
- If the task is paid Meta or Google ad creative, then use polished cinematics and looping motion graphics rather than lo-fi footage (T17951). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 14:55](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=895)
- If the task is TikTok-style organic short form, then favor lo-fi developer diaries and organic storytelling over polished ad styling (T17951, Q1839). [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 14:55](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=895)
- If an existing rule says to post short-form early and a source says to ramp full promo near release, then keep early organic short form for acquisition and save paid or full-tempo promo for near launch (T17806). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Matt The Method 2026-08 3:17](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=197)
- If you cite the 15202-view figure (N7715), then state that it is self-reported and that the video and timeframe are unknown.
- If you report indie figures from these sources, then label them self-reported and low-advice where tagged (T17951, Q1839). [indie, 2026-05] -- [Cumin 2026-05 13:09](https://youtu.be/tT-u3b6LMkQ?t=789)
- If a user asks how long to make Shorts from long videos, suggest about 10-20 seconds with the hook in the first two seconds (T18608, self-reported). [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If a user is a beginner who hesitates to post dev content, encourage starting now and note they only need to be a few steps ahead of viewers (T18580, Q1900). [indie, 2025-02] -- [SmartyRBX 2025-02 4:46](https://youtu.be/Da83Ft52DLQ?t=286)
- If a user is building brand assets, recommend a durable style guide covering logos, lockup, and character art (T18680). [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 13:42](https://youtu.be/cYTtcwp7FS8?t=822)
- Label all of these items as indie, self-reported advice, not platform ground truth. [indie, 2024-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2024-04 2:39](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=159)
- Do not treat the 10-20 second length as an official platform rule. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If a developer asks how to build hype before launch, suggest a recurring YouTube build-series in addition to short-form, and label the traffic claim as indie self-reported. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 0:31](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=31)
- Do not treat a single devlog as a series; advise a regular, numbered cadence if the developer wants the hype effect. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 0:31](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=31)

## trend-research-and-validation (coverage: covered)

- If asked for a game idea, first check Up and Coming/Trending/breakout charts and a tracker, then name 3 candidates with CCU trajectory. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 1:04](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=64)
- If a candidate is already at peak or on the front page for weeks, don't clone it; look for a smaller growing game. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 1:04](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=64)
- If a keyword search shows 0-2 games, treat the niche as open but verify off-platform demand. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Tizzy 2026-06 1:42](https://youtu.be/4ms4cp2mrtM?t=102)
- If only one dominant game exists in a high-interest fantasy, recommend a twist on it. [indie, 2026-10] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-10 2:45](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=165)
- If a trend's origin is TikTok or a meme, verify it is still rising and ship fast, or skip.
- If the competitor has low like ratio, many bug complaints, stale updates or weak monetisation, recommend a better version. [indie, 2026-06] -- [VuukStudios 2026-06 12:01](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=721)
- If proposing an idea, state your differentiator and confirm the twist isn't already tried.
- If recommending numbers, label indie CCU, visit and revenue claims as unverified and prefer official stats for mechanics.
- If the user wants a launch plan, include an initial traffic source (ads or short-form content) since no organic lift exists at zero visits.
- If the user is buying a game or template, check peak vs average CCU and duplicates. [indie, 2026-09] -- [janisjanis01 2026-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=0)
- If a tracker shows a high breakout score but flat velocity metrics, treat the trend as unconfirmed and keep validating (N7559). [indie, 2026-10] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-10](https://rowatcher.com/games/9363735110/escape-tsunami-for-brainrots)
- If a game's growth roughly matches platform-wide growth (visits up 55% YoY, daily users up 10% YoY), do not conclude it is a breakout; look for game-specific signals (N7526, N7527). [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If new games already hold a large share of the charts in your target genre, treat the chart as open to newcomers, but still validate demand first (N7529).
- Always cite the date of platform benchmarks used in a decision.
- If a tracker shows a title declining but its mechanic appearing in new titles, research the new titles rather than dropping the mechanic. [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 0:02](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=2)
- If a revenue figure comes from a single indie source, flag it as unverified and recommend checking it directly. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 10:15](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=615)
- If a genre-share figure is used for a decision, label it as self-reported and check it against platform or tracker data. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- If a genre has two or three dominant titles, do not enter head-on; look for a twist, sub-niche, or unmet player want first. [indie, 2022-11] -- [GameMakers 2022-11 22:18](https://youtu.be/NaR3-VIg5-I?t=1338)
- If a genre's ad trend has been observed for less than 90 days, collect more data before acting. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 21:12](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1272) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Treat impressions per ad as a quality proxy, not a direct demand measure. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 22:50](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1370) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If you cannot state the market void, unmet want, and update objective, stop before building. [indie, 2024-03] -- [GameMakers 2024-03 2:11](https://youtu.be/zvNQHk1hUYc?t=131)
- If genre ad CTR is low or falling, lower the priority of that genre and check saturation. [indie, 2020-05] -- [TheDevKing 2020-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=617) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- When choosing a genre or mechanic, start from front page, charts or Reblocks discover page, but avoid cloning a specific hit title. [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 1:04](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=64)
- Do not apply mobile UA market figures to Roblox decisions without Roblox-specific data.
- Treat host-claimed figures (e.g., Liftoff reach) as unverified; do not use them as benchmarks. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=60)
- Use a reference game's player count only as a rough benchmark, not as a forecast for your own game. [indie, 2021-06] -- [GameMakers 2021-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/c-LQvmtT-Co?t=60)
- Save ads from a clean browsing setup so the research reflects competitor creatives, not your own ad-feed bias. [indie, 2022-03] -- [GameMakers 2022-03 24:53](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1493) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a trend is clearly short-lived (meme- or plugin-driven), propose a time-boxed throwaway build instead of a full production effort. [indie, 2024-10] -- [janisjanis01 2024-10 2:50](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=170)
- If ad CTR for a creative is near 2%, test a replacement creative aiming for ~3% before scaling spend. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 13:58](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=838) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- Treat single-creator figures such as the 8M CCU peak as outlier context, not as expected outcomes for a new game.

## ugc-and-avatar-economy (coverage: covered)

- If a user wants to sell a Marketplace item, then first confirm Plus/Premium (layered or rigid), the publishing advance, and the upload fee; do not assume free listing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating)
- If a user asks about Limited items, then state: 1 Limited publish per day, no cap on non-Limited; only Limited can be resold or sold free; 100 Robux per unit for free Limited; paid max 3000. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- If a user wants in-experience avatar sales, then require a Creation Token bought with Robux first, match creation type, enable third-party sales, and note items are not resellable or listed on the Marketplace. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation)
- If a user uploads an avatar accessory or body, then route through model, fitting, Avatar Setup, validate, publish; never delete cage vertices. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/tutorials)
- If a user asks about emotes or animation packs, then state 80 Robux fee; packs are one per day, original work only, and all seven state groups must validate. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/animation-packs)
- If a user cites indie revenue or CCU figures (rasdev, Erthlord, janisjanis01, bubDEV), then label them self-reported and unverified; never present them as platform facts. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If an official statement and an indie claim conflict on mechanics, then prefer the official docs (2026-10). [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)
- If a user asks about Today's Picks, then check age 17+, ID verification, and Community Standards; note weekly review and reapplication. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating)
- If a user is about to insert a free model, then inspect for hidden scripts and remotes first and heed the insert-with-scripts prompt. [devforum, 2022-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-exploits-work-and-how-to-combat-them/1627229)
- If a user asks about PromptCreateAvatarAsync or other alpha features, then note alpha status and advise checking the DevForum before building. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/avatar-generation)
- If the user plans to sell an animation pack, emote, or Avatar Background, first confirm pilot eligibility or Roblox enablement; otherwise plan for listing and testing only. [devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-animation-packs-and-emotes-on-marketplace/4684642)
- If an accessory or layered clothing model lacks inner and outer cage meshes, stop and fix the cages before recommending a Marketplace upload. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/automatic-skinning-transfer)
- If user code filters AssetType 'Face' or 'Head', rewrite to GetHeadShapesAsync, BodyPartDescription.HeadShape, and Roblox-creator Dynamic Head searches. [devforum, 2026-01] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/completing-the-dynamic-head-migration/4301387)
- If the user asks for cheap high-volume emotes or accessories based on indie advice, state the official spec requirements first and label revenue claims as unverified.
- If an item is taken down, explain that buyers are refunded and point the user to the Appeals Portal. [devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pilot-create-and-sell-animation-packs-and-emotes-on-marketplace/4684642)
- Before recommending saved outfits or inventory-based features, add PlayerOwnsAsset and PromptAllowInventoryReadAccess checks. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/avatar-editor)
- If the user archives development items, warn that archived assets disappear from their own games too. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/frequently-asked-questions)
- Quote fee and revenue-share numbers only with their date; 2021 values are historical unless the current fees page confirms them.
- For pilot deadlines (June 29 and August 13, 2026), state that they have passed as of 2026-10-29.
- If the user relies on avatar items to reach the 18+ rate, correct them: avatar items do not count toward it. [devforum, 2026-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091)
- If asked about revenue share on items sold inside other games, give the 2026 30/40/30 split first, note it is indie-reported, and mention the older 2022 30% figure as superseded.
- If asked about 2019-era splits such as 70/30, label them as unconfirmed speculation, not platform fact.
- If planning a Limited drop, say it is reported as the biggest earner but only recommend announcing it if you can deliver as stated.
- If a user wants to earn commission from UGC they did not make, recommend displaying items in a game with in-game purchase prompts, and warn that the figures are self-reported. [indie, 2022-07] -- [Mulch 2022-07 2:06](https://youtu.be/An_5Mu0aR5I?t=126) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a user is publishing an emote, check the length (10 s), the body part distance (5 studs), the validator status ('valid'), and the tags before submission. [indie, 2024-11] -- [Suphi Kaner 2024-11 2:07:55](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=7675)
- If a user is publishing an accessory, confirm the Avatar tab path, Hat type upload, face accessory category, and bundle requirements. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/emotes)
- If a user cites a fee, ask which step it applies to (animation upload, item publish, or submit) and note that reports conflict.
- Always attribute Robux figures to their source creator and mark them as self-reported; do not present them as platform data. [indie, 2025-12] -- [{EISABOOS} "RBX" 2025-12 4:12](https://youtu.be/fy3Z0P9mG3Y?t=252)
- If a user asks about a copy or knockoff threat, refer to the existing copy/takedown guidance rather than assuming a new policy.
- When a figure conflicts with an official source, defer to the official source and flag the discrepancy.
- When advising on scarcity, separate two cases: releasing new items regularly (existing rule) and re-releasing existing scarce items (avoid frequent re-releases, T17481). [indie, 2026-05] -- [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-05](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-may-2026/)
- When a progression design pairs a grind with a Robux speed boost, flag the scrutiny risk (T17528) and label the advice as indie and self-reported. [indie, 2026-08] -- [eGamers 2026-08](https://egamers.io/robloxs-no-1-game-steal-an-egg-was-pulled-hours-after-reaching-the-top/)
- If a feature is described as announced or upcoming, state that it is unverified and check its availability before advising a developer to depend on it.
- Treat indie-reported prices and community sizes as examples only; do not present them as platform benchmarks. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78773-roblox-animal-hospital-hits-1.2-million-ccu-with-major-update-featuring-new-character-and-items)
- When advising on borrowed code, flag decompiled or copied sources as an IP and trust risk, not just a quality issue. [indie, 2026-08] -- [AllThingsHow (guide) 2026-08](https://allthings.how/steal-an-egg-roblox-ban-why-it-happened-and-how-it-returned/)
- If a user asks whether uploaded UGC earns passively, say that this is one indie creator's unverified claim and that earnings may decline without new releases. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/WIkCweVdd88?t=0)
- If budgeting a Marketplace listing, use about 1,000 to 1,500 Robux only as a self-reported estimate and tell the user to confirm the current fee. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 6:17](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=377)
- If a user asks to convert Robux earnings to USD, state that the ~$0.0053-per-Robux rate is derived from one creator's figures and is not a platform rate.
- If a user asks about the cost of a profile-only upload versus a Marketplace listing, present the self-reported figures (about 80 vs about 1,000 to 1,500 Robux) with the date and source caveat. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Xaiyne 2026-10 6:17](https://youtu.be/vGfWK6vbuYk?t=377)
- Do not treat per-item daily earnings examples (N7687) as benchmarks for a new item; they are approximate screen readings from a single creator.
- If the task is paid promotion of UGC, first ask whether the content is commissioned from creators (T18661), since paid amplification applies to that case and not to creator-channel influencer posts. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 36:20](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2180)
- Treat both new items as indie, self-reported advice. Do not present them as platform policy or as proven results. [indie, 2025-08] -- [Erthlord 2025-08 2:41](https://youtu.be/ylBgNBMiyW4?t=161)

## ui-ux-design (coverage: covered)

- If you lay out a new UI, then use Scale for size and position, add UIAspectRatioConstraint or RelativeYY for aspect, and cap pixel size with UISizeConstraint. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers)
- If an interactive ScreenGui is placed, then keep it on CoreUISafeInsets. Use ScreenInsets None only for purely decorative backgrounds. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/on-screen-containers)
- If a control must be tappable on mobile, then keep it in thumb-reachable zones clear of the thumbstick and jump areas, and verify on a real phone, including TextBox focus and the on-screen keyboard. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/user-interface-design/wireframe-your-layouts)
- If a layout uses UIListLayout, UIGridLayout or UITableLayout, then never set Position or Size on the layout's siblings; use LayoutOrder and keep them as siblings. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/grid-table-layouts)
- If a constraint and a UIListLayout target the same object, then expect the constraint to override the layout sizing; avoid combining them unintentionally. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers)
- If text length is unknown or localised, then use AutomaticSize (and TextWrapped where wrapping is needed) rather than a fixed size, and test the longest language. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers)
- If text must scale, then use TextScaled inside a Frame, keep MinTextSize at 9 or higher, and test on the lowest-supported device. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/size-modifiers)
- If a SurfaceGui must be interactive, then place it in StarterGui with Adornee set to the target part; otherwise place a non-interactive SurfaceGui directly on the part. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/user-interface)
- If a menu or modal opens, then give it a close button or back path, a DisplayOrder above the HUD, and hide the HUD while a Menu is open. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui)
- If you tween UI, then reuse tweens per state, cancel running tweens first, avoid tweening UIStroke.Thickness on text, and fade whole groups with a CanvasGroup. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation)
- If a layout must work on phones, read UserInputService.PreferredInput (not TouchEnabled) and listen for its change signal to swap layouts. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/input)
- If a button is added, connect Activated in a LocalScript under that button; avoid MouseButton1Click for new code. [indie, 2024-04] -- [ByteBlox 2024-04 20:46](https://youtu.be/cWfsm8rwgDg?t=1246)
- If placing custom mobile buttons, keep them about 60px from each side, out of bottom corners, and sized relative to JumpButton. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/position-and-size)
- If a frame or label is sized, use Scale; use Offset only for fixed padding, icon sizes or pixel details. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/animation)
- If a bar or cooldown updates, clamp the ratio to 0-1, tween changes, and disconnect previous Heartbeat connections on re-trigger. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2024-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543)
- If a ScreenGui should survive respawn, set ResetOnSpawn false; if it is a health HUD, leave it true. [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 47:17](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2837)
- If building a shop or list, clone a hidden template per item and order it with LayoutOrder. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/create-leaderboard)
- If a feature is locked or owned, hide or swap its buy button instead of leaving it live. [indie, 2025-09] -- [PineappleDev 2025-09 3:21](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=201)
- If the game needs accessibility, expose toggles for camera shake, bobbing, particles, brightness and text size, and respect reduced motion. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/styling/css-comparisons)
- If indie advice conflicts with newer official docs or 2026 properties, follow the official docs and say which source you chose. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager)
- If a consumable weapon needs manual reload, switch to auto-reload or a single button (T16187). [indie, 2025-06] -- [Cumin 2025-06 13:15](https://youtu.be/Z1tj1OPOEZg?t=795)
- If a feature is unfinished, show a Coming Soon sign instead of an NPC or error message (T16397). [indie, 2025-12] -- [Cumin 2025-12 28:30](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1710)
- If players report getting lost or not knowing what to do next, add labelled buttons and signage for the main flow (T16587, T16600, T16438). [indie, 2026-08] -- [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:45](https://youtu.be/LpqqLed1dBU?t=165)
- If a state change must update only one property, bind that property to its source instead of rebuilding the element (T16508). [indie, 2026-07] -- [Chase Higgins 2026-07 8:17](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=497)
- If a state change must trigger an effect, use bind.effect rather than click handlers (T16509). [indie, 2026-07] -- [Chase Higgins 2026-07 9:11](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=551)
- If building Fusion UI, keep it in client scripts to offload server work (T16295). [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 2:36](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=156)
- If a component has many variants, expose one configurable component with variant props and raw-or-state inputs (T16322, T16325). [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 5:00](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=300)
- If a UI scale issue appears, test across screen sizes before adding more layers (T16360). [indie, 2020-02] -- [Last Level Studios 2020-02 27:52](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=1672)
- If a HUD element must survive respawn, set ResetOnSpawn to false (T15820). [indie, 2020-08] -- [Emancyphur 2020-08 47:17](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=2837)
- If low-advice items conflict with existing rules, keep the existing rule and treat the new item as a supplement. [indie, 2026-00] -- [GosuGamers 2026-00](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78741-animal-hospital-becomes-roblox-s-latest-horror-sensation-here-s-everything-you-need-to-know)
- If a label needs legibility over a busy background and the current Studio build supports native text shadows, use the native shadow before adding strokes or duplicate labels. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If an effect uses a native gradient, blur or animated image container, check it on mobile and at low graphics settings before treating it as ready. [indie, 2026-09] -- [GamesBeat 2026-09](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-hits-20-and-opens-rdc-2026-to-invite-the-world-to-play-and-make-games-dave-baszucki-keynote/)
- If a game has several vendors or shop booths that players or managers visit often, propose one central hub with a clear sign, and cite the walking-time reduction as a self-reported indie claim. [indie, 2026-06] -- [GosuGamers 2026-06](https://www.gosugamers.net/entertainment/news/78614-grow-a-garden-2-has-launched-on-roblox-here-s-everything-new-added-in-this-sequel)
- If the 2D tools update is relevant to a UI task, verify the exact property names and Studio version in the official docs first, because the sources do not give them.
- If the target Studio or engine version is confirmed to include native UI blur, UI shadows, gradients or text shadows, use them over existing workarounds and note the change. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- If native support is not confirmed, keep existing workaround rules in place and do not assume the announced features exist. [indie, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm)
- Do not state that the Input Action Label or input action manager is available unless verified in current docs.
- When designing HUD hints, reference actions by name so they can be bound to platform-provided labels later. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208)
- Treat the chat-nudge figure as Roblox's own claim and not a guaranteed result for other games. [indie, 2024-11] -- [SmartyRBX 2024-11 4:02](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=242)
- If a task asks for a custom teleport or loading screen, use TeleportService:SetTeleportGui() on the client and build it as a ScreenGui with no scripts inside it. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If a custom loading-screen ScreenGui needs behaviour, place the script in a separate client script, not inside the ScreenGui. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport)
- If players must sell items one at a time and errors repeat, add sell-all and an inventory selection menu (T17809). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 12:09](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=729)
- If travel feels slow at the base walk speed, add a sprint control wired through existing input rules (T17810). [indie, 2026-08] -- [Cumin 2026-08 12:09](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=729)
- When asked to change UI with an AI assistant, specify the exact target file or frame and require a self-check (T17927). [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=284)
- Treat the 20 walk-speed figure as an example from one creator, not a platform default. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 5:50](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=350)
- If a HUD label or bar must reflect server state, connect a LocalScript to the replicated value's .Changed event instead of polling. [indie, 2025-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 40:47](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=2447)
- If placing an overhead tag, set StudsOffsetWorldSpace near 0,3,0 and enable AlwaysOnTop so parts don't hide it. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=0)
- If a progress fill frame is very small, skip UICorner to avoid artifacts. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 1:24](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=84)
- If a dialogue line uses a typewriter, block click-to-skip until typing finishes. [indie, 2025-01] -- [ByteBlox 2025-01 10:29](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=629)
- If a player clicks a TextBox, do not play the button click sound. [indie, 2025-03] -- [PineappleDev 2025-03 3:54](https://youtu.be/2z4rebjBnQA?t=234)
- If a ProximityPrompt's location is already unlocked, disable the prompt on the client. [indie, 2025-06] -- [ArTheDev 2025-06 20:03](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=1203)
- If InputBegan fires with gameProcessed true, return early and do not trigger game actions. [indie, 2025-09] -- [SimTek Game Development 2025-09 18:36](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1116)
- If a resource bar or buff appears, show the exact number next to it. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 8:01](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=481)
- If a level-up or encounter outcome happens, show a visible message and list new abilities or effects. [indie, 2026-06] -- [Cumin 2026-06 7:21](https://youtu.be/2IdxQNOGoqA?t=441)
- If you need to gate a touch-only button, prefer PreferredInput; use TouchEnabled only as a quick check and verify in Studio. For touch-only devices, set ScrollBarThickness to about 2% of the parent AbsoluteSize.X. [indie, 2026-04] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-04 10:15](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=615)
- If a request asks for a shop, build a toggled ScreenGui with product frames, and trigger purchase prompts from a button rather than automatically. [indie, 2019-12] -- [TheDevKing 2019-12 7:07](https://youtu.be/QC2bDZsFiz4?t=427)
- If a request asks for a simple UI, use only text buttons, text labels and frames styled with UICorner, UIGradient and UIStroke. [indie, 2026-03] -- [VuukStudios 2026-03 13:22](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=802)
- If a request names a genre, match the UI style to it: expressive for simulators, minimalistic for FPS. [indie, 2026-08] -- [janisjanis01 2026-08 4:42](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=282)
- If a request needs an icon, use an ImageLabel with AnchorPoint 0.5,0.5, scale sizing, a transparent background and a UIAspectRatioConstraint.
- If a source is from 2019 or 2020, check it against current engine and purchase APIs before reusing its code. [indie, 2020-03] -- [TheDevKing 2020-03 11:08](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=668)
- If a shop or inventory has many thin categories, merge them before adding more tabs. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 5:11](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=311)
- If a bulk-action list is needed and drag selection lags, use click and hover multi-select instead. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 8:45](https://youtu.be/yxd5GMEjtDA?t=525)
- If a container or egg icon hides its contents, show the item or pet on the icon. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 14:30](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=870)
- If a billboard notification blocks the view, remove it or move it to screen UI. [indie, 2024-11] -- [janisjanis01 2024-11 19:08](https://youtu.be/vJekPDaIT30?t=1148)
- If a tutorial arrow sits near notifications, test the overlap explicitly before release. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 11:54](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=714)
- If the game has a background track, provide a volume control as well as a mute button. [indie, 2025-06] -- [janisjanis01 2025-06 10:31](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=631)
- If a UI element displays a shared or replicated value, set its initial text or size on join from the current value, then keep it updated with .Changed. [indie, 2024-06] -- [AlvinBlox 2024-06 5:28](https://youtu.be/paRHxtuk_Mc?t=328)
- If a health bar is built, use a clamped current/max ratio for its size; treat 500 as a tutorial figure, not a default, and check MaxHealth in the project. [indie+devforum, 2025-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705)

## updates-live-ops (coverage: covered)

- If the game is new, then plan daily or near-daily patches in the first week or two, then weekly planned updates, then bi-weekly once stable. [indie, 2026-10] -- [kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:12](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=612)
- If the team is small or near burnout, then move to bi-weekly or monthly and keep a backlog; do not copy another game's schedule. [devforum, 2020-07] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805)
- If a big expansion is in development, then keep shipping cadence drops using existing systems so the base doesn't shrink. [official, 2021-07] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-07 34:33](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=2073)
- If adding a new mechanic, currency or system, then put it in a major update, not a cadence drop. [official, 2022-12] -- [Roblox Creators 2022-12 2:10](https://youtu.be/bEEd75mnBIg?t=130)
- If scheduling a release, then target Friday, cut dev off Wednesday night or Thursday midday, and announce the date ahead. [official, 2023-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 15:37](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=937)
- If hyping an update, then run a 1-2 week countdown, post small teasers 3-7 days before, and avoid teasing 6-8 months out. [official, 2021-11] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-11 31:29](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1889)
- If building a live event, then trigger it with DateTime at a fixed UTC time that fits your main audience, keep it long enough not to feel dead, and avoid server shutdowns. [mixed, 2021-12] -- [Roblox Creators 2021-12 8:49](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=529)
- If a holiday is coming, then release about a week early, put about 60% in week one, reuse past content, and favour internationally appealing themes. [official, 2023-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 6:54](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=414)
- If a feature threatens the economy, then remove or fix it despite objections; prefer buffing underused items over nerfs.
- If the team cannot ship at least monthly, flag the schedule risk and plan a recovery phase rather than going silent. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization)
- If an update needs more than three weeks of effort, split it or move it to the major-update tier (every 2-3 months). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies)
- If an announcement is needed sooner than three days after the last one, combine it with the pending announcement; keep text within 60 characters and notifications within 99. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events)
- If an event would exceed 10 published ongoing or upcoming events, end or retire one before publishing another. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion)
- If submitting for Off-Platform Featuring, submit at least 7 days ahead and do not edit after submission. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning)
- If the event goal is unclear, define the target KPI (e.g. DAU) before building anything. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/liveops-planning)
- If a metric is lagging, target the next update at that metric (e.g. D1 or D7 retention) and check it after release. [indie, 2026-05] -- [Alex Builds Games 2026-05 13:43](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=823)
- If an update severely hurts metrics, roll back first, then profile with Developer Console, Script Profiler and MicroProfiler; set a crash-rate alert and webhook before any release. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance)
- If a seasonal update is being rushed under deadline pressure, scale it down rather than ship a weak version. [indie, 2025-11] -- [DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 2:50](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=170)
- If a value needs tuning, prefer a config change over a republish, and test feature versions with experiments first. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/get-started)
- If the update changes the player data format, restart all outdated servers instead of relying on bleed-off (T9620). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/update-games)
- If the update is non-disruptive, keep the default migration and accept that versions may split for a while (T9619).
- For time-sensitive releases, deploy content hidden behind a config, then flip the value (T9621, T8936). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/update-games)
- Before publishing, test the update and monitor errors and crash rate in the first minutes; roll back severe regressions (T6415, T6627). [indie, 2026-07] -- [janisjanis01 2026-07 6:03](https://youtu.be/OL1FJ17wzZE?t=363)
- When planning the next update, use a pre-update follow notification to bring players back (T6431, N2713). [indie, 2026-10] -- [rasdev 2026-10 4:39](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=279)
- For events and rewards that need no code change, use the LiveOps dashboard rather than republishing (T7532). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging)
- For holiday or month-long offers, use a FixedTime bundle that ends at a set UTC time (T7813). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles)
- Deactivate leaderboards instead of deleting them to preserve data (T9006). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards)
- For UGC items, limit post-publish changes to fixes; do not redesign items after publishing (T9594). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/publish-to-marketplace)
- Verify tool names and defaults against current Creator Hub docs; treat 2019-2023 numbers as possibly outdated, and poll Open Cloud async operations until done is true (T8338). [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments)
- If a game has just launched, plan a 7-14 day window of daily feedback checks and fixes before judging the idea. [indie, 2026-08] -- [VuukStudios 2026-08 16:27](https://youtu.be/Tk0qXybUK0E?t=987)
- If a bug is severe, ship a same-day silent patch and add it to the changelog. [indie+devforum, 2026-08] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057)
- If a game shows traction (for example home recommendations), move to weekly or bi-weekly content updates. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 13:08](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=788)
- If a game shows no traction after the post-release window, flag it for a continue-or-abandon decision instead of forcing more updates. [indie, 2026-09] -- [VuukStudios 2026-09 13:08](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=788)
- If pursuing Kids/Select eligibility, track highly engaged players over 60 days and keep the game actively maintained. [devforum, 2026-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944)
- If an update depends on third-party audio, run Audio Discovery on the published place and start review early. [devforum, 2022-03] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If a season is planned, set startUtc and endUtc before launch. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes)
- If the team plans a 17+ conversion, warn that Team Create members will be removed. [devforum, 2023-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2023-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184)
- If a group transfer is completed, re-release the game publicly or it stays hidden. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/game-ownership-transfer)
- If a breaking change is planned, cap it at once per week and announce it a day ahead. [devforum, 2026-02] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904)
- If a game has been dormant, prioritise shipping an update to it before building a new game. [indie, 2019-10] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-10 3:16](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=196)
- If a restart is needed, use a soft-shutdown flow with a lobby and TeleportService and avoid hard shutdowns. [indie, 2019-10] -- [Last Level Studios 2019-10 18:45](https://youtu.be/vY0om5O-Oh8?t=1125)
- If a major update is planned, staff it with specialists rather than the existing core team. [indie, 2021-08] -- [Last Level Studios 2021-08 30:36](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=1836)
- If an old feature is being re-released, rebalance it and gather feedback first. [indie, 2024-09] -- [CORSO 2024-09 12:40](https://youtu.be/lFD1wKdwSUs?t=760)
- If a new player is churning, weigh onboarding fixes against new-content work. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Cumin 2025-11 6:55](https://youtu.be/cbOZKwPmmko?t=415)
- If the team is about to run ads, confirm fresh content, icon and thumbnail are ready first. [indie, 2026-08] -- [LovJak 2026-08 4:45](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=285)
- If the game receives harsh critique, ship a targeted update that addresses the specific complaints rather than a generic one. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 2:47](https://youtu.be/evyIBvylLdc?t=167)
- If a game's chart position is falling and it has not shipped content recently, recommend a content drop before adding more events.
- If a game is anime-tied, recommend timing a major update to a season launch, and label the claim as unverified. [indie, 2026-03] -- [RoWatcher (tracker blog) 2026-03](https://rowatcher.com/news/the-10-fastest-growing-roblox-games-in-march-2026)
- Treat indie figures (GGAID, RoWatcher, GamesBeat) as directional, not benchmarks.

- Do not present a long seasonal event as a fix for retention; pair it with a content drop. [official, 2023-08] -- [Roblox Creators 2023-08 3:57](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=237)

## version-control-and-tooling (coverage: covered)

- If the user asks about a new project, then recommend the 2026 official stack (Rokit, Rojo or Script Sync, Wally, Luau LSP plus companion, selene, StyLua) over the 2021 indie steps. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools)
- If the user wants only scripts in Git with the place kept in Studio, then recommend Script Sync. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync)
- If the user wants the whole project on disk as the source of truth, then recommend Rojo with default.project.json. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync)
- If the user collaborates in Studio without external tools, then recommend enabling Drafts Mode and using Compare With Server and Merge From Server before committing. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration)
- If the user installs Script Sync, then tell them to install both the Luau LSP VS Code extension and the Luau Language Server Companion plugin. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync)
- If the user asks about the place file in Git, then recommend .rbxlx with a .gitignore excluding .rbxlx, and mention the .rbxl binary alternative. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/coding-harness)
- If the user asks about Open Cloud automation, then recommend Open Cloud scripts with narrowly scoped keys and warn against legacy cookie-auth APIs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools)
- If the user asks about version pinning, then recommend Rokit with a rokit.toml, bumping one tool at a time, and downgrading if problems appear. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools)
- If the user asks about the 2021 Rojo one-way sync claim or port 34872, then label them as unverified indie claims and advise checking the current Rojo release. [indie, 2021-03] -- [B Ricey 2021-03 10:22](https://youtu.be/ulBM7UdHk_4?t=622)
- If the user asks about revenue, CCU or visit numbers, then say they are unverified indie self-reports (none appear in these items) and prefer official statements. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 40:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2432)
- If the user asks how to version a game, recommend Git plus commit and push before risky changes, and add Studio save notes for in-Studio checkpoints. [indie+devforum, 2024-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If the user asks about rolling back, point them to Git file history and to the Studio version history with notes. [indie+devforum, 2024-04] -- [Roblox DevForum 2018-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780)
- If the user asks about Open Cloud publishing, check the Content-Type for .rbxl versus .rbxlx and verify versionNumber in the response. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-place-publishing)
- If the user asks about parallel AI work, recommend splitting by file or feature with one integrator. [indie, 2026-10] -- [MonarchDev 2026-10 5:20](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=320)
- If the user asks about Place Branch and Merge, say it is announced for early 2027 and not yet available. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If the user asks about pinning tools in CI, prefer Rokit per existing guidance; mention Foreman only as older 2022 practice.
- If the user asks about third-party libraries, recommend installing via Wally and checking maintenance status first. [devforum, 2026-06] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535)
- If the user asks about x-roblox-* fields, warn they are experimental and prefer official OpenAPI fields. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/openapi)
- If a source is a 2020–2022 indie post, check whether its Rojo, Wally, or plugin versions still apply before advising. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/validation-tool)
- If the user asks about CI for Luau packages, run StyLua --check and Selene and keep packed .rbxm files out of Git. [devforum, 2022-05] -- [Roblox DevForum 2022-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-proper-open-source-project-with-devops/1796689)
- If a project uses Rojo and a Wally package, check default.project.json mapping, then restart rojo serve and reconnect Studio after wally install before testing. [indie, 2026-07] -- [Chase Higgins 2026-07 3:26](https://youtu.be/p86bt9ROQfk?t=206)
- If a library is requested, verify the Wally package name against the upstream repo and avoid forks that are not maintained. [indie, 2025-12] -- [Possible Panda 2025-12 0:41](https://youtu.be/aSE2D4_TwoU?t=41)
- If Rojo is not in use, the manual .rbxm import is the fallback; mention that it has no version tracking. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Possible Panda 2025-11 1:36](https://youtu.be/3e406QQHt7o?t=96)
- If the user asks whether to commit the .rbxl, flag the disagreement and default to the existing rule (sources in Git, place binary out) unless the user asks for binary history. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/coding-harness)
- Do not state that rojo init creates a Git repo or .gitignore without verifying it; the only source is T15985. [indie, 2022-11] -- [Dev Total 2022-11 12:58](https://youtu.be/0K-5sifYhxs?t=778)
- Prefer 2025-2026 package and LSP workflow advice over 2022 Git advice when they conflict in tooling details. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/external-tools)
- If an external AI agent needs Studio access, then point it at the Studio MCP server rather than building a custom bridge. [official, 2026-04] -- [Roblox 2026-04 39:20](https://youtu.be/8IcYpOl4Sdk?t=2360)
- If a Roblox feature is announced but not yet released (for example Branch and Merge), then keep the current Git-based workflow and do not assume the feature exists. [devforum, 2026-09] -- [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880)
- If a forum request has strong community demand and Roblox has not shipped it, then consider building and publishing a plugin.
- If the user asks for shared and server-only libraries, place server-only Wally packages in the server dependency section. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Leif 2025-05 8:38](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=518)
- If Wally packages are installed but types are missing in autocomplete, run wally-package-types and check the editor again. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Leif 2025-05 10:17](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=617)
- If the user is committing a Wally project to Git, add generated folders and sourcemap.json to .gitignore, and ask before ignoring wally.lock. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Leif 2025-05 11:55](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=715)
- If the user asks to set up linting or formatting, add selene.toml and StyLua format-on-save locally in addition to the CI gate. [indie, 2025-05] -- [Leif 2025-05 5:45](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=345)
- When citing these 2025-05 indie tips, mark them as self-reported and recommend checking current tool versions. [indie, 2024-03] -- [janisjanis01 2024-03 4:11](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=251) **[OUTDATED-UNSURE]**
- If advising on data-loss risk in Studio, recommend manual saves and version control together; do not present autosave as sufficient protection.
- If a user reports a crash that lost work, check whether recent sources (sources from 2026) are available that describe current recovery behavior before stating how recovery works.

## vfx-and-particles (coverage: covered)

- If a request involves particle Rate above 100/s, then warn about the mobile cap of 100/s and suggest Size, Speed or LightEmission instead of more particles. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/particle-emitters)
- If an effect needs to fade, then animate Transparency with a NumberSequence whose last keypoint is 1; do not hide it abruptly. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/custom-particle-effects)
- If a particle must glow, then set LightEmission 1 and LightInfluence 0; if it must light the surroundings, then add a PointLight in an invisible part. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/create-volcanoes)
- If the user builds a Beam, then assign two distinct Attachments to Attachment0 and Attachment1 and use CurveSize0/1 for arcs. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/trails)
- If a Beam must be a collision hitbox, then pair it with an invisible anchored part (Transparency 1) and use Touched. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/laser-traps-with-beams)
- If a Lifetime is set above 20 s, then reduce it to 20 s or less, because larger values are silently clamped. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/particle-emitters)
- If a particle effect is triggered by code, then name the emitter exactly as the script expects, set Enabled false, and call Emit or enable it on demand. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/using-particles-for-actions)
- If a trail is wanted, then leave LockToPart off; if a single blast or muzzle flash must follow one part, then enable it. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/finish-the-blast)
- If a visual must follow an animated skinned mesh limb, then sync it to the bone every frame (PreSimulation) instead of parenting it once. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/beyond-the-dark/custom-characters)
- If the effect should look different on low- and high-end devices, then test at the lowest and highest Editor Quality Level before shipping. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/performance)
- If an effect is one-shot, disable its emitter's Enabled and call Emit(count) from script. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/use-particles-for-explosions)
- If you scale Emit counts, read SavedQualityLevel first and reduce counts on low settings. [devforum, 2020-10] -- [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499)
- If a beam or trail vanishes from some camera angles, enable FaceCamera. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/effects/trails)
- If a flat texture z-fights with its surface, set ZOffset to about 2. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/create-waterfalls)
- If an effect faces the wrong way, rotate it on the correct axis rather than leaving rotation at zero. [indie, 2026-10] -- [ArTheDev 2026-10 29:33](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1773)
- If you raise Squash on a particle emitter, raise Size to keep particles from thinning. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world)
- If you need dust or ambient particles in a huge area, use a high Rate in a large volume and disable CanCollide. [official, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/create-basic-visual-effects)
- If rain should drift with wind, enable WindAffectsDrag and set Drag above 0. [mixed, 2026-10] -- [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/global-wind)
- If newer Creator Docs (2026-10) conflict with older DevForum advice, follow the docs and state the conflict.
- If a figure comes from an indie source, treat it as self-reported and verify it against a test build. [indie, 2026-10] -- [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 23:56](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=1436)
- If particles look thin, check the Rate and Lifetime caps first; do not keep raising Rate. [indie, 2015-04] -- [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 12:45](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=765)
- If particles must fall, set Acceleration Y negative; remember Acceleration uses world axes. [indie, 2015-04] -- [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 11:09](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=669)
- If overlapping emitters on one part flicker, adjust ZOffset (render priority), not position. [indie, 2015-04] -- [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 9:37](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=577)
- If a stretched particle looks wrong on a large surface, test a small particle scaled up instead. [indie, 2015-04] -- [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 16:50](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1010)
- If a character dies, fade parts with TweenService on Humanoid.Died after about a 0.25 s delay. [indie, 2024-09] -- [BubuTheDev 2024-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/oYQ5jIZKE8s?t=0)
- If a surface needs glow, prefer the emissive mask/strength/tint approach over export-twice neon, and confirm the property names in Studio. [indie, 2025-11] -- [Last Level Studios 2025-11 6:46](https://youtu.be/KNHngRRMPVI?t=406)
- If a key moment feels flat, add a visible motion or VFX payoff and playtest it. [indie, 2026-10] -- [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:49](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=109)
- When sources conflict, prefer the 2024 to 2026 advice over the 2015 advice and say so. [mixed, 2026-09] -- [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880)
- If a pickup or collectible needs to stand out, add a looping spin and bob tween and a distinct color before adding other effects. [indie, 2025-06] -- [JustDucksey 2025-06 3:26](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=206)
- If a beam needs organic motion, drive CurveSize0 and CurveSize1 from math.noise over elapsed time rather than a fixed value. [indie, 2026-03] -- [Stewiepfing 2026-03 19:10](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=1150)
- If a game event should trigger a celebration effect, send it from the server through a RemoteEvent and play it only on that event, not on a timer or constantly. [indie, 2024-04] -- [janisjanis01 2024-04 7:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=420)
- If a water part looks static, add a texture-scrolling script to it before tuning other effects. [indie, 2024-06] -- [janisjanis01 2024-06 4:44](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=284)
